# 中华人民共和国刑法

**分类**：法律  
**法律部门**：刑法  
**公布日期**：2020-12-26  
**生效日期**：2021-03-01

> （1979年7月1日第五届全国人民代表大会第二次会议通过　1997年3月14日第八届全国人民代表大会第五次会议修订　根据1998年12月29日第九届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第六次会议通过的《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定》、1999年12月25日第九届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第十三次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案》、2001年8月31日第九届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第二十三次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（二）》、2001年12月29日第九届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第二十五次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（三）》、2002年12月28日第九届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第三十一次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）》、2005年2月28日第十届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第十四次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（五）》、2006年6月29日第十届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第二十二次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》、2009年2月28日第十一届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第七次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》、2009年8月27日第十一届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第十次会议通过的《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于修改部分法律的决定》、2011年2月25日第十一届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第十九次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》、2015年8月29日第十二届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第十六次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》、2017年11月4日第十二届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第三十次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十）》和2020年12月26日第十三届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第二十四次会议通过的《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》修正） 目　　录 第一编　总　　则 第一章　刑法的任务、基本原则和适用范围 第二章　犯　　罪 第一节　犯罪和刑事责任 第二节　犯罪的预备、未遂和中止 第三节　共同犯罪 第四节　单位犯罪 第三章　刑　　罚 第一节　刑罚的种类 第二节　管　　制 第三节　拘　　役 第四节　有期徒刑、无期徒刑 第五节　死　　刑

---

## 第一编　总则

### 第一章　刑法的任务、基本原则和适用范围

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条　为了惩罚犯罪，保护人民，根据宪法，结合我国同犯罪作斗争的具体经验及实际情况，制定本法。

**Article 1** This Law is formulated in accordance with the Constitution and in light of the specific experience and actual circumstances of China's struggle against crime, with a view to punishing crime and protecting the people.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条　中华人民共和国刑法的任务，是用刑罚同一切犯罪行为作斗争，以保卫国家安全，保卫人民民主专政的政权和社会主义制度，保护国有财产和劳动群众集体所有的财产，保护公民私人所有的财产，保护公民的人身权利、民主权利和其他权利，维护社会秩序、经济秩序，保障社会主义建设事业的顺利进行。

**Article 2** The tasks of the Criminal Law of the People's Republic of China are to use criminal penalties to combat all criminal acts in order to safeguard national security, to defend the state power of the people's democratic dictatorship and the socialist system, to protect state-owned property and property collectively owned by the working people, to protect citizens' privately owned property, to protect citizens' personal rights, democratic rights and other rights, to maintain social and economic order, and to ensure the smooth progress of the socialist construction cause.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条　法律明文规定为犯罪行为的，依照法律定罪处刑；法律没有明文规定为犯罪行为的，不得定罪处刑。

**Article 3** Where the law expressly stipulates an act as a crime, the offender shall be convicted and punished in accordance with law; where the law does not expressly stipulate an act as a crime, no offender shall be convicted or punished.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条　对任何人犯罪，在适用法律上一律平等。不允许任何人有超越法律的特权。

**Article 4** The law shall be equally applied to anyone who commits a crime. No one shall be privileged to be beyond the law.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条　刑罚的轻重，应当与犯罪分子所犯罪行和承担的刑事责任相适应。

**Article 5** The severity of punishments shall be commensurate with the crime committed by the offender and the criminal liability he or she bears.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条　凡在中华人民共和国领域内犯罪的，除法律有特别规定的以外，都适用本法。  
凡在中华人民共和国船舶或者航空器内犯罪的，也适用本法。  
犯罪的行为或者结果有一项发生在中华人民共和国领域内的，就认为是在中华人民共和国领域内犯罪。

**Article 6** This Law shall apply to all crimes committed within the territory of the People's Republic of China, except as otherwise specifically provided by law.  
This Law shall also apply to crimes committed on board a vessel or aircraft of the People's Republic of China.  
If an act of crime or its consequence occurs within the territory of the People's Republic of China, the crime shall be deemed to have been committed within the territory of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条　中华人民共和国公民在中华人民共和国领域外犯本法规定之罪的，适用本法，但是按本法规定的最高刑为三年以下有期徒刑的，可以不予追究。  
中华人民共和国国家工作人员和军人在中华人民共和国领域外犯本法规定之罪的，适用本法。

**Article 7** This Law shall apply to citizens of the People's Republic of China who commit crimes prescribed by this Law outside the territory of the People's Republic of China; however, if the maximum punishment prescribed by this Law is fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, the matter may be disregarded.  
This Law shall apply to state functionaries and military personnel of the People's Republic of China who commit crimes prescribed by this Law outside the territory of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条　外国人在中华人民共和国领域外对中华人民共和国国家或者公民犯罪，而按本法规定的最低刑为三年以上有期徒刑的，可以适用本法，但是按照犯罪地的法律不受处罚的除外。

**Article 8** This Law may apply to a foreigner who, outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, commits a crime against the state of the People's Republic of China or against a citizen of the People's Republic of China, if the minimum punishment prescribed by this Law for such crime is fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years; however, this shall not apply if the crime is not punishable according to the law of the place where it is committed.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条　对于中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约所规定的罪行，中华人民共和国在所承担条约义务的范围内行使刑事管辖权的，适用本法。

**Article 9** This Law shall apply to crimes specified in international treaties to which the People's Republic of China is a party or has acceded, and over which the People's Republic of China exercises criminal jurisdiction within the scope of the obligations it assumes under such treaties.

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条　凡在中华人民共和国领域外犯罪，依照本法应当负刑事责任的，虽然经过外国审判，仍然可以依照本法追究，但是在外国已经受过刑罚处罚的，可以免除或者减轻处罚。

**Article 10** Any person who commits a crime outside the territory of the People's Republic of China and who, according to this Law, shall bear criminal liability may still be subject to investigation according to this Law, even if the person has been tried by a foreign court. However, if the person has already received criminal punishment in the foreign country, the punishment may be exempted or mitigated.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条　享有外交特权和豁免权的外国人的刑事责任，通过外交途径解决。

**Article 11** Criminal liability of a foreigner who enjoys diplomatic privileges and immunities shall be resolved through diplomatic channels.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条　中华人民共和国成立以后本法施行以前的行为，如果当时的法律不认为是犯罪的，适用当时的法律；如果当时的法律认为是犯罪的，依照本法总则第四章第八节的规定应当追诉的，按照当时的法律追究刑事责任，但是如果本法不认为是犯罪或者处刑较轻的，适用本法。  
本法施行以前，依照当时的法律已经作出的生效判决，继续有效。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于认真学习宣传贯彻修订的《中华人民共和国刑法》的通知.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于认真学习宣传贯彻修订的《中华人民共和国刑法》的通知》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/文书、送达与废止/最高人民法院印发《关于规范人民法院再审立案的若干意见（试行）》的通知.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院印发《关于规范人民法院再审立案的若干意见（试行）》的通知》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 12** For acts committed after the founding of the People's Republic of China but before the coming into force of this Law, if the law in force at the time did not deem the acts as crimes, the applicable law shall be the law in force at the time. If the law in force at the time deemed the acts as crimes and the acts are subject to prosecution according to the provisions of Article 8, Chapter 4 of the General Provisions of this Law, criminal liability shall be investigated according to the law in force at the time. However, if this Law does not deem the acts as crimes or imposes a lighter punishment, this Law shall apply.  
Judgments that have taken effect before the coming into force of this Law, which were rendered according to the law in force at the time, shall remain valid.

### 第二章　犯罪

#### 第一节　犯罪和刑事责任

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条　一切危害国家主权、领土完整和安全，分裂国家、颠覆人民民主专政的政权和推翻社会主义制度，破坏社会秩序和经济秩序，侵犯国有财产或者劳动群众集体所有的财产，侵犯公民私人所有的财产，侵犯公民的人身权利、民主权利和其他权利，以及其他危害社会的行为，依照法律应当受刑罚处罚的，都是犯罪，但是情节显著轻微危害不大的，不认为是犯罪。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理非法制造、买卖、运输、储存毒鼠强等禁用剧毒化学品刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理非法制造、买卖、运输、储存毒鼠强等禁用剧毒化学品刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第5条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 13** All acts that endanger national sovereignty, territorial integrity and security, split the state, subvert the state power of the people's democratic dictatorship, overthrow the socialist system, undermine social and economic order, infringe upon state-owned property or property collectively owned by the working people, infringe upon citizens' privately owned property, infringe upon citizens' personal rights, democratic rights and other rights, and other acts that endanger society, which are subject to punishment according to law, are crimes. However, if the circumstances are manifestly minor and the harm caused is not significant, the acts shall not be deemed crimes.

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条　明知自己的行为会发生危害社会的结果，并且希望或者放任这种结果发生，因而构成犯罪的，是故意犯罪。  
故意犯罪，应当负刑事责任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院印发《关于人民法院执行〈中华人民共和国国家赔偿法〉几个问题的解释》的通知.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院印发《关于人民法院执行〈中华人民共和国国家赔偿法〉几个问题的解释》的通知》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 14** A crime intentionally committed is one which occurs when a person knows that his or her act will cause socially harmful consequences and wishes or allows such consequences to occur, thus constituting a crime.  
A crime intentionally committed shall bear criminal liability.

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条　应当预见自己的行为可能发生危害社会的结果，因为疏忽大意而没有预见，或者已经预见而轻信能够避免，以致发生这种结果的，是过失犯罪。  
过失犯罪，法律有规定的才负刑事责任。

**Article 15** A crime negligently committed is one which occurs when a person should have foreseen that his or her act would possibly cause socially harmful consequences but, due to negligence, fails to foresee them, or, having foreseen them, believes they can be avoided lightly, thereby causing such consequences.  
A crime negligently committed shall bear criminal liability only if the law so provides.

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条　行为在客观上虽然造成了损害结果，但是不是出于故意或者过失，而是由于不能抗拒或者不能预见的原因所引起的，不是犯罪。

**Article 16** An act which objectively results in harmful consequences but is not caused by intent or negligence, but rather by irresistible or unforeseeable causes, shall not be deemed a crime.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条　已满十六周岁的人犯罪，应当负刑事责任。  
已满十四周岁不满十六周岁的人，犯故意杀人、故意伤害致人重伤或者死亡、强奸、抢劫、贩卖毒品、放火、爆炸、投放危险物质罪的，应当负刑事责任。  
已满十二周岁不满十四周岁的人，犯故意杀人、故意伤害罪，致人死亡或者以特别残忍手段致人重伤造成严重残疾，情节恶劣，经最高人民检察院核准追诉的，应当负刑事责任。  
对依照前三款规定追究刑事责任的不满十八周岁的人，应当从轻或者减轻处罚。  
因不满十六周岁不予刑事处罚的，责令其父母或者其他监护人加以管教；在必要的时候，依法进行专门矫治教育。  
第十七条之一　已满七十五周岁的人故意犯罪的，可以从轻或者减轻处罚；过失犯罪的，应当从轻或者减轻处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民检察院关于对涉嫌盗窃的不满十六周岁未成年人采取刑事拘留强制措施是否违法问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于对涉嫌盗窃的不满十六周岁未成年人采取刑事拘留强制措施是否违法问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-1" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-1" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第1条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国国家赔偿法.md#art-19" title="被《中华人民共和国国家赔偿法》第19条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 17** Any person who has reached the age of sixteen and commits a crime shall bear criminal liability.  
Any person who has reached the age of fourteen but not the age of sixteen and commits the crimes of intentional homicide, intentional injury causing serious injury or death, rape, robbery, drug trafficking, arson, explosion, or placing dangerous substances shall bear criminal liability.  
Any person who has reached the age of twelve but not the age of fourteen and commits the crimes of intentional homicide or intentional injury, causing death or causing serious injury with extreme cruelty resulting in severe disability, under aggravating circumstances, and with approval of the Supreme People's Procuratorate for prosecution, shall bear criminal liability.  
Any person who is under the age of eighteen and is investigated for criminal liability according to the provisions of the preceding three paragraphs shall be given a light or mitigated punishment.  
Where a person is not subject to criminal punishment due to being under the age of sixteen, his or her parents or other guardians shall be ordered to discipline and supervise him or her; when necessary, the person may undergo specialized correctional education in accordance with law.  
Article 17 bis Any person who has reached the age of seventy-five and commits a crime intentionally may be given a light or mitigated punishment; if the crime is committed negligently, a light or mitigated punishment shall be given.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条　精神病人在不能辨认或者不能控制自己行为的时候造成危害结果，经法定程序鉴定确认的，不负刑事责任，但是应当责令他的家属或者监护人严加看管和医疗；在必要的时候，由政府强制医疗。  
间歇性的精神病人在精神正常的时候犯罪，应当负刑事责任。  
尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人犯罪的，应当负刑事责任，但是可以从轻或者减轻处罚。  
醉酒的人犯罪，应当负刑事责任。

**Article 18** A mental patient who causes harmful consequences at a time when he or she is unable to recognize or control his or her own conduct, and this is confirmed through statutory procedures, shall not bear criminal liability, but his or her family members or guardians shall be ordered to keep the patient under strict surveillance and provide medical treatment; when necessary, the government may compel medical treatment.  
A person with intermittent mental illness who commits a crime during a period of mental normality shall bear criminal liability.  
A mental patient who has not yet completely lost the ability to recognize or control his or her conduct and commits a crime shall bear criminal liability, but may be given a light or mitigated punishment.  
A person who commits a crime while intoxicated shall bear criminal liability.

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条　又聋又哑的人或者盲人犯罪，可以从轻、减轻或者免除处罚。

**Article 19** A deaf-mute or a blind person who commits a crime may be given a light or mitigated punishment or be exempted from punishment.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条　为了使国家、公共利益、本人或者他人的人身、财产和其他权利免受正在进行的不法侵害，而采取的制止不法侵害的行为，对不法侵害人造成损害的，属于正当防卫，不负刑事责任。  
正当防卫明显超过必要限度造成重大损害的，应当负刑事责任，但是应当减轻或者免除处罚。  
对正在进行行凶、杀人、抢劫、强奸、绑架以及其他严重危及人身安全的暴力犯罪，采取防卫行为，造成不法侵害人伤亡的，不属于防卫过当，不负刑事责任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部印发关于依法适用正当防卫制度的指导意见的通知.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部印发关于依法适用正当防卫制度的指导意见的通知》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部印发关于依法适用正当防卫制度的指导意见的通知.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部印发关于依法适用正当防卫制度的指导意见的通知》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 20** An act that is taken to stop an ongoing unlawful infringement, with a view to protecting the state, public interest, or the person, property, or other rights of oneself or another person, and that causes damage to the unlawful infringer, constitutes justifiable defense and shall not bear criminal liability.  
Where justifiable defense clearly exceeds the necessary limits and causes serious damage, criminal liability shall be borne, but the punishment shall be mitigated or exempted.  
Where a defensive act is taken against ongoing assault, homicide, robbery, rape, kidnapping, or any other violent crime that seriously endangers personal safety, causing injury or death to the unlawful infringer, it shall not be considered as excessive defense and shall not bear criminal liability.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条　为了使国家、公共利益、本人或者他人的人身、财产和其他权利免受正在发生的危险，不得已采取的紧急避险行为，造成损害的，不负刑事责任。  
紧急避险超过必要限度造成不应有的损害的，应当负刑事责任，但是应当减轻或者免除处罚。  
第一款中关于避免本人危险的规定，不适用于职务上、业务上负有特定责任的人。

**Article 21** An act of necessity taken in an emergency to avert an ongoing danger that threatens the state, public interest, or the person, property, or other rights of oneself or another person, and that causes damage, shall not bear criminal liability.  
Where an act of necessity taken in an emergency exceeds the necessary limits and causes undue damage, criminal liability shall be borne, but the punishment shall be mitigated or exempted.  
The provisions in the first paragraph concerning averting danger to oneself shall not apply to persons who have specific responsibilities arising from their office or profession.

#### 第二节　犯罪的预备、未遂和中止

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条　为了犯罪，准备工具、制造条件的，是犯罪预备。  
对于预备犯，可以比照既遂犯从轻、减轻处罚或者免除处罚。

**Article 22** Preparation for a crime, such as preparing tools or creating conditions for the commission of a crime, constitutes preparation for a crime.  
An offender who prepares for a crime may, in comparison with an offender who completes the crime, be given a light or mitigated punishment or be exempted from punishment.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条　已经着手实行犯罪，由于犯罪分子意志以外的原因而未得逞的，是犯罪未遂。  
对于未遂犯，可以比照既遂犯从轻或者减轻处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部印发《关于当前办理集团犯罪案件中具体应用法律的若干问题的解答》的通知.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部印发《关于当前办理集团犯罪案件中具体应用法律的若干问题的解答》的通知》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 23** An attempt to commit a crime occurs when the offender has already started to commit the crime but fails to complete it due to reasons independent of his or her will.  
An attempted offender may, in comparison with an offender who completes the crime, be given a light or mitigated punishment.

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条　在犯罪过程中，自动放弃犯罪或者自动有效地防止犯罪结果发生的，是犯罪中止。  
对于中止犯，没有造成损害的，应当免除处罚；造成损害的，应当减轻处罚。

**Article 24** Discontinuance of a crime occurs when, during the process of committing a crime, the offender voluntarily abandons the crime or voluntarily and effectively prevents the criminal consequence from occurring.  
An offender who discontinues a crime and causes no damage shall be exempted from punishment; if damage is caused, the punishment shall be mitigated.

#### 第三节　共同犯罪

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条　共同犯罪是指二人以上共同故意犯罪。  
二人以上共同过失犯罪，不以共同犯罪论处；应当负刑事责任的，按照他们所犯的罪分别处罚。

**Article 25** A joint crime refers to a crime intentionally committed by two or more persons acting jointly.  
A crime negligently committed by two or more persons acting jointly shall not be treated as a joint crime; where criminal liability shall be borne, they shall be punished separately according to the crimes they have committed.

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条　组织、领导犯罪集团进行犯罪活动的或者在共同犯罪中起主要作用的，是主犯。  
三人以上为共同实施犯罪而组成的较为固定的犯罪组织，是犯罪集团。  
对组织、领导犯罪集团的首要分子，按照集团所犯的全部罪行处罚。  
对于第三款规定以外的主犯，应当按照其所参与的或者组织、指挥的全部犯罪处罚。

**Article 26** A principal offender is one who organizes or leads a criminal group in carrying out criminal activities or who plays a major role in a joint crime.  
A criminal group is a relatively stable criminal organization formed by three or more persons for the purpose of jointly committing crimes.  
The ringleader who organizes or leads a criminal group shall be punished according to all the crimes committed by the group.  
For principal offenders other than those specified in the third paragraph, they shall be punished according to all the crimes they participated in or organized or directed.

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条　在共同犯罪中起次要或者辅助作用的，是从犯。  
对于从犯，应当从轻、减轻处罚或者免除处罚。

**Article 27** An accomplice is one who plays a secondary or auxiliary role in a joint crime.  
An accomplice shall be given a light or mitigated punishment or be exempted from punishment.

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条　对于被胁迫参加犯罪的，应当按照他的犯罪情节减轻处罚或者免除处罚。

**Article 28** A person who is coerced to participate in a crime shall be given a mitigated punishment or be exempted from punishment according to the circumstances of his or her crime.

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条　教唆他人犯罪的，应当按照他在共同犯罪中所起的作用处罚。教唆不满十八周岁的人犯罪的，应当从重处罚。  
如果被教唆的人没有犯被教唆的罪，对于教唆犯，可以从轻或者减轻处罚。

**Article 29** A person who instigates another to commit a crime shall be punished according to the role he or she plays in the joint crime. A person who instigates a person under the age of eighteen to commit a crime shall be given a severe punishment.  
If the instigated person does not commit the instigated crime, the instigator may be given a light or mitigated punishment.

#### 第四节　单位犯罪

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条　公司、企业、事业单位、机关、团体实施的危害社会的行为，法律规定为单位犯罪的，应当负刑事责任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/法律解释/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第三十条的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第三十条的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 30** Where a company, enterprise, institution, state organ, or organization commits an act that endangers society, and the law provides that such act constitutes a crime committed by an organization, the organization shall bear criminal liability.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条　单位犯罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员判处刑罚。本法分则和其他法律另有规定的，依照规定。

**Article 31** Where an organization commits a crime, a fine shall be imposed on the organization, and the directly responsible supervisor and other directly responsible persons shall be subject to criminal punishment. Where the Specific Provisions of this Law or other laws provide otherwise, those provisions shall apply.

### 第三章　刑罚

#### 第一节　刑罚的种类

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条　刑罚分为主刑和附加刑。

**Article 32** Punishments are divided into principal punishments and supplementary punishments.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条　主刑的种类如下：  
（一）管制；  
（二）拘役；  
（三）有期徒刑；  
（四）无期徒刑；  
（五）死刑。

**Article 33** The types of principal punishments are as follows:  
(1) public surveillance;  
(2) criminal detention;  
(3) fixed-term imprisonment;  
(4) life imprisonment;  
(5) the death penalty.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条　附加刑的种类如下：  
（一）罚金；  
（二）剥夺政治权利；  
（三）没收财产。  
附加刑也可以独立适用。

**Article 34** The types of supplementary punishments are as follows:  
(1) a fine;  
(2) deprivation of political rights;  
(3) confiscation of property.  
Supplementary punishments may also be imposed independently.

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条　对于犯罪的外国人，可以独立适用或者附加适用驱逐出境。

**Article 35** For a foreigner who commits a crime, deportation may be imposed independently or in addition to other punishments.

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条　由于犯罪行为而使被害人遭受经济损失的，对犯罪分子除依法给予刑事处罚外，并应根据情况判处赔偿经济损失。  
承担民事赔偿责任的犯罪分子，同时被判处罚金，其财产不足以全部支付的，或者被判处没收财产的，应当先承担对被害人的民事赔偿责任。

**Article 36** Where a victim suffers economic losses as a result of a criminal act, the criminal offender shall, in addition to receiving criminal punishment according to law, be sentenced to compensate for the economic losses according to the circumstances.  
Where a criminal offender who bears civil liability for compensation is also sentenced to a fine, and his or her property is insufficient to pay both the compensation and the fine, or where the offender is sentenced to confiscation of property, the criminal offender shall first bear civil liability for compensation to the victim.

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条　对于犯罪情节轻微不需要判处刑罚的，可以免予刑事处罚，但是可以根据案件的不同情况，予以训诫或者责令具结悔过、赔礼道歉、赔偿损失，或者由主管部门予以行政处罚或者行政处分。  
第三十七条之一　因利用职业便利实施犯罪，或者实施违背职业要求的特定义务的犯罪被判处刑罚的，人民法院可以根据犯罪情况和预防再犯罪的需要，禁止其自刑罚执行完毕之日或者假释之日起从事相关职业，期限为三年至五年。  
被禁止从事相关职业的人违反人民法院依照前款规定作出的决定的，由公安机关依法给予处罚；情节严重的，依照[本法第三百一十三条](#art-313)的规定定罪处罚。  
其他法律、行政法规对其从事相关职业另有禁止或者限制性规定的，从其规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/人身犯罪/人民检察院办理未成年人刑事案件的规定.md#art-27" title="被《人民检察院办理未成年人刑事案件的规定》第27条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-1" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 37** Where the circumstances of a crime are minor and no punishment is necessary, the offender may be exempted from criminal punishment; however, depending on the different circumstances of the case, the offender may be reprimanded or ordered to make a statement of repentance, offer an apology, or compensate for losses, or may be subject to an administrative penalty or disciplinary sanction by the competent authority.  
Article 37 bis Where a person is sentenced to criminal punishment for committing a crime by taking advantage of occupational convenience or for committing a crime violating specific obligations required by an occupation, the People's Court may, based on the circumstances of the crime and the need to prevent recidivism, prohibit the person from engaging in the relevant occupation for a period of three to five years, commencing from the date of completion of the execution of the punishment or the date of parole.  
Where a person prohibited from engaging in a relevant occupation violates a decision made by the People's Court in accordance with the preceding paragraph, the public security organ shall impose punishment according to law; if the circumstances are serious, the person shall be convicted and punished in accordance with Article 313 of this Law.  
Where other laws or administrative regulations contain prohibitive or restrictive provisions on the engagement in the relevant occupation, those provisions shall apply.

#### 第二节　管制

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条　管制的期限，为三个月以上二年以下。  
判处管制，可以根据犯罪情况，同时禁止犯罪分子在执行期间从事特定活动，进入特定区域、场所，接触特定的人。  
对判处管制的犯罪分子，依法实行社区矫正。  
违反第二款规定的禁止令的，由公安机关依照《中华人民共和国治安管理处罚法》的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-2" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 38** The term of public surveillance shall be not less than three months and not more than two years.  
Where public surveillance is imposed, the criminal offender may, based on the circumstances of the crime, be prohibited during the execution period from engaging in specific activities, entering specific areas or premises, or contacting specific persons.  
Community correction shall be implemented according to law for criminal offenders sentenced to public surveillance.  
Where a person violates a prohibition order imposed under the provisions of the second paragraph, the public security organ shall impose punishment in accordance with the provisions of the Public Security Administration Punishments Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条　被判处管制的犯罪分子，在执行期间，应当遵守下列规定：  
（一）遵守法律、行政法规，服从监督；  
（二）未经执行机关批准，不得行使言论、出版、集会、结社、游行、示威自由的权利；  
（三）按照执行机关规定报告自己的活动情况；  
（四）遵守执行机关关于会客的规定；  
（五）离开所居住的市、县或者迁居，应当报经执行机关批准。  
对于被判处管制的犯罪分子，在劳动中应当同工同酬。

**Article 39** A criminal offender sentenced to public surveillance shall, during the execution period, comply with the following provisions:  
(1) observe laws and administrative regulations, and submit to supervision;  
(2) without the approval of the executing authority, shall not exercise the rights to freedom of speech, press, assembly, association, procession, or demonstration;  
(3) report his or her activities in accordance with the regulations of the executing authority;  
(4) observe the regulations of the executing authority concerning meeting visitors;  
(5) report to the executing authority for approval when leaving the city or county where he or she resides or moving to another residence.  
For criminal offenders sentenced to public surveillance, they shall receive equal pay for equal work in labor.

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条　被判处管制的犯罪分子，管制期满，执行机关应即向本人和其所在单位或者居住地的群众宣布解除管制。

**Article 40** When a criminal offender sentenced to public surveillance completes the term of public surveillance, the executing authority shall promptly announce the removal of the public surveillance to the offender and to the offender's unit or the residents of the place of residence.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条　管制的刑期，从判决执行之日起计算；判决执行以前先行羁押的，羁押一日折抵刑期二日。

**Article 41** : The term of public surveillance shall be calculated from the date on which the judgment begins execution; if the criminal suspect is held in custody before the execution of the judgment, one day in custody shall be offset against two days of the sentence.

#### 第三节　拘役

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条　拘役的期限，为一个月以上六个月以下。

**Article 42** : The term of criminal detention shall be not less than one month but not more than six months.

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条　被判处拘役的犯罪分子，由公安机关就近执行。  
在执行期间，被判处拘役的犯罪分子每月可以回家一天至两天；参加劳动的，可以酌量发给报酬。

**Article 43** : A criminal sentenced to criminal detention shall be executed by the public security organ at a nearby location.  
During the execution period, a criminal sentenced to criminal detention may go home for one to two days each month; those who participate in labor may be given remuneration as appropriate.

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条　拘役的刑期，从判决执行之日起计算；判决执行以前先行羁押的，羁押一日折抵刑期一日。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院研究室关于如何理解“审判的时候怀孕的妇女不适用死刑”问题的电话答复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院研究室关于如何理解“审判的时候怀孕的妇女不适用死刑”问题的电话答复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 44** : The term of criminal detention shall be calculated from the date on which the judgment begins execution; if the criminal suspect is held in custody before the execution of the judgment, one day in custody shall be offset against one day of the sentence.

#### 第四节　有期徒刑、无期徒刑

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条　有期徒刑的期限，除[本法第五十条](#art-50)、第六十九条规定外，为六个月以上十五年以下。

**Article 45** : The term of fixed-term imprisonment, except as provided for in Articles 50 and 69 of this Law, shall be not less than six months but not more than fifteen years.

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条　被判处有期徒刑、无期徒刑的犯罪分子，在监狱或者其他执行场所执行；凡有劳动能力的，都应当参加劳动，接受教育和改造。

**Article 46** : Criminals sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment or life imprisonment shall be executed in prison or another place of execution; all those with ability to work shall participate in labor, receive education, and undergo reform.

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条　有期徒刑的刑期，从判决执行之日起计算；判决执行以前先行羁押的，羁押一日折抵刑期一日。

**Article 47** : The term of fixed-term imprisonment shall be calculated from the date on which the judgment begins execution; if the criminal suspect is held in custody before the execution of the judgment, one day in custody shall be offset against one day of the sentence.

#### 第五节　死刑

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条　死刑只适用于罪行极其严重的犯罪分子。对于应当判处死刑的犯罪分子，如果不是必须立即执行的，可以判处死刑同时宣告缓期二年执行。  
死刑除依法由最高人民法院判决的以外，都应当报请最高人民法院核准。死刑缓期执行的，可以由高级人民法院判决或者核准。

**Article 48** : The death penalty shall only apply to criminals whose crimes are extremely serious. For a criminal who should be sentenced to death, if immediate execution is not necessary, a death sentence may be imposed concurrently with a two-year reprieve.  
Except for death sentences adjudicated by the Supreme People's Court according to law, all death sentences shall be submitted to the Supreme People's Court for approval. A death sentence with a reprieve may be adjudicated or approved by a Higher People's Court.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条　犯罪的时候不满十八周岁的人和审判的时候怀孕的妇女，不适用死刑。  
审判的时候已满七十五周岁的人，不适用死刑，但以特别残忍手段致人死亡的除外。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-3" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 49** : The death penalty shall not apply to persons who are under the age of 18 at the time of the crime or to women who are pregnant at the time of adjudication.  
The death penalty shall not apply to persons who have reached the age of 75 at the time of adjudication, except for those who have caused death by particularly cruel means.

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条　判处死刑缓期执行的，在死刑缓期执行期间，如果没有故意犯罪，二年期满以后，减为无期徒刑；如果确有重大立功表现，二年期满以后，减为二十五年有期徒刑；如果故意犯罪，情节恶劣的，报请最高人民法院核准后执行死刑；对于故意犯罪未执行死刑的，死刑缓期执行的期间重新计算，并报最高人民法院备案。  
对被判处死刑缓期执行的累犯以及因故意杀人、强奸、抢劫、绑架、放火、爆炸、投放危险物质或者有组织的暴力性犯罪被判处死刑缓期执行的犯罪分子，人民法院根据犯罪情节等情况可以同时决定对其限制减刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-2" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-4" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第4条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-15" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第15条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 50** : For those sentenced to death with a reprieve, during the period of the reprieve, if there is no intentional crime, upon the expiration of two years, it shall be commuted to life imprisonment; if there is a major meritorious service, upon the expiration of two years, it shall be commuted to 25 years of fixed-term imprisonment; if there is an intentional crime with egregious circumstances, after reporting to the Supreme People's Court for approval, the death penalty shall be executed; if an intentional crime is committed but the death penalty is not executed, the period of the death sentence with a reprieve shall be recalculated, and reported to the Supreme People's Court for record.  
For recidivists sentenced to death with a reprieve and for criminals sentenced to death with a reprieve due to intentional homicide, rape, robbery, kidnapping, arson, explosion, dissemination of dangerous substances, or organized violent crimes, the People's Court may, based on the circumstances of the crime, simultaneously decide to restrict commutation of punishment.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条　死刑缓期执行的期间，从判决确定之日起计算。死刑缓期执行减为有期徒刑的刑期，从死刑缓期执行期满之日起计算。

**Article 51** : The period of a death sentence with a reprieve shall be calculated from the date on which the judgment is finalized. The term of fixed-term imprisonment resulting from commutation of a death sentence with a reprieve shall be calculated from the date on which the reprieve period expires.

#### 第六节　罚金

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条　判处罚金，应当根据犯罪情节决定罚金数额。

**Article 52** : When imposing a fine, the amount of the fine shall be determined based on the circumstances of the crime.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条　罚金在判决指定的期限内一次或者分期缴纳。期满不缴纳的，强制缴纳。对于不能全部缴纳罚金的，人民法院在任何时候发现被执行人有可以执行的财产，应当随时追缴。  
由于遭遇不能抗拒的灾祸等原因缴纳确实有困难的，经人民法院裁定，可以延期缴纳、酌情减少或者免除。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-3" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 53** : A fine shall be paid in a lump sum or in installments within the period specified in the judgment. If payment is not made upon expiration, compulsory payment shall be enforced. If the fine cannot be paid in full, the People's Court shall, whenever it discovers that the person subjected to execution has executable property, pursue recovery at any time.  
If payment is genuinely difficult due to reasons such as encountering an irresistible disaster, the People's Court may, by ruling, grant an extension, reduce the amount as deemed appropriate, or exempt the payment.

#### 第七节　剥夺政治权利

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条　剥夺政治权利是剥夺下列权利：  
（一）选举权和被选举权；  
（二）言论、出版、集会、结社、游行、示威自由的权利；  
（三）担任国家机关职务的权利；  
（四）担任国有公司、企业、事业单位和人民团体领导职务的权利。

**Article 54** : Deprivation of political rights means depriving the following rights:  
(1) The right to vote and the right to be elected;  
(2) The rights to freedom of speech, press, assembly, association, procession, and demonstration;  
(3) The right to hold a position in a state organ;  
(4) The right to hold a leading position in a state-owned company, enterprise, institution, or people's organization.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条　剥夺政治权利的期限，除[本法第五十七条](#art-57)规定外，为一年以上五年以下。  
判处管制附加剥夺政治权利的，剥夺政治权利的期限与管制的期限相等，同时执行。

**Article 55** : The term of deprivation of political rights, except as provided for in Article 57 of this Law, shall be not less than one year but not more than five years.  
For a sentence of public surveillance with supplementary deprivation of political rights, the term of deprivation of political rights shall be equal to the term of public surveillance, and shall be executed concurrently.

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条　对于危害国家安全的犯罪分子应当附加剥夺政治权利；对于故意杀人、强奸、放火、爆炸、投毒、抢劫等严重破坏社会秩序的犯罪分子，可以附加剥夺政治权利。  
独立适用剥夺政治权利的，依照本法分则的规定。

**Article 56** : For criminals who endanger national security, supplementary deprivation of political rights shall be imposed; for criminals who commit serious disruption of social order such as intentional homicide, rape, arson, explosion, poisoning, or robbery, supplementary deprivation of political rights may be imposed.  
Where deprivation of political rights is applied independently, it shall be governed by the provisions of the Specific Provisions of this Law.

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条　对于被判处死刑、无期徒刑的犯罪分子，应当剥夺政治权利终身。  
在死刑缓期执行减为有期徒刑或者无期徒刑减为有期徒刑的时候，应当把附加剥夺政治权利的期限改为三年以上十年以下。

**Article 57** : For criminals sentenced to death or life imprisonment, political rights shall be deprived for life.  
When a death sentence with a reprieve is commuted to fixed-term imprisonment or life imprisonment is commuted to fixed-term imprisonment, the term of supplementary deprivation of political rights shall be modified to not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条　附加剥夺政治权利的刑期，从徒刑、拘役执行完毕之日或者从假释之日起计算；剥夺政治权利的效力当然施用于主刑执行期间。  
被剥夺政治权利的犯罪分子，在执行期间，应当遵守法律、行政法规和国务院公安部门有关监督管理的规定，服从监督；不得行使[本法第五十四条](#art-54)规定的各项权利。

**Article 58** : The term of supplementary deprivation of political rights shall be calculated from the date on which imprisonment or criminal detention is fully executed or from the date of parole; the effect of deprivation of political rights naturally applies during the period of execution of the principal punishment.  
A criminal who is deprived of political rights shall, during the execution period, observe the laws, administrative regulations, and the provisions of the public security department of the State Council regarding supervision and management, and submit to supervision; and shall not exercise any of the rights specified in Article 54 of this Law.

#### 第八节　没收财产

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条　没收财产是没收犯罪分子个人所有财产的一部或者全部。没收全部财产的，应当对犯罪分子个人及其扶养的家属保留必需的生活费用。  
在判处没收财产的时候，不得没收属于犯罪分子家属所有或者应有的财产。

**Article 59** : Confiscation of property means confiscating part or all of the property personally owned by the criminal. If all property is confiscated, necessary living expenses shall be reserved for the criminal personally and the family members whom he or she supports.  
When imposing confiscation of property, property that belongs to or should belong to the family members of the criminal shall not be confiscated.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条　没收财产以前犯罪分子所负的正当债务，需要以没收的财产偿还的，经债权人请求，应当偿还。

**Article 60** : Where a legitimate debt incurred by the criminal before the confiscation of property needs to be repaid with the confiscated property, it shall be repaid upon the request of the creditor.

### 第四章　刑罚的具体运用

#### 第一节　量刑

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条　对于犯罪分子决定刑罚的时候，应当根据犯罪的事实、犯罪的性质、情节和对于社会的危害程度，依照本法的有关规定判处。

**Article 61** : When deciding punishment for a criminal, it shall be sentenced in accordance with the relevant provisions of this Law, based on the facts of the crime, the nature of the crime, the circumstances, and the degree of harm to society.

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条　犯罪分子具有本法规定的从重处罚、从轻处罚情节的，应当在法定刑的限度以内判处刑罚。

**Article 62** : If a criminal has circumstances for severe punishment or light punishment as provided for in this Law, the punishment shall be imposed within the limits of the statutory punishment.

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条　犯罪分子具有本法规定的减轻处罚情节的，应当在法定刑以下判处刑罚；本法规定有数个量刑幅度的，应当在法定量刑幅度的下一个量刑幅度内判处刑罚。  
犯罪分子虽然不具有本法规定的减轻处罚情节，但是根据案件的特殊情况，经最高人民法院核准，也可以在法定刑以下判处刑罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-5" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第5条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 63** : If a criminal has circumstances for mitigated punishment as provided for in this Law, the punishment shall be imposed below the statutory punishment; if this Law provides for several sentencing ranges, the punishment shall be imposed within the next lower sentencing range below the statutory sentencing range.  
Even if a criminal does not have circumstances for mitigated punishment as provided for in this Law, based on the special circumstances of the case and upon approval by the Supreme People's Court, punishment may also be imposed below the statutory punishment.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条　犯罪分子违法所得的一切财物，应当予以追缴或者责令退赔；对被害人的合法财产，应当及时返还；违禁品和供犯罪所用的本人财物，应当予以没收。没收的财物和罚金，一律上缴国库，不得挪用和自行处理。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-11" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第11条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于适用刑法第六十四条有关问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用刑法第六十四条有关问题的批复》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 64** : All property obtained illegally by a criminal shall be recovered or ordered to be refunded; the lawful property of the victim shall be returned in a timely manner; contraband and the criminal's own property used for the crime shall be confiscated. All confiscated property and fines shall be turned over to the state treasury and shall not be misappropriated or disposed of without authorization.

#### 第二节　累犯

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条　被判处有期徒刑以上刑罚的犯罪分子，刑罚执行完毕或者赦免以后，在五年以内再犯应当判处有期徒刑以上刑罚之罪的，是累犯，应当从重处罚，但是过失犯罪和不满十八周岁的人犯罪的除外。  
前款规定的期限，对于被假释的犯罪分子，从假释期满之日起计算。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民检察院关于认定累犯如何确定刑罚执行完毕以后“五年以内”起始日期的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于认定累犯如何确定刑罚执行完毕以后“五年以内”起始日期的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院+最高人民检察院关于缓刑犯在考验期满后五年内再犯应当判处有期徒刑以上刑罚之罪应否认定为累犯问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院+最高人民检察院关于缓刑犯在考验期满后五年内再犯应当判处有期徒刑以上刑罚之罪应否认定为累犯问题的批复》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释》第3条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-6" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第6条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 65** : If a criminal who has been sentenced to a punishment of fixed-term imprisonment or more, within five years after the completion of the execution of the punishment or after receiving a pardon, commits another crime for which he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment or more, that criminal is a recidivist and shall be subject to severe punishment; however, this shall not apply to negligent crimes or crimes committed by persons under the age of 18.  
The period specified in the preceding paragraph shall, with respect to a criminal who is paroled, be calculated from the date on which the parole period expires.

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条　危害国家安全犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪、黑社会性质的组织犯罪的犯罪分子，在刑罚执行完毕或者赦免以后，在任何时候再犯上述任一类罪的，都以累犯论处。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院研究室关于对再审改判前因犯新罪被加刑的罪犯再审时如何确定执行的刑罚问题的电话答复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院研究室关于对再审改判前因犯新罪被加刑的罪犯再审时如何确定执行的刑罚问题的电话答复》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第7条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 66** : If a criminal who commits a crime of endangering national security, a crime of terrorist activities, or a crime of an organized crime of a mafia nature, after the completion of the execution of the punishment or after receiving a pardon, commits any of the above types of crimes at any time, he or she shall be treated as a recidivist.

#### 第三节　自首和立功

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条　犯罪以后自动投案，如实供述自己的罪行的，是自首。对于自首的犯罪分子，可以从轻或者减轻处罚。其中，犯罪较轻的，可以免除处罚。  
被采取强制措施的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人和正在服刑的罪犯，如实供述司法机关还未掌握的本人其他罪行的，以自首论。  
犯罪嫌疑人虽不具有前两款规定的自首情节，但是如实供述自己罪行的，可以从轻处罚；因其如实供述自己罪行，避免特别严重后果发生的，可以减轻处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第8条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释》第4条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-8" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第8条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 67** : Voluntary surrender after committing a crime and truthful confession of one's own crime constitutes voluntary surrender. For a criminal who voluntarily surrenders, punishment may be lightened or mitigated. Among them, for a minor crime, punishment may be exempted.  
If a criminal suspect, defendant, or convict serving a sentence who is subjected to compulsory measures truthfully confesses other crimes of his or her own that are not yet known to the judicial organs, he or she shall be treated as having voluntarily surrendered.  
Even if a criminal suspect does not have the circumstances of voluntary surrender as provided for in the preceding two paragraphs, if he or she truthfully confesses his or her own crime, punishment may be lightened; if, because of his or her truthful confession, particularly serious consequences are avoided, punishment may be mitigated.

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条　犯罪分子有揭发他人犯罪行为，查证属实的，或者提供重要线索，从而得以侦破其他案件等立功表现的，可以从轻或者减轻处罚；有重大立功表现的，可以减轻或者免除处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释.md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释》第5条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-9" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第9条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 68** : If a criminal performs meritorious service, such as exposing the criminal conduct of another person, which is verified as true, or providing important clues, thereby enabling the detection of other cases, punishment may be lightened or mitigated; if major meritorious service is performed, punishment may be mitigated or exempted.

#### 第四节　数罪并罚

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条　判决宣告以前一人犯数罪的，除判处死刑和无期徒刑的以外，应当在总和刑期以下、数刑中最高刑期以上，酌情决定执行的刑期，但是管制最高不能超过三年，拘役最高不能超过一年，有期徒刑总和刑期不满三十五年的，最高不能超过二十年，总和刑期在三十五年以上的，最高不能超过二十五年。  
数罪中有判处有期徒刑和拘役的，执行有期徒刑。数罪中有判处有期徒刑和管制，或者拘役和管制的，有期徒刑、拘役执行完毕后，管制仍须执行。  
数罪中有判处附加刑的，附加刑仍须执行，其中附加刑种类相同的，合并执行，种类不同的，分别执行。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-4" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第4条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-10" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第10条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 69** : If a person commits several crimes before the judgment is pronounced, except for those sentenced to death or life imprisonment, the term of execution shall be determined, below the total term of the sentences but above the highest term among the several sentences, as appropriate, subject to a maximum of three years for public surveillance, a maximum of one year for criminal detention, and a maximum of twenty years for fixed-term imprisonment if the total term is less than thirty-five years, or a maximum of twenty-five years if the total term is thirty-five years or more.  
If among the several crimes, some are punishable by fixed-term imprisonment and some by criminal detention, fixed-term imprisonment shall be executed. If among the several crimes, some are punishable by fixed-term imprisonment and public surveillance, or by criminal detention and public surveillance, after the fixed-term imprisonment or criminal detention is fully executed, the public surveillance shall still be executed.  
If among the several crimes, supplementary punishments are imposed, the supplementary punishments shall still be executed; where the types of supplementary punishments are the same, they shall be executed concurrently; where the types differ, they shall be executed separately.

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条　判决宣告以后，刑罚执行完毕以前，发现被判刑的犯罪分子在判决宣告以前还有其他罪没有判决的，应当对新发现的罪作出判决，把前后两个判决所判处的刑罚，依照[本法第六十九条](#art-69)的规定，决定执行的刑罚。已经执行的刑期，应当计算在新判决决定的刑期以内。

**Article 70** : If, after a judgment is pronounced but before the execution of the punishment is completed, it is discovered that the sentenced criminal committed other crimes before the judgment was pronounced that were not adjudicated, a judgment shall be made on the newly discovered crime, and the punishment to be executed shall be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 69 of this Law, taking the punishments imposed by the first and second judgments together. The term already executed shall be counted within the term decided by the new judgment.

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条　判决宣告以后，刑罚执行完毕以前，被判刑的犯罪分子又犯罪的，应当对新犯的罪作出判决，把前罪没有执行的刑罚和后罪所判处的刑罚，依照[本法第六十九条](#art-69)的规定，决定执行的刑罚。

**Article 71** : If, after a judgment is pronounced but before the execution of the punishment is completed, the sentenced criminal commits another crime, a judgment shall be made on the newly committed crime, and the punishment to be executed shall be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 69 of this Law, taking the unexecuted punishment for the prior crime and the punishment imposed for the new crime together.

#### 第五节　缓刑

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条　对于被判处拘役、三年以下有期徒刑的犯罪分子，同时符合下列条件的，可以宣告缓刑，对其中不满十八周岁的人、怀孕的妇女和已满七十五周岁的人，应当宣告缓刑：  
（一）犯罪情节较轻；  
（二）有悔罪表现；  
（三）没有再犯罪的危险；  
（四）宣告缓刑对所居住社区没有重大不良影响。  
宣告缓刑，可以根据犯罪情况，同时禁止犯罪分子在缓刑考验期限内从事特定活动，进入特定区域、场所，接触特定的人。  
被宣告缓刑的犯罪分子，如果被判处附加刑，附加刑仍须执行。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-11" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第11条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 72** : For a criminal sentenced to criminal detention or fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, if the following conditions are met concurrently, probation may be declared; for a person under the age of 18, a pregnant woman, or a person who has reached the age of 75 among them, probation shall be declared:  
(1) The circumstances of the crime are minor;  
(2) There is a showing of repentance;  
(3) There is no danger of committing another crime;  
(4) The declaration of probation does not have a major adverse impact on the community where the person resides.  
When declaring probation, the criminal may, based on the circumstances of the crime, be concurrently prohibited from engaging in specific activities, entering specific areas or places, or contacting specific persons during the probation period.  
If a criminal placed on probation is also sentenced to supplementary punishment, the supplementary punishment shall still be executed.

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条　拘役的缓刑考验期限为原判刑期以上一年以下，但是不能少于二个月。  
有期徒刑的缓刑考验期限为原判刑期以上五年以下，但是不能少于一年。  
缓刑考验期限，从判决确定之日起计算。

**Article 73** : The probation period for criminal detention shall be not less than the original sentence but not more than one year, and shall in no case be less than two months.  
The probation period for fixed-term imprisonment shall be not less than the original sentence but not more than five years, and shall in no case be less than one year.  
The probation period shall be calculated from the date on which the judgment is finalized.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条　对于累犯和犯罪集团的首要分子，不适用缓刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-12" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第12条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 74** : Probation shall not apply to recidivists or ringleaders of criminal groups.

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条　被宣告缓刑的犯罪分子，应当遵守下列规定：  
（一）遵守法律、行政法规，服从监督；  
（二）按照考察机关的规定报告自己的活动情况；  
（三）遵守考察机关关于会客的规定；  
（四）离开所居住的市、县或者迁居，应当报经考察机关批准。

**Article 75** : A criminal placed on probation shall observe the following provisions:  
(1) Observe laws and administrative regulations, and submit to supervision;  
(2) Report his or her activities in accordance with the provisions of the supervisory authority;  
(3) Observe the provisions of the supervisory authority regarding receiving visitors;  
(4) Report to and obtain approval from the supervisory authority before leaving the city or county of residence or changing residence.

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条　对宣告缓刑的犯罪分子，在缓刑考验期限内，依法实行社区矫正，如果没有[本法第七十七条](#art-77)规定的情形，缓刑考验期满，原判的刑罚就不再执行，并公开予以宣告。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于不再追诉去台人员在中华人民共和国成立前的犯罪行为的公告.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于不再追诉去台人员在中华人民共和国成立前的犯罪行为的公告》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于不再追诉去台人员在中华人民共和国成立后当地人民政权建立前的犯罪行为的公告.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于不再追诉去台人员在中华人民共和国成立后当地人民政权建立前的犯罪行为的公告》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-13" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第13条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 76** : For a criminal placed on probation, community correction shall be implemented according to law during the probation period. If there are no circumstances as provided for in Article 77 of this Law, upon the expiration of the probation period, the original sentence shall no longer be executed, and it shall be publicly declared.

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条　被宣告缓刑的犯罪分子，在缓刑考验期限内犯新罪或者发现判决宣告以前还有其他罪没有判决的，应当撤销缓刑，对新犯的罪或者新发现的罪作出判决，把前罪和后罪所判处的刑罚，依照[本法第六十九条](#art-69)的规定，决定执行的刑罚。  
被宣告缓刑的犯罪分子，在缓刑考验期限内，违反法律、行政法规或者国务院有关部门关于缓刑的监督管理规定，或者违反人民法院判决中的禁止令，情节严重的，应当撤销缓刑，执行原判刑罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-13" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第13条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-14" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第14条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 77** : If a criminal placed on probation commits a new crime during the probation period, or if it is discovered before the probation period expires that other crimes were committed before the judgment was pronounced that were not adjudicated, the probation shall be revoked, a judgment shall be made on the new crime or the newly discovered crime, and the punishment to be executed shall be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 69 of this Law, taking the punishments imposed for the prior and subsequent crimes together.  
If a criminal placed on probation, during the probation period, violates the laws, administrative regulations, or the supervision and management provisions of the relevant departments of the State Council regarding probation, or violates a prohibition order in the People's Court judgment, and the circumstances are serious, the probation shall be revoked, and the original sentence shall be executed.

#### 第六节　减刑

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条　被判处管制、拘役、有期徒刑、无期徒刑的犯罪分子，在执行期间，如果认真遵守监规，接受教育改造，确有悔改表现的，或者有立功表现的，可以减刑；有下列重大立功表现之一的，应当减刑：  
（一）阻止他人重大犯罪活动的；  
（二）检举监狱内外重大犯罪活动，经查证属实的；  
（三）有发明创造或者重大技术革新的；  
（四）在日常生产、生活中舍己救人的；  
（五）在抗御自然灾害或者排除重大事故中，有突出表现的；  
（六）对国家和社会有其他重大贡献的。  
减刑以后实际执行的刑期不能少于下列期限：  
（一）判处管制、拘役、有期徒刑的，不能少于原判刑期的二分之一；  
（二）判处无期徒刑的，不能少于十三年；  
（三）人民法院依照[本法第五十条](#art-50)第二款规定限制减刑的死刑缓期执行的犯罪分子，缓期执行期满后依法减为无期徒刑的，不能少于二十五年，缓期执行期满后依法减为二十五年有期徒刑的，不能少于二十年。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-15" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第15条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 78** : For a criminal sentenced to public surveillance, criminal detention, fixed-term imprisonment, or life imprisonment, during the execution period, if he or she earnestly observes the prison regulations, accepts education and reform, and shows genuine repentance, or if he or she performs meritorious service, the punishment may be commuted; if any of the following major meritorious service is performed, the punishment shall be commuted:  
(1) Preventing another person from committing a major criminal activity;  
(2) Reporting major criminal activities inside or outside prison, which is verified as true;  
(3) Making an invention or significant technical innovation;  
(4) Sacrificing oneself to save others in daily production or life;  
(5) Making outstanding contributions in resisting natural disasters or eliminating major accidents;  
(6) Making other major contributions to the state and society.  
The actual term of execution after commutation shall not be less than the following periods:  
(1) For those sentenced to public surveillance, criminal detention, or fixed-term imprisonment, not less than one-half of the original sentence;  
(2) For those sentenced to life imprisonment, not less than 13 years;  
(3) For criminals sentenced to death with a reprieve for whom the People's Court, in accordance with the provisions of paragraph 2 of Article 50 of this Law, restricts commutation, after the reprieve period expires and it is commuted to life imprisonment according to law, not less than 25 years; after the reprieve period expires and it is commuted to 25 years of fixed-term imprisonment according to law, not less than 20 years.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条　对于犯罪分子的减刑，由执行机关向中级以上人民法院提出减刑建议书。人民法院应当组成合议庭进行审理，对确有悔改或者立功事实的，裁定予以减刑。非经法定程序不得减刑。

**Article 79** : For commutation of punishment for a criminal, the executing organ shall submit a proposal for commutation to an Intermediate People's Court or a higher People's Court. The People's Court shall form a collegial panel to hear the case, and if there is indeed fact of repentance or meritorious service, it shall rule to grant commutation. No commutation shall be granted without legal procedures.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条　无期徒刑减为有期徒刑的刑期，从裁定减刑之日起计算。

**Article 80** : The term of fixed-term imprisonment resulting from commutation of life imprisonment shall be calculated from the date on which the ruling of commutation is made.

#### 第七节　假释

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条　被判处有期徒刑的犯罪分子，执行原判刑期二分之一以上，被判处无期徒刑的犯罪分子，实际执行十三年以上，如果认真遵守监规，接受教育改造，确有悔改表现，没有再犯罪的危险的，可以假释。如果有特殊情况，经最高人民法院核准，可以不受上述执行刑期的限制。  
对累犯以及因故意杀人、强奸、抢劫、绑架、放火、爆炸、投放危险物质或者有组织的暴力性犯罪被判处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑的犯罪分子，不得假释。  
对犯罪分子决定假释时，应当考虑其假释后对所居住社区的影响。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于《中华人民共和国刑法修正案(八)》时间效力问题的解释》第8条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-16" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第16条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 81** A criminal sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment who has served more than half of the term of the original sentence, or a criminal sentenced to life imprisonment who has actually served not less than thirteen years, may be granted parole if he or she earnestly observes prison regulations, accepts education and reform, shows true repentance, and has no danger of committing another crime. If there are special circumstances, and with the approval of the Supreme People's Court, the above restrictions on the term of sentence already served may not apply.  
Parole shall not be granted to recidivists or to criminals sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment for intentional homicide, rape, robbery, kidnapping, arson, explosion, dissemination of dangerous substances, or organized violent crimes.  
When deciding to grant parole to a criminal, consideration shall be given to the impact of such parole on the community where the criminal will reside.

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条　对于犯罪分子的假释，依照[本法第七十九条](#art-79)规定的程序进行。非经法定程序不得假释。

**Article 82** Parole for a criminal shall be carried out in accordance with the procedure prescribed in Article 79 of this Law. Parole shall not be granted without legal procedures.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条　有期徒刑的假释考验期限，为没有执行完毕的刑期；无期徒刑的假释考验期限为十年。  
假释考验期限，从假释之日起计算。

**Article 83** The probation period for parole in the case of fixed-term imprisonment shall be the portion of the sentence that has not been completed; the probation period for parole in the case of life imprisonment shall be ten years.  
The probation period for parole shall be counted from the date on which the parole is granted.

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条　被宣告假释的犯罪分子，应当遵守下列规定：  
（一）遵守法律、行政法规，服从监督；  
（二）按照监督机关的规定报告自己的活动情况；  
（三）遵守监督机关关于会客的规定；  
（四）离开所居住的市、县或者迁居，应当报经监督机关批准。

**Article 84** A criminal who is granted parole shall observe the following provisions:  
(1) Observe laws and administrative regulations, and submit to supervision;  
(2) Report his or her activities in accordance with the regulations of the supervisory authority;  
(3) Observe the regulations of the supervisory authority concerning meeting visitors; and  
(4) Obtain approval from the supervisory authority before leaving the city or county where he or she resides or moving to another place.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条　对假释的犯罪分子，在假释考验期限内，依法实行社区矫正，如果没有[本法第八十六条](#art-86)规定的情形，假释考验期满，就认为原判刑罚已经执行完毕，并公开予以宣告。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-17" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第17条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 85** For a criminal who is granted parole, community correction shall be carried out according to law during the probation period for parole. If there is no circumstance as provided for in Article 86 of this Law, upon the expiration of the probation period for parole, the original sentence shall be considered to have been fully executed, and this shall be publicly announced.

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条　被假释的犯罪分子，在假释考验期限内犯新罪，应当撤销假释，依照[本法第七十一条](#art-71)的规定实行数罪并罚。  
在假释考验期限内，发现被假释的犯罪分子在判决宣告以前还有其他罪没有判决的，应当撤销假释，依照[本法第七十条](#art-70)的规定实行数罪并罚。  
被假释的犯罪分子，在假释考验期限内，有违反法律、行政法规或者国务院有关部门关于假释的监督管理规定的行为，尚未构成新的犯罪的，应当依照法定程序撤销假释，收监执行未执行完毕的刑罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-17" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第17条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-18" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第18条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 86** If a criminal who is granted parole commits a new crime during the probation period for parole, the parole shall be revoked, and concurrent punishment for multiple crimes shall be imposed in accordance with the provisions of Article 71 of this Law.  
If, during the probation period for parole, it is discovered that the criminal who is granted parole had other crimes for which no judgment has been rendered before the judgment was pronounced, the parole shall be revoked, and concurrent punishment for multiple crimes shall be imposed in accordance with the provisions of Article 70 of this Law.  
If a criminal who is granted parole, during the probation period for parole, violates laws, administrative regulations, or the supervision and administration provisions of the relevant departments of the State Council on parole, which does not constitute a new crime, the parole shall be revoked according to legal procedures, and the criminal shall be taken back to prison to serve the portion of the sentence that has not been executed.

#### 第八节　时效

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条　犯罪经过下列期限不再追诉：  
（一）法定最高刑为不满五年有期徒刑的，经过五年；  
（二）法定最高刑为五年以上不满十年有期徒刑的，经过十年；  
（三）法定最高刑为十年以上有期徒刑的，经过十五年；  
（四）法定最高刑为无期徒刑、死刑的，经过二十年。如果二十年以后认为必须追诉的，须报请最高人民检察院核准。

**Article 87** A crime shall no longer be prosecuted after the following periods:  
(1) Five years, if the maximum statutory punishment is fixed-term imprisonment of less than five years;  
(2) Ten years, if the maximum statutory punishment is fixed-term imprisonment of five years or more but less than ten years;  
(3) Fifteen years, if the maximum statutory punishment is fixed-term imprisonment of ten years or more; and  
(4) Twenty years, if the maximum statutory punishment is life imprisonment or death penalty. If prosecution is deemed necessary after twenty years, it must be submitted to the Supreme People's Procuratorate for approval.

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条　在人民检察院、公安机关、国家安全机关立案侦查或者在人民法院受理案件以后，逃避侦查或者审判的，不受追诉期限的限制。  
被害人在追诉期限内提出控告，人民法院、人民检察院、公安机关应当立案而不予立案的，不受追诉期限的限制。

**Article 88** If a person escapes from investigation or adjudication after a case has been filed for investigation by the People's Procuratorate, public security organ, or state security organ, or after a People's Court has accepted the case, the statute of limitations for prosecution shall not apply.  
If the victim brings a complaint within the limitation period for prosecution, and the People's Court, People's Procuratorate, or public security organ should have filed the case but fails to do so, the statute of limitations for prosecution shall not apply.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条　追诉期限从犯罪之日起计算；犯罪行为有连续或者继续状态的，从犯罪行为终了之日起计算。  
在追诉期限以内又犯罪的，前罪追诉的期限从犯后罪之日起计算。

**Article 89** The limitation period for prosecution shall be counted from the date of the crime; if the criminal act is continuous or ongoing, it shall be counted from the date on which the criminal act ends.  
If a person commits another crime within the limitation period for prosecution, the limitation period for prosecution of the previous crime shall be counted from the date on which the subsequent crime is committed.

### 第五章　其他规定

<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条　民族自治地方不能全部适用本法规定的，可以由自治区或者省的人民代表大会根据当地民族的政治、经济、文化的特点和本法规定的基本原则，制定变通或者补充的规定，报请全国人民代表大会常务委员会批准施行。

**Article 90** If the provisions of this Law cannot be fully applied in national autonomous areas, the People's Congress of the autonomous region or province may, based on the political, economic, and cultural characteristics of the local ethnic group and the basic principles of this Law, formulate adaptive or supplementary provisions, which shall be submitted to the Standing Committee of the National People's Congress for approval before coming into force.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条　本法所称公共财产，是指下列财产：  
（一）国有财产；  
（二）劳动群众集体所有的财产；  
（三）用于扶贫和其他公益事业的社会捐助或者专项基金的财产。  
在国家机关、国有公司、企业、集体企业和人民团体管理、使用或者运输中的私人财产，以公共财产论。

**Article 91** For the purposes of this Law, "public property" means the following property:  
(1) State-owned property;  
(2) Property collectively owned by the working masses;  
(3) Property donated for poverty alleviation and other public welfare undertakings or special fund property.  
Private property under the management, use, or transportation of state organs, state-owned companies, enterprises, collective enterprises, or people's organizations shall be treated as public property.

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条　本法所称公民私人所有的财产，是指下列财产：  
（一）公民的合法收入、储蓄、房屋和其他生活资料；  
（二）依法归个人、家庭所有的生产资料；  
（三）个体户和私营企业的合法财产；  
（四）依法归个人所有的股份、股票、债券和其他财产。

**Article 92** For the purposes of this Law, "property privately owned by citizens" means the following property:  
(1) Citizens' lawful income, savings, housing, and other means of livelihood;  
(2) Means of production owned by individuals or families according to law;  
(3) Lawful property of individual businesses and private enterprises; and  
(4) Shares, stocks, bonds, and other property owned by individuals according to law.

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条　本法所称国家工作人员，是指国家机关中从事公务的人员。  
国有公司、企业、事业单位、人民团体中从事公务的人员和国家机关、国有公司、企业、事业单位委派到非国有公司、企业、事业单位、社会团体从事公务的人员，以及其他依照法律从事公务的人员，以国家工作人员论。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/法律解释/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第九十三条第二款的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第九十三条第二款的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 93** For the purposes of this Law, "state personnel" means persons engaged in public service in state organs.  
Persons engaged in public service in state-owned companies, enterprises, institutions, or people's organizations, persons dispatched by state organs, state-owned companies, enterprises, or institutions to engage in public service in non-state-owned companies, enterprises, institutions, or social organizations, and other persons engaged in public service according to law, shall be treated as state personnel.

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条　本法所称司法工作人员，是指有侦查、检察、审判、监管职责的工作人员。

**Article 94** For the purposes of this Law, "judicial personnel" means persons who have the duties of investigation, prosecution, adjudication, or supervision.

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条　本法所称重伤，是指有下列情形之一的伤害：  
（一）使人肢体残废或者毁人容貌的；  
（二）使人丧失听觉、视觉或者其他器官机能的；  
（三）其他对于人身健康有重大伤害的。

**Article 95** For the purposes of this Law, "serious injury" means injury in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) Causing disability of a person's limb or disfigurement of a person's face;  
(2) Causing loss of hearing, vision, or function of any other organ; or  
(3) Other serious harm to a person's health.

<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条　本法所称违反国家规定，是指违反全国人民代表大会及其常务委员会制定的法律和决定，国务院制定的行政法规、规定的行政措施、发布的决定和命令。

**Article 96** For the purposes of this Law, "violation of state regulations" means violation of the laws and decisions enacted by the National People's Congress or its Standing Committee, or the administrative regulations, prescribed administrative measures, decisions, and orders issued by the State Council.

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条　本法所称首要分子，是指在犯罪集团或者聚众犯罪中起组织、策划、指挥作用的犯罪分子。

**Article 97** For the purposes of this Law, "principal offender" means a criminal who plays an organizing, planning, or directing role in a criminal group or in the commission of a crime by a mob.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条　本法所称告诉才处理，是指被害人告诉才处理。如果被害人因受强制、威吓无法告诉的，人民检察院和被害人的近亲属也可以告诉。

**Article 98** For the purposes of this Law, "to be handled only upon complaint" means that the case shall only be handled if the victim brings a complaint. If the victim is unable to bring a complaint due to coercion or intimidation, the People's Procuratorate and the close relatives of the victim may also bring the complaint.

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条　本法所称以上、以下、以内，包括本数。

**Article 99** For the purposes of this Law, "or more," "or less," and "within" include the given number itself.

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条　依法受过刑事处罚的人，在入伍、就业的时候，应当如实向有关单位报告自己曾受过刑事处罚，不得隐瞒。  
犯罪的时候不满十八周岁被判处五年有期徒刑以下刑罚的人，免除前款规定的报告义务。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-19" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第19条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 100** A person who has been subjected to criminal punishment according to law shall, when enlisting in the military or seeking employment, truthfully report the criminal punishment he or she has received to the relevant unit, and shall not conceal it.  
A person who was under the age of 18 at the time of the crime and was sentenced to a punishment of fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years shall be exempt from the reporting obligation provided for in the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条　本法总则适用于其他有刑罚规定的法律，但是其他法律有特别规定的除外。

**Article 101** The General Provisions of this Law shall apply to other laws that provide for criminal penalties, unless otherwise specifically provided in such other laws.

## 第二编　分则

### 第一章　危害国家安全罪

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条　勾结外国，危害中华人民共和国的主权、领土完整和安全的，处无期徒刑或者十年以上有期徒刑。  
与境外机构、组织、个人相勾结，犯前款罪的，依照前款的规定处罚。

**Article 102** Whoever colludes with a foreign state to endanger the sovereignty, territorial integrity, and security of the People's Republic of China shall be sentenced to life imprisonment or fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years.  
Whoever colludes with an overseas institution, organization, or individual to commit the crime provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条　组织、策划、实施分裂国家、破坏国家统一的，对首要分子或者罪行重大的，处无期徒刑或者十年以上有期徒刑；对积极参加的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；对其他参加的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利。  
煽动分裂国家、破坏国家统一的，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；首要分子或者罪行重大的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 103** Whoever organizes, plans, or carries out acts to split the state or undermine national unity shall, if he or she is a principal offender or if his or her crime is major, be sentenced to life imprisonment or fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years; if he or she actively participates, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; and if he or she otherwise participates, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights.  
Whoever incites the splitting of the state or the undermining of national unity shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights; if he or she is a principal offender or if his or her crime is major, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条　组织、策划、实施武装叛乱或者武装暴乱的，对首要分子或者罪行重大的，处无期徒刑或者十年以上有期徒刑；对积极参加的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；对其他参加的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利。  
策动、胁迫、勾引、收买国家机关工作人员、武装部队人员、人民警察、民兵进行武装叛乱或者武装暴乱的，依照前款的规定从重处罚。

**Article 104** Whoever organizes, plans, or carries out armed rebellion or armed riot shall, if he or she is a principal offender or if his or her crime is major, be sentenced to life imprisonment or fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years; if he or she actively participates, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; and if he or she otherwise participates, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights.  
Whoever incites, coerces, lures, or bribes personnel of state organs, armed forces, people's police, or the militia to engage in armed rebellion or armed riot shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条　组织、策划、实施颠覆国家政权、推翻社会主义制度的，对首要分子或者罪行重大的，处无期徒刑或者十年以上有期徒刑；对积极参加的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；对其他参加的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利。  
以造谣、诽谤或者其他方式煽动颠覆国家政权、推翻社会主义制度的，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；首要分子或者罪行重大的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 105** Whoever organizes, plans, or carries out acts to subvert state power or overthrow the socialist system shall, if he or she is a principal offender or if his or her crime is major, be sentenced to life imprisonment or fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years; if he or she actively participates, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; and if he or she otherwise participates, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights.  
Whoever incites the subversion of state power or the overthrow of the socialist system by spreading rumors, defamation, or other means shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights; if he or she is a principal offender or if his or her crime is major, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条　与境外机构、组织、个人相勾结，实施本章第一百零三条、第一百零四条、第一百零五条规定之罪的，依照各该条的规定从重处罚。

**Article 106** Whoever colludes with an overseas institution, organization, or individual to commit the crimes provided for in Articles 103, 104, and 105 of this Chapter shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of those respective articles.

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条　境内外机构、组织或者个人资助实施本章第一百零二条、第一百零三条、第一百零四条、第一百零五条规定之罪的，对直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；情节严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-20" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第20条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 107** Where any institution, organization, or individual, whether within or outside the territory, provides financial support for the commission of the crimes provided for in Articles 102, 103, 104, and 105 of this Chapter, the person directly responsible shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights; if the circumstances are serious, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条　投敌叛变的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节严重或者带领武装部队人员、人民警察、民兵投敌叛变的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑。

**Article 108** Whoever defects to the enemy and betrays his or her country shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; if the circumstances are serious, or if he or she leads personnel of the armed forces, people's police, or the militia to defect to the enemy and betray the country, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment.

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条　国家机关工作人员在履行公务期间，擅离岗位，叛逃境外或者在境外叛逃的，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
掌握国家秘密的国家工作人员叛逃境外或者在境外叛逃的，依照前款的规定从重处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-21" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第21条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 109** Any state personnel who, during the execution of official duties, deserts his or her post and defects abroad or defects while abroad shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights; if the circumstances are serious, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years.  
Any state personnel who holds state secrets and defects abroad or defects while abroad shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-110"></a>第一百一十条　有下列间谍行为之一，危害国家安全的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑；情节较轻的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑：  
（一）参加间谍组织或者接受间谍组织及其代理人的任务的；  
（二）为敌人指示轰击目标的。

**Article 110** Whoever commits any of the following acts of espionage that endanger national security shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment; if the circumstances are minor, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years:  
(1) Joining an espionage organization or accepting tasks from an espionage organization or its agent; or  
(2) Directing the enemy to targets for attack.

<a id="art-111"></a>第一百一十一条　为境外的机构、组织、人员窃取、刺探、收买、非法提供国家秘密或者情报的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑；情节较轻的，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利。

**Article 111** Whoever steals, spies, buys, or unlawfully provides state secrets or intelligence for an overseas institution, organization, or personnel shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; if the circumstances are especially serious, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment; if the circumstances are minor, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights.

<a id="art-112"></a>第一百一十二条　战时供给敌人武器装备、军用物资资敌的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑；情节较轻的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 112** Whoever, during wartime, supplies weapons, equipment, or military materials to the enemy to aid the enemy shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment; if the circumstances are minor, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-113"></a>第一百一十三条　本章上述危害国家安全罪行中，除第一百零三条第二款、第一百零五条、第一百零七条、第一百零九条外，对国家和人民危害特别严重、情节特别恶劣的，可以判处死刑。  
犯本章之罪的，可以并处没收财产。

**Article 113** Among the crimes of endangering national security provided for in the above articles of this Chapter, except for paragraph 2 of Article 103, Article 105, Article 107, and Article 109, if the crime causes especially serious harm to the state and the people, or the circumstances are especially flagrant, the death penalty may be imposed.  
Whoever commits a crime provided for in this Chapter may also be sentenced to confiscation of property.

### 第二章　危害公共安全罪

<a id="art-114"></a>第一百一十四条　放火、决水、爆炸以及投放毒害性、放射性、传染病病原体等物质或者以其他危险方法危害公共安全，尚未造成严重后果的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（三）.md#art-1" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（三）》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 114** Whoever commits arson, breaches a dike, causes an explosion, disseminates toxic, radioactive, or infectious disease pathogen substances, or uses other dangerous means to endanger public safety, if no serious consequences have yet resulted, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-115"></a>第一百一十五条　放火、决水、爆炸以及投放毒害性、放射性、传染病病原体等物质或者以其他危险方法致人重伤、死亡或者使公私财产遭受重大损失的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。  
过失犯前款罪的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑；情节较轻的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（三）.md#art-2" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（三）》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 115** Whoever commits arson, breaches a dike, causes an explosion, disseminates toxic, radioactive, or infectious disease pathogen substances, or uses other dangerous means to cause serious injury or death, or cause major damage to public or private property, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.  
Whoever negligently commits the crime provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years; if the circumstances are minor, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-116"></a>第一百一十六条　破坏火车、汽车、电车、船只、航空器，足以使火车、汽车、电车、船只、航空器发生倾覆、毁坏危险，尚未造成严重后果的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 116** Whoever sabotages a train, motor vehicle, tram, vessel, or aircraft, which creates a risk of overturning or destruction of the train, motor vehicle, tram, vessel, or aircraft, if no serious consequences have yet resulted, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-117"></a>第一百一十七条　破坏轨道、桥梁、隧道、公路、机场、航道、灯塔、标志或者进行其他破坏活动，足以使火车、汽车、电车、船只、航空器发生倾覆、毁坏危险，尚未造成严重后果的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 117** Whoever sabotages a track, bridge, tunnel, highway, airport, waterway, lighthouse, or sign, or engages in other sabotage activities, which creates a risk of overturning or destruction of a train, motor vehicle, tram, vessel, or aircraft, if no serious consequences have yet resulted, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-118"></a>第一百一十八条　破坏电力、燃气或者其他易燃易爆设备，危害公共安全，尚未造成严重后果的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部关于办理盗窃油气、破坏油气设备等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的意见.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部关于办理盗窃油气、破坏油气设备等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的意见》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>
