Whoever counterfeits currency and also sells or transports the counterfeit currency shall be convicted and given a severe punishment in accordance with Article 170 of this Law.

<a id="art-172"></a>第一百七十二条　明知是伪造的货币而持有、使用，数额较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处一万元以上十万元以下罚金；数额巨大的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大的，处十年以上有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产。

**Article 172** Whoever, knowing it to be counterfeit currency, holds or uses it, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined not less than RMB 10,000 but not more than RMB 100,000; if the amount is huge, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000; if the amount is particularly huge, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000 or have property confiscated.

<a id="art-173"></a>第一百七十三条　变造货币，数额较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处一万元以上十万元以下罚金；数额巨大的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金。

**Article 173** Whoever alters currency, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined not less than RMB 10,000 but not more than RMB 100,000; if the amount is huge, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000.

<a id="art-174"></a>第一百七十四条　未经国家有关主管部门批准，擅自设立商业银行、证券交易所、期货交易所、证券公司、期货经纪公司、保险公司或者其他金融机构的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金。  
伪造、变造、转让商业银行、证券交易所、期货交易所、证券公司、期货经纪公司、保险公司或者其他金融机构的经营许可证或者批准文件的，依照前款的规定处罚。  
单位犯前两款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照第一款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案.md#art-3" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 174** Whoever, without the approval of the relevant competent state authority, establishes a commercial bank, stock exchange, futures exchange, securities company, futures brokerage company, insurance company, or other financial institution shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000; if the circumstances are serious, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000.  
  
Whoever forges, alters, or transfers the business license or approval document of a commercial bank, stock exchange, futures exchange, securities company, futures brokerage company, insurance company, or other financial institution shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
  
Where a unit commits any of the crimes specified in the preceding two paragraphs, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the first paragraph.

<a id="art-175"></a>第一百七十五条　以转贷牟利为目的，套取金融机构信贷资金高利转贷他人，违法所得数额较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处违法所得一倍以上五倍以下罚金；数额巨大的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处违法所得一倍以上五倍以下罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
第一百七十五条之一　以欺骗手段取得银行或者其他金融机构贷款、票据承兑、信用证、保函等，给银行或者其他金融机构造成重大损失的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；给银行或者其他金融机构造成特别重大损失或者有其他特别严重情节的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-10" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第10条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-11" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第11条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 175** Whoever, for the purpose of relending for profit, obtains credit funds from a financial institution and relends them to another person at a high interest, if the amount of illegal income is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of illegal income; if the amount is huge, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of illegal income.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime specified in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.  
  
Article 175-1 Whoever obtains loans, bill acceptances, letters of credit, letters of guarantee, etc., from a bank or other financial institution by fraudulent means, thereby causing significant losses to the bank or other financial institution, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined; if they cause particularly significant losses to the bank or other financial institution or if other particularly serious circumstances exist, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime specified in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-176"></a>第一百七十六条　非法吸收公众存款或者变相吸收公众存款，扰乱金融秩序的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。  
有前两款行为，在提起公诉前积极退赃退赔，减少损害结果发生的，可以从轻或者减轻处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-12" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第12条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 176** Whoever illegally absorbs public deposits or does so in a disguised form, thereby disrupting financial order, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined; if the amount is huge or if other serious circumstances exist, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined; if the amount is particularly huge or if other particularly serious circumstances exist, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, and shall also be fined.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime specified in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
  
Where, before the initiation of a public prosecution, the person voluntarily returns the illegal gains and compensates for the losses, thereby reducing the occurrence of harmful consequences, the punishment may be mitigated or reduced.

<a id="art-177"></a>第一百七十七条　有下列情形之一，伪造、变造金融票证的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产：  
（一）伪造、变造汇票、本票、支票的；  
（二）伪造、变造委托收款凭证、汇款凭证、银行存单等其他银行结算凭证的；  
（三）伪造、变造信用证或者附随的单据、文件的；  
（四）伪造信用卡的。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。  
第一百七十七条之一　有下列情形之一，妨害信用卡管理的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处一万元以上十万元以下罚金；数量巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金：  
（一）明知是伪造的信用卡而持有、运输的，或者明知是伪造的空白信用卡而持有、运输，数量较大的；  
（二）非法持有他人信用卡，数量较大的；  
（三）使用虚假的身份证明骗领信用卡的；  
（四）出售、购买、为他人提供伪造的信用卡或者以虚假的身份证明骗领的信用卡的。  
窃取、收买或者非法提供他人信用卡信息资料的，依照前款规定处罚。  
银行或者其他金融机构的工作人员利用职务上的便利，犯第二款罪的，从重处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（五）.md#art-1" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（五）》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 177** Whoever forges or alters financial instruments under any of the following circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000; if the circumstances are serious, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000; if the circumstances are particularly serious, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000 or have property confiscated:  
  
(1) forging or altering bills of exchange, promissory notes, or cheques;  
(2) forging or altering other bank settlement vouchers such as entrusted collection vouchers, remittance vouchers, or bank deposit certificates;  
(3) forging or altering letters of credit or accompanying documents or instruments; or  
(4) forging credit cards.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime specified in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
  
Article 177-1 Whoever hinders credit card management under any of the following circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined not less than RMB 10,000 but not more than RMB 100,000; if the quantity is huge or if other serious circumstances exist, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000:  
  
(1) holding or transporting counterfeit credit cards knowing them to be counterfeit, or holding or transporting counterfeit blank credit cards knowing them to be counterfeit, in relatively large quantities;  
(2) illegally holding credit cards belonging to another person in relatively large quantities;  
(3) obtaining credit cards by using false identity certificates to defraud them; or  
(4) selling, purchasing, or providing credit cards that are counterfeited or obtained by using false identity certificates, for another person.  
  
Whoever steals, purchases, or illegally provides the information data of credit cards belonging to another person shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
  
Where an employee of a bank or other financial institution, by taking advantage of their position, commits the crime specified in the second paragraph, they shall be given a severe punishment.

<a id="art-178"></a>第一百七十八条　伪造、变造国库券或者国家发行的其他有价证券，数额较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数额巨大的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产。  
伪造、变造股票或者公司、企业债券，数额较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处一万元以上十万元以下罚金；数额巨大的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金。  
单位犯前两款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前两款的规定处罚。

**Article 178** Whoever forges or alters treasury bonds or other negotiable securities issued by the state, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000; if the amount is huge, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000; if the amount is particularly huge, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000 or have property confiscated.  
  
Whoever forges or alters shares or company or enterprise bonds, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined not less than RMB 10,000 but not more than RMB 100,000; if the amount is huge, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000.  
  
Where a unit commits any of the crimes specified in the preceding two paragraphs, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding two paragraphs.

<a id="art-179"></a>第一百七十九条　未经国家有关主管部门批准，擅自发行股票或者公司、企业债券，数额巨大、后果严重或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处非法募集资金金额百分之一以上百分之五以下罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 179** Whoever, without the approval of the relevant competent state authority, issues shares or company or enterprise bonds, if the amount is huge, the consequences are serious, or other serious circumstances exist, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined not less than one percent but not more than five percent of the illegally raised funds.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime specified in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-180"></a>第一百八十条　证券、期货交易内幕信息的知情人员或者非法获取证券、期货交易内幕信息的人员，在涉及证券的发行，证券、期货交易或者其他对证券、期货交易价格有重大影响的信息尚未公开前，买入或者卖出该证券，或者从事与该内幕信息有关的期货交易，或者泄露该信息，或者明示、暗示他人从事上述交易活动，情节严重的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处违法所得一倍以上五倍以下罚金；情节特别严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处违法所得一倍以上五倍以下罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
内幕信息、知情人员的范围，依照法律、行政法规的规定确定。  
证券交易所、期货交易所、证券公司、期货经纪公司、基金管理公司、商业银行、保险公司等金融机构的从业人员以及有关监管部门或者行业协会的工作人员，利用因职务便利获取的内幕信息以外的其他未公开的信息，违反规定，从事与该信息相关的证券、期货交易活动，或者明示、暗示他人从事相关交易活动，情节严重的，依照第一款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案.md#art-4" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-2" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 180** Where any person who has knowledge of inside information concerning securities or futures transactions, or any person who illegally obtains such inside information, before the disclosure of information that relates to the issuance of securities, securities or futures transactions, or other information having a significant impact on the price of securities or futures, buys or sells the relevant securities, or engages in futures transactions related to such inside information, or discloses such information, or expressly or impliedly suggests that another person engage in the aforementioned trading activities, if the circumstances are serious, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also or solely be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of illegal income; if the circumstances are particularly serious, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of illegal income.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime specified in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.  
  
The scope of inside information and persons with knowledge of such information shall be determined in accordance with laws and administrative regulations.  
  
Where an employee of a financial institution such as a stock exchange, futures exchange, securities company, futures brokerage company, fund management company, commercial bank, or insurance company, or an employee of a relevant regulatory department or industry association, by taking advantage of their position, obtains other undisclosed information other than inside information, and in violation of regulations, engages in securities or futures trading activities related to such information, or expressly or impliedly suggests that another person engage in relevant trading activities, if the circumstances are serious, they shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the first paragraph.

<a id="art-181"></a>第一百八十一条　编造并且传播影响证券、期货交易的虚假信息，扰乱证券、期货交易市场，造成严重后果的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处一万元以上十万元以下罚金。  
证券交易所、期货交易所、证券公司、期货经纪公司的从业人员，证券业协会、期货业协会或者证券期货监督管理部门的工作人员，故意提供虚假信息或者伪造、变造、销毁交易记录，诱骗投资者买卖证券、期货合约，造成严重后果的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处一万元以上十万元以下罚金；情节特别恶劣的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金。  
单位犯前两款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案.md#art-5" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案》第5条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 181** Any person who fabricates and disseminates false information affecting securities or futures trading, disrupts the securities or futures trading market, and causes serious consequences, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan.  
Any employee of a stock exchange, futures exchange, securities company, or futures brokerage company, or any staff member of a securities industry association, futures industry association, or securities and futures regulatory authority, who intentionally provides false information or forges, alters, or destroys trading records to induce investors to buy or sell securities or futures contracts, and causes serious consequences, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan; if the circumstances are especially flagrant, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan.  
Where a unit commits any of the crimes mentioned in the preceding two paragraphs, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-182"></a>第一百八十二条　有下列情形之一，操纵证券、期货市场，影响证券、期货交易价格或者证券、期货交易量，情节严重的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；情节特别严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金：  
（一）单独或者合谋，集中资金优势、持股或者持仓优势或者利用信息优势联合或者连续买卖的；  
（二）与他人串通，以事先约定的时间、价格和方式相互进行证券、期货交易的；  
（三）在自己实际控制的帐户之间进行证券交易，或者以自己为交易对象，自买自卖期货合约的；  
（四）不以成交为目的，频繁或者大量申报买入、卖出证券、期货合约并撤销申报的；  
（五）利用虚假或者不确定的重大信息，诱导投资者进行证券、期货交易的；  
（六）对证券、证券发行人、期货交易标的公开作出评价、预测或者投资建议，同时进行反向证券交易或者相关期货交易的；  
（七）以其他方法操纵证券、期货市场的。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案.md#art-6" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-11" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第11条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-13" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第13条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 182** Whoever, under any of the following circumstances, manipulates the securities or futures market to affect the price or volume of securities or futures trading, and the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined:  
(1) acting alone or in collusion, concentrating capital, shareholding, or position advantages, or utilizing information advantages, to jointly or continuously trade in securities or futures;  
(2) colluding with another person to engage in mutual securities or futures transactions at a prearranged time, price, and manner;  
(3) conducting securities transactions between accounts actually controlled by oneself, or selling and buying futures contracts with oneself as the counterparty;  
(4) frequently or substantially entering purchase or sale orders for securities or futures contracts without the intention of completing the transaction, and subsequently canceling such orders;  
(5) inducing investors to trade in securities or futures by using false or uncertain material information;  
(6) making public evaluations, forecasts, or investment recommendations regarding securities, securities issuers, or futures trading targets, while simultaneously engaging in reverse securities trading or related futures trading; or  
(7) manipulating the securities or futures market by other means.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-183"></a>第一百八十三条　保险公司的工作人员利用职务上的便利，故意编造未曾发生的保险事故进行虚假理赔，骗取保险金归自己所有的，依照[本法第二百七十一条](#art-271)的规定定罪处罚。  
国有保险公司工作人员和国有保险公司委派到非国有保险公司从事公务的人员有前款行为的，依照[本法第三百八十二条](#art-382)、第三百八十三条的规定定罪处罚。

**Article 183** Where any staff member of an insurance company, taking advantage of his position, intentionally fabricates an insurance accident that has never occurred to make a false claim and defrauds the insurance money for his own possession, he shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 271 of this Law.  
Where any staff member of a State-owned insurance company or any person assigned by a State-owned insurance company to a non-State-owned insurance company to engage in public service commits the act mentioned in the preceding paragraph, he shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Articles 382 and 383 of this Law.

<a id="art-184"></a>第一百八十四条　银行或者其他金融机构的工作人员在金融业务活动中索取他人财物或者非法收受他人财物，为他人谋取利益的，或者违反国家规定，收受各种名义的回扣、手续费，归个人所有的，依照[本法第一百六十三条](#art-163)的规定定罪处罚。  
国有金融机构工作人员和国有金融机构委派到非国有金融机构从事公务的人员有前款行为的，依照[本法第三百八十五条](#art-385)、第三百八十六条的规定定罪处罚。

**Article 184** Where any staff member of a bank or any other financial institution extorts or illegally accepts property from another person in the course of financial business activities to seek benefits for such person, or, in violation of State regulations, accepts rebates or handling fees of any name for his own possession, he shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 163 of this Law.  
Where any staff member of a State-owned financial institution or any person assigned by a State-owned financial institution to a non-State-owned financial institution to engage in public service commits the act mentioned in the preceding paragraph, he shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Articles 385 and 386 of this Law.

<a id="art-185"></a>第一百八十五条　商业银行、证券交易所、期货交易所、证券公司、期货经纪公司、保险公司或者其他金融机构的工作人员利用职务上的便利，挪用本单位或者客户资金的，依照[本法第二百七十二条](#art-272)的规定定罪处罚。  
国有商业银行、证券交易所、期货交易所、证券公司、期货经纪公司、保险公司或者其他国有金融机构的工作人员和国有商业银行、证券交易所、期货交易所、证券公司、期货经纪公司、保险公司或者其他国有金融机构委派到前款规定中的非国有机构从事公务的人员有前款行为的，依照[本法第三百八十四条](#art-384)的规定定罪处罚。  
第一百八十五条之一　商业银行、证券交易所、期货交易所、证券公司、期货经纪公司、保险公司或者其他金融机构，违背受托义务，擅自运用客户资金或者其他委托、信托的财产，情节严重的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处三万元以上三十万元以下罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金。  
社会保障基金管理机构、住房公积金管理机构等公众资金管理机构，以及保险公司、保险资产管理公司、证券投资基金管理公司，违反国家规定运用资金的，对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-12" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第12条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 185** Where any staff member of a commercial bank, stock exchange, futures exchange, securities company, futures brokerage company, insurance company, or any other financial institution, taking advantage of his position, misappropriates funds of the unit or clients, he shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 272 of this Law.  
Where any staff member of a State-owned commercial bank, stock exchange, futures exchange, securities company, futures brokerage company, insurance company, or any other State-owned financial institution, or any person assigned by such a State-owned financial institution to a non-State-owned institution as specified in the preceding paragraph to engage in public service, commits the act mentioned in the preceding paragraph, he shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 384 of this Law.  
Article 185a Where any commercial bank, stock exchange, futures exchange, securities company, futures brokerage company, insurance company, or any other financial institution, in breach of its fiduciary duties, unauthorizedly uses client funds or other entrusted or trust property, and the circumstances are serious, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the offense shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 30,000 yuan but not more than 300,000 yuan; if the circumstances are especially serious, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan.  
Where any public fund management institution such as a social security fund management institution or a housing provident fund management institution, or an insurance company, an insurance asset management company, or a securities investment fund management company, uses funds in violation of State regulations, the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the offense shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-186"></a>第一百八十六条　银行或者其他金融机构的工作人员违反国家规定发放贷款，数额巨大或者造成重大损失的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处一万元以上十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大或者造成特别重大损失的，处五年以上有期徒刑，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金。  
银行或者其他金融机构的工作人员违反国家规定，向关系人发放贷款的，依照前款的规定从重处罚。  
单位犯前两款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前两款的规定处罚。  
关系人的范围，依照《中华人民共和国商业银行法》和有关金融法规确定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-13" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第13条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 186** Where any staff member of a bank or any other financial institution, in violation of State regulations, grants loans in an especially large amount or causes especially heavy losses, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan; if the amount is especially huge or especially heavy losses are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan.  
Where any staff member of a bank or any other financial institution, in violation of State regulations, grants loans to related persons, he shall be given a heavier punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Where a unit commits any of the crimes mentioned in the preceding two paragraphs, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding two paragraphs.  
The scope of related persons shall be determined in accordance with the Law of the People's Republic of China on Commercial Banks and relevant financial regulations.

<a id="art-187"></a>第一百八十七条　银行或者其他金融机构的工作人员吸收客户资金不入帐，数额巨大或者造成重大损失的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大或者造成特别重大损失的，处五年以上有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-14" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第14条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 187** Where any staff member of a bank or any other financial institution absorbs client funds without entering them into the accounts, if the amount is huge or heavy losses are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the amount is especially huge or especially heavy losses are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-188"></a>第一百八十八条　银行或者其他金融机构的工作人员违反规定，为他人出具信用证或者其他保函、票据、存单、资信证明，情节严重的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-15" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第15条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 188** Where any staff member of a bank or any other financial institution, in violation of regulations, issues letters of credit, other letters of guarantee, negotiable instruments, certificates of deposit, or creditworthiness certificates for another person, and the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; if the circumstances are especially serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-189"></a>第一百八十九条　银行或者其他金融机构的工作人员在票据业务中，对违反票据法规定的票据予以承兑、付款或者保证，造成重大损失的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成特别重大损失的，处五年以上有期徒刑。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。

**Article 189** Where any staff member of a bank or any other financial institution, in the course of negotiable instrument business, accepts, pays, or guarantees a negotiable instrument that violates the provisions of the Negotiable Instruments Law, and causes heavy losses, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; if especially heavy losses are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-190"></a>第一百九十条　公司、企业或者其他单位，违反国家规定，擅自将外汇存放境外，或者将境内的外汇非法转移到境外，数额较大的，对单位判处逃汇数额百分之五以上百分之三十以下罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，对单位判处逃汇数额百分之五以上百分之三十以下罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以上有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../经济、税务与金融/金融、证券与保险/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定.md#art-3" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 190** Where any company, enterprise, or other unit, in violation of State regulations, unauthorizedly deposits foreign exchange outside the territory of China or illegally transfers foreign exchange within the territory of China outside the territory, and the amount is relatively large, a fine of not less than 5% but not more than 30% of the amount of evaded foreign exchange shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the offense shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, a fine of not less than 5% but not more than 30% of the amount of evaded foreign exchange shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the offense shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-191"></a>第一百九十一条　为掩饰、隐瞒毒品犯罪、黑社会性质的组织犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪、走私犯罪、贪污贿赂犯罪、破坏金融管理秩序犯罪、金融诈骗犯罪的所得及其产生的收益的来源和性质，有下列行为之一的，没收实施以上犯罪的所得及其产生的收益，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金：  
（一）提供资金帐户的；  
（二）将财产转换为现金、金融票据、有价证券的；  
（三）通过转帐或者其他支付结算方式转移资金的；  
（四）跨境转移资产的；  
（五）以其他方法掩饰、隐瞒犯罪所得及其收益的来源和性质的。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（三）.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（三）》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-16" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第16条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-14" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第14条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 191** Whoever, for the purpose of concealing or disguising the source and nature of the proceeds and the gains derived from any drug-related crime, organized crime of a mafia nature, terrorist activities crime, smuggling crime, embezzlement or bribery crime, crime of disrupting the order of financial management, or financial fraud crime, commits any of the following acts, shall be subject to confiscation of the proceeds and gains derived from the above crimes, and shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined:  
(1) providing capital accounts;  
(2) converting property into cash, financial instruments, or negotiable securities;  
(3) transferring funds through transfer or other payment settlement methods;  
(4) cross-border transfer of assets; or  
(5) concealing or disguising the source and nature of criminal proceeds and their gains by other means.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

#### 第五节　金融诈骗罪

<a id="art-192"></a>第一百九十二条　以非法占有为目的，使用诈骗方法非法集资，数额较大的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处七年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-15" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第15条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 192** Whoever, for the purpose of illegal possession, uses fraudulent methods to illegally raise funds, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than seven years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined or shall have his property confiscated.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-193"></a>第一百九十三条　有下列情形之一，以非法占有为目的，诈骗银行或者其他金融机构的贷款，数额较大的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产：  
（一）编造引进资金、项目等虚假理由的；  
（二）使用虚假的经济合同的；  
（三）使用虚假的证明文件的；  
（四）使用虚假的产权证明作担保或者超出抵押物价值重复担保的；  
（五）以其他方法诈骗贷款的。

**Article 193** Whoever, under any of the following circumstances, for the purpose of illegal possession, defrauds a bank or any other financial institution of a loan, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan or shall have his property confiscated:  
(1) fabricating false reasons such as introducing foreign capital or projects;  
(2) using false economic contracts;  
(3) using false supporting documents;  
(4) using false property right certificates as security or repeatedly providing security exceeding the value of the mortgaged property; or  
(5) defrauding loans by other means.

<a id="art-194"></a>第一百九十四条　有下列情形之一，进行金融票据诈骗活动，数额较大的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产：  
（一）明知是伪造、变造的汇票、本票、支票而使用的；  
（二）明知是作废的汇票、本票、支票而使用的；  
（三）冒用他人的汇票、本票、支票的；  
（四）签发空头支票或者与其预留印鉴不符的支票，骗取财物的；  
（五）汇票、本票的出票人签发无资金保证的汇票、本票或者在出票时作虚假记载，骗取财物的。  
使用伪造、变造的委托收款凭证、汇款凭证、银行存单等其他银行结算凭证的，依照前款的规定处罚。

**Article 194** Whoever, under any of the following circumstances, engages in financial instrument fraud activities, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan or shall have his property confiscated:  
(1) using forged or altered bills of exchange, promissory notes, or checks knowing them to be forged or altered;  
(2) using invalidated bills of exchange, promissory notes, or checks knowing them to be invalid;  
(3) fraudulently using another person's bills of exchange, promissory notes, or checks;  
(4) issuing a rubber check or a check inconsistent with the reserved seal impression to defraud property; or  
(5) the drawer of a bill of exchange or promissory note issues a bill of exchange or promissory note without a guaranteed source of funds or makes false entries at the time of issue to defraud property.  
Whoever uses forged or altered other bank settlement vouchers, such as entrusted collection vouchers, remittance vouchers, or bank deposit certificates, shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-195"></a>第一百九十五条　有下列情形之一，进行信用证诈骗活动的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产：  
（一）使用伪造、变造的信用证或者附随的单据、文件的；  
（二）使用作废的信用证的；  
（三）骗取信用证的；  
（四）以其他方法进行信用证诈骗活动的。

**Article 195** Whoever engages in any of the following activities of defrauding through letters of credit shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan or shall have his property confiscated:  
(1) using forged or altered letters of credit or attached documents or instruments;  
(2) using invalidated letters of credit;  
(3) fraudulently obtaining letters of credit; or  
(4) engaging in defrauding through letters of credit by other means.

<a id="art-196"></a>第一百九十六条　有下列情形之一，进行信用卡诈骗活动，数额较大的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产：  
（一）使用伪造的信用卡，或者使用以虚假的身份证明骗领的信用卡的；  
（二）使用作废的信用卡的；  
（三）冒用他人信用卡的；  
（四）恶意透支的。  
前款所称恶意透支，是指持卡人以非法占有为目的，超过规定限额或者规定期限透支，并且经发卡银行催收后仍不归还的行为。  
盗窃信用卡并使用的，依照[本法第二百六十四条](#art-264)的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民检察院关于拾得他人信用卡并在自动柜员机（ATM机）上使用的行为如何定性问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于拾得他人信用卡并在自动柜员机（ATM机）上使用的行为如何定性问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（五）.md#art-2" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（五）》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 196** Whoever, under any of the following circumstances, engages in credit card fraud activities, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan or shall have his property confiscated:  
(1) using a forged credit card, or using a credit card fraudulently obtained with false identification;  
(2) using an invalidated credit card;  
(3) fraudulently using another person's credit card; or  
(4) maliciously overdrafting.  
For the purpose of the preceding paragraph, "malicious overdrafting" means the act of a cardholder who, for the purpose of illegal possession, overdrafts beyond the prescribed limit or prescribed period, and still fails to repay after being urged by the issuing bank.  
Whoever steals a credit card and uses it shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 264 of this Law.

<a id="art-197"></a>第一百九十七条　使用伪造、变造的国库券或者国家发行的其他有价证券，进行诈骗活动，数额较大的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产。

**Article 197** Whoever uses forged or altered treasury bonds or other securities issued by the State to engage in fraudulent activities, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan or shall have his property confiscated.

<a id="art-198"></a>第一百九十八条　有下列情形之一，进行保险诈骗活动，数额较大的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处一万元以上十万元以下罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金或者没收财产：  
（一）投保人故意虚构保险标的，骗取保险金的；  
（二）投保人、被保险人或者受益人对发生的保险事故编造虚假的原因或者夸大损失的程度，骗取保险金的；  
（三）投保人、被保险人或者受益人编造未曾发生的保险事故，骗取保险金的；  
（四）投保人、被保险人故意造成财产损失的保险事故，骗取保险金的；  
（五）投保人、受益人故意造成被保险人死亡、伤残或者疾病，骗取保险金的。  
有前款第四项、第五项所列行为，同时构成其他犯罪的，依照数罪并罚的规定处罚。  
单位犯第一款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑。  
保险事故的鉴定人、证明人、财产评估人故意提供虚假的证明文件，为他人诈骗提供条件的，以保险诈骗的共犯论处。

**Article 198** Whoever, under any of the following circumstances, engages in insurance fraud activities, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan or shall have his property confiscated:  
(1) the policyholder intentionally fabricates the subject matter of the insurance to defraud insurance money;  
(2) the policyholder, the insured, or the beneficiary fabricates a false cause or exaggerates the extent of loss with respect to an insurance accident that has occurred to defraud insurance money;  
(3) the policyholder, the insured, or the beneficiary fabricates an insurance accident that has never occurred to defraud insurance money;  
(4) the policyholder or the insured intentionally causes an insurance accident involving property damage to defraud insurance money; or  
(5) the policyholder or the beneficiary intentionally causes the death, disability, or illness of the insured to defraud insurance money.  
Where any of the acts listed in subparagraphs (4) and (5) of the preceding paragraph also constitutes another crime, it shall be punished in accordance with the provisions on combined punishment for multiple crimes.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the first paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years.  
Where an expert evaluator, a witness, or a property assessor of an insurance accident intentionally provides false supporting documents to facilitate fraud by another person, he shall be deemed an accomplice to insurance fraud.

<a id="art-199"></a>第一百九十九条　（删去）&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-12" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第12条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-30" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第30条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 199** (Deleted)

<a id="art-200"></a>第二百条　单位犯本节第一百九十四条、第一百九十五条规定之罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，可以并处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-31" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第31条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-16" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第16条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 200** Where a unit commits the crimes as provided for in Articles 194 and 195 of this Section, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons who are directly in charge and the other persons who are directly responsible for the crime shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and may also be fined; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined.

#### 第六节　危害税收征管罪

<a id="art-201"></a>第二百零一条　纳税人采取欺骗、隐瞒手段进行虚假纳税申报或者不申报，逃避缴纳税款数额较大并且占应纳税额百分之十以上的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；数额巨大并且占应纳税额百分之三十以上的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
扣缴义务人采取前款所列手段，不缴或者少缴已扣、已收税款，数额较大的，依照前款的规定处罚。  
对多次实施前两款行为，未经处理的，按照累计数额计算。  
有第一款行为，经税务机关依法下达追缴通知后，补缴应纳税款，缴纳滞纳金，已受行政处罚的，不予追究刑事责任；但是，五年内因逃避缴纳税款受过刑事处罚或者被税务机关给予二次以上行政处罚的除外。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第4条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-3" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第3条引用">[5]</a></sup>

**Article 201** : Where a taxpayer uses fraudulent or concealment means to file false tax returns or fails to file tax returns, and the amount of tax evaded is relatively large and accounts for more than ten percent of the tax payable, the taxpayer shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined; where the amount is huge and accounts for more than thirty percent of the tax payable, the taxpayer shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined.  
  
Where a withholding agent fails to pay or underpays the tax that has been withheld or collected, using the means listed in the preceding paragraph, and the amount is relatively large, the withholding agent shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
  
Where the acts prescribed in the preceding two paragraphs are committed multiple times and have not been dealt with, the amounts shall be calculated cumulatively.  
  
Where a person commits the act prescribed in the first paragraph but, after receiving a demand for payment of tax issued by the tax authority according to law, pays the tax payable and any late payment surcharge, and has been subjected to an administrative penalty, the person shall not be investigated for criminal liability; however, this does not apply if the person has previously been subjected to criminal punishment for tax evasion within the past five years or has been subjected to two or more administrative penalties by the tax authority within the past five years.

<a id="art-202"></a>第二百零二条　以暴力、威胁方法拒不缴纳税款的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处拒缴税款一倍以上五倍以下罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处拒缴税款一倍以上五倍以下罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第5条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 202** : Where a person refuses to pay tax by means of violence or threat, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of tax refused to be paid; where the circumstances are serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of tax refused to be paid.

<a id="art-203"></a>第二百零三条　纳税人欠缴应纳税款，采取转移或者隐匿财产的手段，致使税务机关无法追缴欠缴的税款，数额在一万元以上不满十万元的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处欠缴税款一倍以上五倍以下罚金；数额在十万元以上的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处欠缴税款一倍以上五倍以下罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 203** : Where a taxpayer who owes tax payable transfers or conceals property, thereby making it impossible for the tax authority to recover the tax owed, if the amount is not less than 10,000 yuan but less than 100,000 yuan, the taxpayer shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of tax owed; if the amount is not less than 100,000 yuan, the taxpayer shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of tax owed.

<a id="art-204"></a>第二百零四条　以假报出口或者其他欺骗手段，骗取国家出口退税款，数额较大的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处骗取税款一倍以上五倍以下罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处骗取税款一倍以上五倍以下罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处骗取税款一倍以上五倍以下罚金或者没收财产。  
纳税人缴纳税款后，采取前款规定的欺骗方法，骗取所缴纳的税款的，依照[本法第二百零一条](#art-201)的规定定罪处罚；骗取税款超过所缴纳的税款部分，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第8条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 204** : Where a person obtains State export tax refunds by falsely declaring exports or by other fraudulent means, if the amount is relatively large, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of tax fraudulently obtained; if the amount is huge or if there are other serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of tax fraudulently obtained; if the amount is especially huge or if there are other especially serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the amount of tax fraudulently obtained or shall have property confiscated.  
  
Where a taxpayer, after paying tax, uses the fraudulent means prescribed in the preceding paragraph to fraudulently obtain the paid tax, the taxpayer shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 201 of this Law; where the amount of tax fraudulently obtained exceeds the paid tax, the excess portion shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-205"></a>第二百零五条　虚开增值税专用发票或者虚开用于骗取出口退税、抵扣税款的其他发票的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；虚开的税款数额较大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；虚开的税款数额巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产。  
单位犯本条规定之罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；虚开的税款数额较大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；虚开的税款数额巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑。  
虚开增值税专用发票或者虚开用于骗取出口退税、抵扣税款的其他发票，是指有为他人虚开、为自己虚开、让他人为自己虚开、介绍他人虚开行为之一的。  
第二百零五条之一　虚开[本法第二百零五条](#art-205)规定以外的其他发票，情节严重的，处二年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节特别严重的，处二年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第10条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-11" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第11条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-12" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第12条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-13" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第13条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-32" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第32条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-33" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第33条引用">[6]</a></sup>

**Article 205** : Where a person falsely issues special VAT invoices or falsely issues other invoices for fraudulently obtaining export tax refunds or deducting taxes, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; where the amount of tax falsely invoiced is relatively large or if there are other serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; where the amount of tax falsely invoiced is huge or if there are other especially serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan or shall have property confiscated.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime prescribed in this Article, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and the other persons directly liable shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where the amount of tax falsely invoiced is relatively large or if there are other serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; where the amount of tax falsely invoiced is huge or if there are other especially serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment.  
  
Falsely issuing special VAT invoices or falsely issuing other invoices for fraudulently obtaining export tax refunds or deducting taxes means any of the following acts: issuing false invoices for others, issuing false invoices for oneself, asking others to issue false invoices for oneself, or introducing others to issue false invoices.  
  
Article 205a: Where a person falsely issues invoices other than those specified in Article 205 of this Law, if the circumstances are serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than two years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime prescribed in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and the other persons directly liable shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-206"></a>第二百零六条　伪造或者出售伪造的增值税专用发票的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数量较大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；数量巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产。  
单位犯本条规定之罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制；数量较大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；数量巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-14" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第14条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-34" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第34条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 206** : Where a person forges or sells forged special VAT invoices, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; where the quantity is relatively large or if there are other serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; where the quantity is huge or if there are other especially serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan or shall have property confiscated.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime prescribed in this Article, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and the other persons directly liable shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance; where the quantity is relatively large or if there are other serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; where the quantity is huge or if there are other especially serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment.

<a id="art-207"></a>第二百零七条　非法出售增值税专用发票的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数量较大的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；数量巨大的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产。

**Article 207** : Where a person illegally sells special VAT invoices, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; where the quantity is relatively large, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; where the quantity is huge, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan or shall have property confiscated.

<a id="art-208"></a>第二百零八条　非法购买增值税专用发票或者购买伪造的增值税专用发票的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金。  
非法购买增值税专用发票或者购买伪造的增值税专用发票又虚开或者出售的，分别依照[本法第二百零五条](#art-205)、第二百零六条、第二百零七条的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-16" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第16条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 208** : Where a person illegally purchases special VAT invoices or purchases forged special VAT invoices, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan.  
  
Where a person who illegally purchases special VAT invoices or purchases forged special VAT invoices also falsely issues or sells them, the person shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Articles 205, 206, and 207 of this Law, respectively.

<a id="art-209"></a>第二百零九条　伪造、擅自制造或者出售伪造、擅自制造的可以用于骗取出口退税、抵扣税款的其他发票的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处二万元以上二十万元以下罚金；数量巨大的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金；数量特别巨大的，处七年以上有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金或者没收财产。  
伪造、擅自制造或者出售伪造、擅自制造的前款规定以外的其他发票的，处二年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处一万元以上五万元以下罚金；情节严重的，处二年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处五万元以上五十万元以下罚金。  
非法出售可以用于骗取出口退税、抵扣税款的其他发票的，依照第一款的规定处罚。  
非法出售第三款规定以外的其他发票的，依照第二款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第17条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 209** : Where a person forges or without authorization manufactures, or sells forged or without authorization manufactured, other invoices that may be used for fraudulently obtaining export tax refunds or deducting taxes, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and shall also be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; where the quantity is huge, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; where the quantity is especially huge, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than seven years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan or shall have property confiscated.  
  
Where a person forges or without authorization manufactures, or sells forged or without authorization manufactured, other invoices other than those prescribed in the preceding paragraph, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and shall also, or shall only, be fined not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan; where the circumstances are serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than two years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan.  
  
Where a person illegally sells other invoices that may be used for fraudulently obtaining export tax refunds or deducting taxes, the person shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the first paragraph.  
  
Where a person illegally sells other invoices other than those prescribed in the third paragraph, the person shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the second paragraph.

<a id="art-210"></a>第二百一十条　盗窃增值税专用发票或者可以用于骗取出口退税、抵扣税款的其他发票的，依照[本法第二百六十四条](#art-264)的规定定罪处罚。  
使用欺骗手段骗取增值税专用发票或者可以用于骗取出口退税、抵扣税款的其他发票的，依照[本法第二百六十六条](#art-266)的规定定罪处罚。  
第二百一十条之一　明知是伪造的发票而持有，数量较大的，处二年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；数量巨大的，处二年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-18" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第18条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-35" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第35条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 210** : Where a person steals special VAT invoices or other invoices that may be used for fraudulently obtaining export tax refunds or deducting taxes, the person shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 264 of this Law.  
  
Where a person uses fraudulent means to obtain special VAT invoices or other invoices that may be used for fraudulently obtaining export tax refunds or deducting taxes, the person shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 266 of this Law.  
  
Article 210a: Where a person, knowing that invoices are forged, holds such invoices, if the quantity is relatively large, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and shall also be fined; if the quantity is huge, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than two years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined.  
  
Where a unit commits the crime prescribed in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and the other persons directly liable shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-211"></a>第二百一十一条　单位犯本节第二百零一条、第二百零三条、第二百零四条、第二百零七条、第二百零八条、第二百零九条规定之罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照各该条的规定处罚。

**Article 211** : Where a unit commits the crimes prescribed in Articles 201, 203, 204, 207, 208, and 209 of this Section, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and the other persons directly liable shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the respective Articles.

<a id="art-212"></a>第二百一十二条　犯本节第二百零一条至第二百零五条规定之罪，被判处罚金、没收财产的，在执行前，应当先由税务机关追缴税款和所骗取的出口退税款。

**Article 212** : Where a person commits the crimes prescribed in Articles 201 through 205 of this Section and is sentenced to a fine or confiscation of property, the tax authority shall, before the enforcement, first recover the tax and the fraudulently obtained export tax refunds.

#### 第七节　侵犯知识产权罪

<a id="art-213"></a>第二百一十三条　未经注册商标所有人许可，在同一种商品、服务上使用与其注册商标相同的商标，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑，并处或者单处罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三).md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三)》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第8条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-13" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第13条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-15" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第15条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-17" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第17条引用">[6]</a></sup>

**Article 213** : Where a person, without permission of the owner of a registered trademark, uses a trademark identical to the registered trademark on the same kind of goods or services, if the circumstances are serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-214"></a>第二百一十四条　销售明知是假冒注册商标的商品，违法所得数额较大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以下有期徒刑，并处或者单处罚金；违法所得数额巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-18" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第18条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 214** : Where a person sells goods that the person knows bear a counterfeit registered trademark, if the amount of illegal gains is relatively large or if there are other serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the amount of illegal gains is huge or if there are other especially serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-215"></a>第二百一十五条　伪造、擅自制造他人注册商标标识或者销售伪造、擅自制造的注册商标标识，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑，并处或者单处罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-19" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第19条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 215** : Where a person forges or without authorization manufactures another person's registered trademark representations, or sells forged or without authorization manufactured registered trademark representations, if the circumstances are serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-216"></a>第二百一十六条　假冒他人专利，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第10条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 216** : Where a person passes off another person's patent, if the circumstances are serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined.

<a id="art-217"></a>第二百一十七条　以营利为目的，有下列侵犯著作权或者与著作权有关的权利的情形之一，违法所得数额较大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以下有期徒刑，并处或者单处罚金；违法所得数额巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金：  
（一）未经著作权人许可，复制发行、通过信息网络向公众传播其文字作品、音乐、美术、视听作品、计算机软件及法律、行政法规规定的其他作品的；  
（二）出版他人享有专有出版权的图书的；  
（三）未经录音录像制作者许可，复制发行、通过信息网络向公众传播其制作的录音录像的；  
（四）未经表演者许可，复制发行录有其表演的录音录像制品，或者通过信息网络向公众传播其表演的；  
（五）制作、出售假冒他人署名的美术作品的；  
（六）未经著作权人或者与著作权有关的权利人许可，故意避开或者破坏权利人为其作品、录音录像制品等采取的保护著作权或者与著作权有关的权利的技术措施的。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三).md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三)》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第5条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-11" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第11条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-14" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第14条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-20" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第20条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-21" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第21条引用">[6]</a></sup>

**Article 217** : Where a person, for purposes of profit, commits any of the following acts infringing copyright or rights related to copyright, if the amount of illegal gains is relatively large or if there are other serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the amount of illegal gains is huge or if there are other especially serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined:  
  
(1) without permission of the copyright owner, reproducing, distributing, or communicating to the public through information networks the owner's written works, music, works of fine art, audiovisual works, computer software, or other works as prescribed by laws and administrative regulations;  
  
(2) publishing a book of which another person enjoys the exclusive right of publication;  
  
(3) without permission of the producer of sound recordings or video recordings, reproducing, distributing, or communicating to the public through information networks the producer's sound recordings or video recordings;  
  
(4) without permission of the performer, reproducing or distributing sound recordings or video recordings containing the performer's performance, or communicating the performer's performance to the public through information networks;  
  
(5) producing or selling works of fine art bearing a forged signature of another person; or  
  
(6) without permission of the copyright owner or owner of rights related to copyright, intentionally circumventing or destroying the technological measures adopted by the right owner to protect copyright or rights related to copyright in respect of the owner's works, sound recordings, video recordings, or the like.

<a id="art-218"></a>第二百一十八条　以营利为目的，销售明知是[本法第二百一十七条](#art-217)规定的侵权复制品，违法所得数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以下有期徒刑，并处或者单处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-21" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第21条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 218** : Where a person, for purposes of profit, sells infringing reproductions that the person knows are those prescribed in Article 217 of this Law, if the amount of illegal gains is huge or if there are other serious circumstances, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, and shall also, or shall only, be fined.

<a id="art-219"></a>第二百一十九条　有下列侵犯商业秘密行为之一，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑，并处或者单处罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金：  
（一）以盗窃、贿赂、欺诈、胁迫、电子侵入或者其他不正当手段获取权利人的商业秘密的；  
（二）披露、使用或者允许他人使用以前项手段获取的权利人的商业秘密的；  
（三）违反保密义务或者违反权利人有关保守商业秘密的要求，披露、使用或者允许他人使用其所掌握的商业秘密的。  
明知前款所列行为，获取、披露、使用或者允许他人使用该商业秘密的，以侵犯商业秘密论。  
本条所称权利人，是指商业秘密的所有人和经商业秘密所有人许可的商业秘密使用人。  
第二百一十九条之一　为境外的机构、组织、人员窃取、刺探、收买、非法提供商业秘密的，处五年以下有期徒刑，并处或者单处罚金；情节严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三).md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三)》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三).md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三)》第4条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三).md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三)》第5条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三).md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释(三)》第6条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理侵犯知识产权刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-22" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第22条引用">[6]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-23" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第23条引用">[7]</a></sup>

**Article 219** : Any of the following acts infringing trade secrets, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and shall also be fined:  
  
(1) acquiring a right holder's trade secrets by theft, bribery, fraud, coercion, electronic intrusion, or other illegitimate means;
