(2) disclosing, using, or allowing another person to use the right holder's trade secrets acquired by the means described in the preceding subparagraph; or  
  
(3) violating a confidentiality obligation or violating the right holder's requirements for keeping trade secrets, by disclosing, using, or allowing another person to use the trade secrets in the person's possession.  
  
Where a person, knowing the acts listed in the preceding paragraph, acquires, discloses, uses, or allows another person to use the trade secrets, the person shall be deemed to have infringed trade secrets.  
  
For the purposes of this Article, "right holder" means the owner of trade secrets and the person licensed by the owner of trade secrets to use the trade secrets.  
  
Article 219a: Where a person steals, spies on, buys, or illegally provides trade secrets for an institution, organization, or individual outside the territory, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the circumstances are serious, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years, and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-220"></a>第二百二十条　单位犯本节第二百一十三条至第二百一十九条之一规定之罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照本节各该条的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-24" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第24条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 220** : Where a unit commits the crimes prescribed in Articles 213 through 219a of this Section, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and the other persons directly liable shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the respective Articles of this Section.

#### 第八节　扰乱市场秩序罪

<a id="art-221"></a>第二百二十一条　捏造并散布虚伪事实，损害他人的商业信誉、商品声誉，给他人造成重大损失或者有其他严重情节的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 221** : Whoever, by fabricating and spreading false facts, damages another person's business reputation or commodity reputation, causing significant losses to another person or where there are other serious circumstances, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined.

<a id="art-222"></a>第二百二十二条　广告主、广告经营者、广告发布者违反国家规定，利用广告对商品或者服务作虚假宣传，情节严重的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第8条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 222** : Advertisers, advertising agents, and advertisement publishers who, in violation of state regulations, use advertisements to make false publicity about commodities or services, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined.

<a id="art-223"></a>第二百二十三条　投标人相互串通投标报价，损害招标人或者其他投标人利益，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金。  
投标人与招标人串通投标，损害国家、集体、公民的合法利益的，依照前款的规定处罚。

**Article 223** : Bidders who collude with each other in tendering prices, damaging the interests of the tenderer or other bidders, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined.  
Where a bidder colludes with the tenderer in bidding, harming the lawful interests of the state, the collective, or citizens, the punishment shall be imposed in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-224"></a>第二百二十四条　有下列情形之一，以非法占有为目的，在签订、履行合同过程中，骗取对方当事人财物，数额较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产：  
（一）以虚构的单位或者冒用他人名义签订合同的；  
（二）以伪造、变造、作废的票据或者其他虚假的产权证明作担保的；  
（三）没有实际履行能力，以先履行小额合同或者部分履行合同的方法，诱骗对方当事人继续签订和履行合同的；  
（四）收受对方当事人给付的货物、货款、预付款或者担保财产后逃匿的；  
（五）以其他方法骗取对方当事人财物的。  
第二百二十四条之一　组织、领导以推销商品、提供服务等经营活动为名，要求参加者以缴纳费用或者购买商品、服务等方式获得加入资格，并按照一定顺序组成层级，直接或者间接以发展人员的数量作为计酬或者返利依据，引诱、胁迫参加者继续发展他人参加，骗取财物，扰乱经济社会秩序的传销活动的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；情节严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-4" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 224** : Whoever, under any of the following circumstances, with the intent of illegal possession, obtains property from the other party in the course of concluding or performing a contract, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined or have property confiscated:  
(1) concluding a contract in the name of a fictitious unit or by impersonating another person;  
(2) providing a guarantee by using forged, altered, or invalidated negotiable instruments or other false documents of title;  
(3) without actual performance capability, by means of first performing a small part of the contract or part of the contract, inducing the other party to continue to conclude and perform the contract;  
(4) after receiving the goods, payment, advance payment, or guaranteed property delivered by the other party, absconding; or  
(5) obtaining the property of the other party by other means.  
Article 224a: Whoever organizes or leads pyramid selling activities which, under the guise of promoting commodities, providing services, or other business operations, require participants to obtain membership by paying fees or purchasing commodities or services, and form hierarchies in a certain order, directly or indirectly using the number of recruited persons as the basis for remuneration or rebate, inducing or coercing participants to continue recruiting others to join, obtaining property, and disrupting the economic and social order, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention and shall also be fined; where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-225"></a>第二百二十五条　违反国家规定，有下列非法经营行为之一，扰乱市场秩序，情节严重的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处违法所得一倍以上五倍以下罚金；情节特别严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑，并处违法所得一倍以上五倍以下罚金或者没收财产：  
（一）未经许可经营法律、行政法规规定的专营、专卖物品或者其他限制买卖的物品的；  
（二）买卖进出口许可证、进出口原产地证明以及其他法律、行政法规规定的经营许可证或者批准文件的；  
（三）未经国家有关主管部门批准非法经营证券、期货、保险业务的，或者非法从事资金支付结算业务的；  
（四）其他严重扰乱市场秩序的非法经营行为。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民检察院关于非法经营国际或港澳台地区电信业务行为法律适用问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于非法经营国际或港澳台地区电信业务行为法律适用问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于办理非法放贷刑事案件若干问题的意见.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于办理非法放贷刑事案件若干问题的意见》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理赌博刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理赌博刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理非法集资刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案.md#art-8" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案》第8条引用">[6]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-5" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第5条引用">[7]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../经济、税务与金融/金融、证券与保险/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定.md#art-4" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定》第4条引用">[8]</a></sup>

**Article 225** : Whoever, in violation of state regulations, commits any of the following illegal business operations, disrupting market order, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the illegal gains; where the circumstances are especially serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the illegal gains or have property confiscated:  
(1) engaging in the business of goods that are exclusively for business or sale by designated entities as provided by laws or administrative regulations, or other goods whose trading is restricted, without permission;  
(2) buying or selling import or export licenses, certificates of origin for import or export, or other business licenses or approval documents provided by laws or administrative regulations;  
(3) illegally engaging in securities, futures, or insurance businesses, or illegally engaging in fund payment settlement business, without the approval of the relevant competent authority of the state; or  
(4) other illegal business operations that seriously disrupt market order.

<a id="art-226"></a>第二百二十六条　以暴力、威胁手段，实施下列行为之一，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金：  
（一）强买强卖商品的；  
（二）强迫他人提供或者接受服务的；  
（三）强迫他人参与或者退出投标、拍卖的；  
（四）强迫他人转让或者收购公司、企业的股份、债券或者其他资产的；  
（五）强迫他人参与或者退出特定的经营活动的。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民检察院关于强迫借贷行为适用法律问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于强迫借贷行为适用法律问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-36" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第36条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 226** : Whoever, by means of violence or threats, commits any of the following acts, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined; where the circumstances are especially serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined:  
(1) forcing others to buy or sell commodities;  
(2) forcing others to provide or accept services;  
(3) forcing others to participate in or withdraw from a bid or auction;  
(4) forcing others to transfer or acquire shares, bonds, or other assets of a company or enterprise; or  
(5) forcing others to participate in or withdraw from specific business activities.

<a id="art-227"></a>第二百二十七条　伪造或者倒卖伪造的车票、船票、邮票或者其他有价票证，数额较大的，处二年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处票证价额一倍以上五倍以下罚金；数额巨大的，处二年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处票证价额一倍以上五倍以下罚金。  
倒卖车票、船票，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处票证价额一倍以上五倍以下罚金。

**Article 227** : Whoever forges or scalps forged tickets for vehicles, ships, stamps, or other valuable tickets, where the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and shall also, or shall only, be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the face value of the tickets; where the amount is huge, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than two years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the face value of the tickets.  
Whoever scalps tickets for vehicles or ships, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and shall also, or shall only, be fined not less than one time but not more than five times the face value of the tickets.

<a id="art-228"></a>第二百二十八条　以牟利为目的，违反土地管理法规，非法转让、倒卖土地使用权，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处非法转让、倒卖土地使用权价额百分之五以上百分之二十以下罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处非法转让、倒卖土地使用权价额百分之五以上百分之二十以下罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/法律解释/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第二百二十八条、第三百四十二条、第四百一十条的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第二百二十八条、第三百四十二条、第四百一十条的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 228** : Whoever, for the purpose of profit, in violation of land management laws and regulations, illegally transfers or scalps the right to use land, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined not less than 5% but not more than 20% of the price of the illegally transferred or scalped land use right; where the circumstances are especially serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years, and shall also be fined not less than 5% but not more than 20% of the price of the illegally transferred or scalped land use right.

<a id="art-229"></a>第二百二十九条　承担资产评估、验资、验证、会计、审计、法律服务、保荐、安全评价、环境影响评价、环境监测等职责的中介组织的人员故意提供虚假证明文件，情节严重的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；有下列情形之一的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金：  
（一）提供与证券发行相关的虚假的资产评估、会计、审计、法律服务、保荐等证明文件，情节特别严重的；  
（二）提供与重大资产交易相关的虚假的资产评估、会计、审计等证明文件，情节特别严重的；  
（三）在涉及公共安全的重大工程、项目中提供虚假的安全评价、环境影响评价等证明文件，致使公共财产、国家和人民利益遭受特别重大损失的。  
有前款行为，同时索取他人财物或者非法收受他人财物构成犯罪的，依照处罚较重的规定定罪处罚。  
第一款规定的人员，严重不负责任，出具的证明文件有重大失实，造成严重后果的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/文书、送达与废止/最高人民检察院关于公证员出具公证书有重大失实行为如何适用法律问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于公证员出具公证书有重大失实行为如何适用法律问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民检察院关于地质工程勘测院和其他履行勘测职责的单位及其工作人员能否成为刑法第二百二十九条规定的有关犯罪主体的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于地质工程勘测院和其他履行勘测职责的单位及其工作人员能否成为刑法第二百二十九条规定的有关犯罪主体的批复》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-25" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第25条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 229** : Where personnel of intermediary organizations undertaking responsibilities such as assets appraisal, capital verification, certification, accounting, auditing, legal services, sponsorship, safety assessment, environmental impact assessment, and environmental monitoring intentionally provide false certification documents, if the circumstances are serious, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention and shall also be fined; under any of the following circumstances, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined:  
(1) providing false certification documents such as assets appraisal, accounting, auditing, legal services, and sponsorship related to securities issuance, where the circumstances are especially serious;  
(2) providing false certification documents such as assets appraisal, accounting, and auditing related to major asset transactions, where the circumstances are especially serious; or  
(3) providing false certification documents such as safety assessment and environmental impact assessment in major projects involving public safety, causing especially heavy losses to public property or the interests of the state or the people.  
Where, while committing the act mentioned in the preceding paragraph, a person simultaneously extorts property from another person or illegally accepts property from another person, constituting a crime, the conviction and punishment shall be imposed in accordance with the provisions for the heavier punishment.  
Where a person specified in paragraph 1 is seriously irresponsible and issues certification documents that are materially false, causing serious consequences, the person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also, or shall only, be fined.

<a id="art-230"></a>第二百三十条　违反进出口商品检验法的规定，逃避商品检验，将必须经商检机构检验的进口商品未报经检验而擅自销售、使用，或者将必须经商检机构检验的出口商品未报经检验合格而擅自出口，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金。

**Article 230** : Whoever, in violation of the provisions of the Law on Import and Export Commodity Inspection, evades commodity inspection, and sells or uses imported commodities which must be inspected by the commodity inspection authorities without having applied for inspection, or exports exported commodities which must be inspected by the commodity inspection authorities without having obtained inspection approval, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and shall also, or shall only, be fined.

<a id="art-231"></a>第二百三十一条　单位犯本节第二百二十一条至第二百三十条规定之罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照本节各该条的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../经济、税务与金融/金融、证券与保险/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定.md#art-4" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 231** : Where a unit commits the crime as provided in Articles 221 through 230 of this Section, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the directly responsible person(s) in charge and other directly liable personnel shall be punished in accordance with the respective provisions of this Section.

### 第四章　侵犯公民人身权利、民主权利罪

<a id="art-232"></a>第二百三十二条　故意杀人的，处死刑、无期徒刑或者十年以上有期徒刑；情节较轻的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 232** : Whoever intentionally kills another person shall be sentenced to death, life imprisonment, or fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years; where the circumstances are less serious, the offender shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-233"></a>第二百三十三条　过失致人死亡的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑；情节较轻的，处三年以下有期徒刑。本法另有规定的，依照规定。

**Article 233** : Whoever negligently causes another person's death shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years; where the circumstances are less serious, the offender shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years. Where this Law provides otherwise, the matter shall be dealt with in accordance with such provisions.

<a id="art-234"></a>第二百三十四条　故意伤害他人身体的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制。  
犯前款罪，致人重伤的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；致人死亡或者以特别残忍手段致人重伤造成严重残疾的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。本法另有规定的，依照规定。  
第二百三十四条之一　组织他人出卖人体器官的，处五年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；情节严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
未经本人同意摘取其器官，或者摘取不满十八周岁的人的器官，或者强迫、欺骗他人捐献器官的，依照[本法第二百三十四条](#art-234)、第二百三十二条的规定定罪处罚。  
违背本人生前意愿摘取其尸体器官，或者本人生前未表示同意，违反国家规定，违背其近亲属意愿摘取其尸体器官的，依照[本法第三百零二条](#art-302)的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民检察院关于强制隔离戒毒所工作人员能否成为虐待被监管人罪主体问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于强制隔离戒毒所工作人员能否成为虐待被监管人罪主体问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/人身犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于依法惩治性侵害未成年人犯罪的意见.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于依法惩治性侵害未成年人犯罪的意见》第3条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理危害税收征管刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第5条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-37" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第37条引用">[5]</a></sup>

**Article 234** : Whoever intentionally inflicts bodily harm upon another person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance.  
Whoever commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph and causes serious bodily injury to another person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; whoever causes death to another person, or causes serious bodily injury to another person by extremely cruel means resulting in severe disability, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or death. Where this Law provides otherwise, the matter shall be dealt with in accordance with such provisions.  
Article 234a: Whoever organizes others to sell human organs shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years and shall also be fined; where the circumstances are serious, the offender shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years and shall also be fined or have property confiscated.  
Whoever removes organs from a person without the person's consent, or removes organs from a person under the age of 18, or forces or deceives another person into donating organs, shall be convicted and punished in accordance with Articles 234 and 232 of this Law.  
Whoever removes organs from a corpse against the deceased person's will before death, or where the deceased person did not express consent before death, violates state regulations and removes organs from a corpse against the will of the deceased person's close relatives, shall be convicted and punished in accordance with Article 302 of this Law.

<a id="art-235"></a>第二百三十五条　过失伤害他人致人重伤的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。本法另有规定的，依照规定。

**Article 235** : Whoever negligently injures another person, causing serious bodily injury, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention. Where this Law provides otherwise, the matter shall be dealt with in accordance with such provisions.

<a id="art-236"></a>第二百三十六条　以暴力、胁迫或者其他手段强奸妇女的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
奸淫不满十四周岁的幼女的，以强奸论，从重处罚。  
强奸妇女、奸淫幼女，有下列情形之一的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑：  
（一）强奸妇女、奸淫幼女情节恶劣的；  
（二）强奸妇女、奸淫幼女多人的；  
（三）在公共场所当众强奸妇女、奸淫幼女的；  
（四）二人以上轮奸的；  
（五）奸淫不满十周岁的幼女或者造成幼女伤害的；  
（六）致使被害人重伤、死亡或者造成其他严重后果的。  
第二百三十六条之一　对已满十四周岁不满十六周岁的未成年女性负有监护、收养、看护、教育、医疗等特殊职责的人员，与该未成年女性发生性关系的，处三年以下有期徒刑；情节恶劣的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
有前款行为，同时又构成[本法第二百三十六条](#art-236)规定之罪的，依照处罚较重的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/人身犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于依法惩治性侵害未成年人犯罪的意见.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于依法惩治性侵害未成年人犯罪的意见》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/人身犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于依法惩治性侵害未成年人犯罪的意见.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于依法惩治性侵害未成年人犯罪的意见》第3条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-26" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第26条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-27" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第27条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 236** : Whoever rapes a woman by violence, coercion, or other means shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.  
Whoever has sexual intercourse with a girl under the age of 14 shall be deemed to have committed rape and shall be given a severe punishment.  
Whoever rapes a woman or has sexual intercourse with a girl under the age of 14 under any of the following circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or death:  
(1) the circumstances of the rape or sexual intercourse are odious;  
(2) the offender has raped multiple women or has had sexual intercourse with multiple girls under the age of 14;  
(3) the offender rapes a woman or has sexual intercourse with a girl under the age of 14 in a public place in a brazen manner;  
(4) the offender commits gang rape;  
(5) the offender has sexual intercourse with a girl under the age of 10 or causes harm to the girl; or  
(6) the offender causes serious bodily injury, death, or other serious consequences to the victim.  
Article 236a: Where a person who has special duties such as guardianship, adoption, care, education, or medical treatment towards a female minor over the age of 14 but under the age of 16 engages in sexual relations with that female minor, the offender shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years; where the circumstances are odious, the offender shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.  
Where, in addition to the act mentioned in the preceding paragraph, the act also constitutes a crime under Article 236 of this Law, the conviction and punishment shall be imposed in accordance with the provisions for the heavier punishment.

<a id="art-237"></a>第二百三十七条　以暴力、胁迫或者其他方法强制猥亵他人或者侮辱妇女的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
聚众或者在公共场所当众犯前款罪的，或者有其他恶劣情节的，处五年以上有期徒刑。  
猥亵儿童的，处五年以下有期徒刑；有下列情形之一的，处五年以上有期徒刑：  
（一）猥亵儿童多人或者多次的；  
（二）聚众猥亵儿童的，或者在公共场所当众猥亵儿童，情节恶劣的；  
（三）造成儿童伤害或者其他严重后果的；  
（四）猥亵手段恶劣或者有其他恶劣情节的。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-13" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第13条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-28" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第28条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 237** : Whoever forcibly indecently assaults another person or insults a woman by violence, coercion, or other means shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.  
Whoever commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph in an assembly or in a public place in a brazen manner, or where there are other odious circumstances, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.  
Whoever indecently assaults a child shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years; under any of the following circumstances, the offender shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years:  
(1) the offender indecently assaults multiple children or repeatedly indecently assaults children;  
(2) the offender indecently assaults a child in an assembly, or indecently assaults a child in a public place in a brazen manner, and the circumstances are odious;  
(3) the offender causes harm or other serious consequences to the child; or  
(4) the means of indecent assault are odious or there are other odious circumstances.

<a id="art-238"></a>第二百三十八条　非法拘禁他人或者以其他方法非法剥夺他人人身自由的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利。具有殴打、侮辱情节的，从重处罚。  
犯前款罪，致人重伤的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；致人死亡的，处十年以上有期徒刑。使用暴力致人伤残、死亡的，依照[本法第二百三十四条](#art-234)、第二百三十二条的规定定罪处罚。  
为索取债务非法扣押、拘禁他人的，依照前两款的规定处罚。  
国家机关工作人员利用职权犯前三款罪的，依照前三款的规定从重处罚。

**Article 238** : Whoever unlawfully detains another person or otherwise unlawfully deprives another person of personal freedom shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights. Where the offender has beaten or humiliated the victim, a severe punishment shall be imposed.  
Whoever commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph and causes serious bodily injury to another person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; whoever causes death to another person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years. Where violence causes injury or death, the offender shall be convicted and punished in accordance with Articles 234 and 232 of this Law.  
Whoever unlawfully detains or holds another person to collect a debt shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding two paragraphs.  
Where a functionary of a state organ commits any of the crimes mentioned in the preceding three paragraphs by abusing the powers of office, the offender shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding three paragraphs.

<a id="art-239"></a>第二百三十九条　以勒索财物为目的绑架他人的，或者绑架他人作为人质的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产；情节较轻的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
犯前款罪，杀害被绑架人的，或者故意伤害被绑架人，致人重伤、死亡的，处无期徒刑或者死刑，并处没收财产。  
以勒索财物为目的偷盗婴幼儿的，依照前两款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-6" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-14" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第14条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 239** : Whoever kidnaps another person for the purpose of extorting property or holds another person as a hostage shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined or have property confiscated; where the circumstances are less serious, the offender shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph and kills the kidnapped person, or intentionally injures the kidnapped person, causing serious bodily injury or death, shall be sentenced to life imprisonment or death and shall also have property confiscated.  
Whoever steals an infant or a child for the purpose of extorting property shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding two paragraphs.

<a id="art-240"></a>第二百四十条　拐卖妇女、儿童的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；有下列情形之一的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产；情节特别严重的，处死刑，并处没收财产：  
（一）拐卖妇女、儿童集团的首要分子；  
（二）拐卖妇女、儿童三人以上的；  
（三）奸淫被拐卖的妇女的；  
（四）诱骗、强迫被拐卖的妇女卖淫或者将被拐卖的妇女卖给他人迫使其卖淫的；  
（五）以出卖为目的，使用暴力、胁迫或者麻醉方法绑架妇女、儿童的；  
（六）以出卖为目的，偷盗婴幼儿的；  
（七）造成被拐卖的妇女、儿童或者其亲属重伤、死亡或者其他严重后果的；  
（八）将妇女、儿童卖往境外的。  
拐卖妇女、儿童是指以出卖为目的，有拐骗、绑架、收买、贩卖、接送、中转妇女、儿童的行为之一的。

**Article 240** : Whoever abducts and traffics in women or children shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; under any of the following circumstances, the offender shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and shall also be fined or have property confiscated; where the circumstances are especially serious, the offender shall be sentenced to death and shall also have property confiscated:  
(1) the ringleader of a group that abducts and traffics in women or children;  
(2) the offender abducts and traffics in three or more women or children;  
(3) the offender rapes an abducted woman;  
(4) the offender induces or forces an abducted woman to engage in prostitution, or sells an abducted woman to another person who forces her to engage in prostitution;  
(5) the offender kidnaps a woman or child by violence, coercion, or anesthesia for the purpose of selling the victim;  
(6) the offender steals an infant or a child for the purpose of selling the victim;  
(7) the offender causes serious bodily injury, death, or other serious consequences to an abducted woman or child or their relatives; or  
(8) the offender sells a woman or child outside the country.  
Abducting and trafficking in women or children means, for the purpose of selling, committing any of the acts of abducting, kidnapping, buying, selling, escorting, or transiting women or children.

<a id="art-241"></a>第二百四十一条　收买被拐卖的妇女、儿童的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制。  
收买被拐卖的妇女，强行与其发生性关系的，依照[本法第二百三十六条](#art-236)的规定定罪处罚。  
收买被拐卖的妇女、儿童，非法剥夺、限制其人身自由或者有伤害、侮辱等犯罪行为的，依照本法的有关规定定罪处罚。  
收买被拐卖的妇女、儿童，并有第二款、第三款规定的犯罪行为的，依照数罪并罚的规定处罚。  
收买被拐卖的妇女、儿童又出卖的，依照[本法第二百四十条](#art-240)的规定定罪处罚。  
收买被拐卖的妇女、儿童，对被买儿童没有虐待行为，不阻碍对其进行解救的，可以从轻处罚；按照被买妇女的意愿，不阻碍其返回原居住地的，可以从轻或者减轻处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-15" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第15条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 241** Whoever buys a trafficked woman or child shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance.  
Whoever buys a trafficked woman and forcibly has sexual relations with her shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 236 of this Law.  
Whoever buys a trafficked woman or child and illegally deprives or restricts his or her personal freedom, or commits any criminal act such as injury or insult, shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the relevant provisions of this Law.  
Whoever buys a trafficked woman or child and commits the criminal acts specified in the second or third paragraph shall be punished in accordance with the provisions on combined punishment for several crimes.  
Whoever buys a trafficked woman or child and then sells her or him shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 240 of this Law.  
Whoever buys a trafficked woman or child and does not abuse the bought child, or does not obstruct the rescue of the child, may be given a lighter punishment; whoever, in accordance with the wishes of the bought woman, does not obstruct her return to her original place of residence, may be given a lighter or mitigated punishment.

<a id="art-242"></a>第二百四十二条　以暴力、威胁方法阻碍国家机关工作人员解救被收买的妇女、儿童的，依照[本法第二百七十七条](#art-277)的规定定罪处罚。  
聚众阻碍国家机关工作人员解救被收买的妇女、儿童的首要分子，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；其他参与者使用暴力、威胁方法的，依照前款的规定处罚。

**Article 242** Whoever, by means of violence or threat, obstructs a functionary of a state organ from rescuing a bought woman or child shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 277 of this Law.  
The ringleader who gathers a crowd to obstruct a functionary of a state organ from rescuing a bought woman or child shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; other participants who resort to violence or threat shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-243"></a>第二百四十三条　捏造事实诬告陷害他人，意图使他人受刑事追究，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制；造成严重后果的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
国家机关工作人员犯前款罪的，从重处罚。  
不是有意诬陷，而是错告，或者检举失实的，不适用前两款的规定。

**Article 243** Whoever fabricates facts to falsely accuse another person with the intention of subjecting the other person to criminal investigation, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance; if the consequences are serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.  
Any functionary of a state organ who commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be given a severe punishment.  
The provisions of the preceding two paragraphs shall not apply to cases where a person makes a false accusation not intentionally but as a result of a mistaken complaint or inaccurate report.

<a id="art-244"></a>第二百四十四条　以暴力、威胁或者限制人身自由的方法强迫他人劳动的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
明知他人实施前款行为，为其招募、运送人员或者有其他协助强迫他人劳动行为的，依照前款的规定处罚。  
单位犯前两款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照第一款的规定处罚。  
第二百四十四条之一　违反劳动管理法规，雇用未满十六周岁的未成年人从事超强度体力劳动的，或者从事高空、井下作业的，或者在爆炸性、易燃性、放射性、毒害性等危险环境下从事劳动，情节严重的，对直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
有前款行为，造成事故，又构成其他犯罪的，依照数罪并罚的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-38" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第38条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）.md#art-4" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）》第4条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 244** Whoever compels another person to work through violence, threat, or restriction of personal freedom shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever, knowing that another person commits the act mentioned in the preceding paragraph, recruits or transports personnel for such person, or otherwise assists in compelling others to work, shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding two paragraphs, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the first paragraph.  
Article 244-1 Whoever, in violation of labor management regulations, employs a minor under the age of 16 to engage in ultra-intensive physical labor, or to work at heights or in underground operations, or to work in explosive, flammable, radioactive, toxic or other hazardous environments, if the circumstances are serious, the directly liable person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Where an act mentioned in the preceding paragraph causes an accident and also constitutes another crime, the offender shall be punished in accordance with the provisions on combined punishment for several crimes.

<a id="art-245"></a>第二百四十五条　非法搜查他人身体、住宅，或者非法侵入他人住宅的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
司法工作人员滥用职权，犯前款罪的，从重处罚。

**Article 245** Whoever unlawfully searches another person's body or residence, or unlawfully enters another person's residence, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.  
Any judicial officer who abuses his or her power to commit the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be given a severe punishment.

<a id="art-246"></a>第二百四十六条　以暴力或者其他方法公然侮辱他人或者捏造事实诽谤他人，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利。  
前款罪，告诉的才处理，但是严重危害社会秩序和国家利益的除外。  
通过信息网络实施第一款规定的行为，被害人向人民法院告诉，但提供证据确有困难的，人民法院可以要求公安机关提供协助。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-16" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第16条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 246** Whoever, by violence or other means, publicly insults another person, or fabricates facts to defame another person, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or deprivation of political rights.  
The crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be handled only upon complaint, except where the act seriously endangers social order or national interests.  
Where an act mentioned in the first paragraph is committed through an information network, and the victim brings a complaint to the People's Court but has genuine difficulty in providing evidence, the People's Court may require the public security organ to provide assistance.

<a id="art-247"></a>第二百四十七条　司法工作人员对犯罪嫌疑人、被告人实行刑讯逼供或者使用暴力逼取证人证言的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。致人伤残、死亡的，依照[本法第二百三十四条](#art-234)、第二百三十二条的规定定罪从重处罚。

**Article 247** Any judicial officer who extorts confessions from a criminal suspect or defendant by torture or extorts testimony from a witness by violence shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention. If the act causes injury or death to the person, the offender shall be convicted and given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of Articles 234 and 232 of this Law.

<a id="art-248"></a>第二百四十八条　监狱、拘留所、看守所等监管机构的监管人员对被监管人进行殴打或者体罚虐待，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。致人伤残、死亡的，依照[本法第二百三十四条](#art-234)、第二百三十二条的规定定罪从重处罚。  
监管人员指使被监管人殴打或者体罚虐待其他被监管人的，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民检察院关于强制隔离戒毒所工作人员能否成为虐待被监管人罪主体问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于强制隔离戒毒所工作人员能否成为虐待被监管人罪主体问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 248** Any supervisory personnel of a prison, detention center, custodial institution or similar supervisory institution who beats or maltreats a person under supervision by physical torture, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; if the circumstances are especially serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years. If the act causes injury or death to the person under supervision, the offender shall be convicted and given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of Articles 234 and 232 of this Law.  
Any supervisory personnel who instructs a person under supervision to beat or maltreat by physical torture another person under supervision shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-249"></a>第二百四十九条　煽动民族仇恨、民族歧视，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；情节特别严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 249** Whoever incites ethnic hatred or ethnic discrimination, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or deprivation of political rights; if the circumstances are especially serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-250"></a>第二百五十条　在出版物中刊载歧视、侮辱少数民族的内容，情节恶劣，造成严重后果的，对直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制。

**Article 250** Whoever publishes content in a publication that discriminates against or insults an ethnic minority, if the circumstances are flagrant and cause serious consequences, the directly liable person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance.

<a id="art-251"></a>第二百五十一条　国家机关工作人员非法剥夺公民的宗教信仰自由和侵犯少数民族风俗习惯，情节严重的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 251** Any functionary of a state organ who unlawfully deprives a citizen of his or her freedom of religious belief or infringes upon the customs and habits of an ethnic minority, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条　隐匿、毁弃或者非法开拆他人信件，侵犯公民通信自由权利，情节严重的，处一年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 252** Whoever conceals, destroys or unlawfully opens another person's letters, infringing upon a citizen's right to freedom of correspondence, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than one year or criminal detention.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条　邮政工作人员私自开拆或者隐匿、毁弃邮件、电报的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
犯前款罪而窃取财物的，依照[本法第二百六十四条](#art-264)的规定定罪从重处罚。  
第二百五十三条之一　违反国家有关规定，向他人出售或者提供公民个人信息，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
违反国家有关规定，将在履行职责或者提供服务过程中获得的公民个人信息，出售或者提供给他人的，依照前款的规定从重处罚。  
窃取或者以其他方法非法获取公民个人信息的，依照第一款的规定处罚。  
单位犯前三款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照各该款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-17" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第17条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 253** Any postal worker who, without authorization, opens, conceals or destroys mail or telegrams shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention.  
Whoever commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph and steals property shall be convicted and given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of Article 264 of this Law.  
Article 253-1 Whoever, in violation of relevant state regulations, sells or provides citizens' personal information to another person, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever, in violation of relevant state regulations, sells or provides citizens' personal information obtained during the course of performing duties or providing services to another person shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Whoever unlawfully obtains citizens' personal information by theft or other means shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the first paragraph.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding three paragraphs, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the respective paragraphs.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条　国家机关工作人员滥用职权、假公济私，对控告人、申诉人、批评人、举报人实行报复陷害的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处二年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 254** Any functionary of a state organ who abuses his or her power or uses public office for private gain to retaliate against or frame a complainant, petitioner, critic or whistleblower shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention; if the circumstances are serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than two years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条　公司、企业、事业单位、机关、团体的领导人，对依法履行职责、抵制违反会计法、统计法行为的会计、统计人员实行打击报复，情节恶劣的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 255** Any leader of a company, enterprise, institution, organ or organization who, for an accounting or statistical officer who lawfully performs his or her duties and resists acts in violation of the Accounting Law or the Statistics Law, retaliates against such officer, if the circumstances are flagrant, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条　在选举各级人民代表大会代表和国家机关领导人员时，以暴力、威胁、欺骗、贿赂、伪造选举文件、虚报选举票数等手段破坏选举或者妨害选民和代表自由行使选举权和被选举权，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者剥夺政治权利。

**Article 256** Whoever, during the election of deputies to the People's Congresses at various levels or leaders of state organs, disrupts an election or obstructs voters or deputies from freely exercising their right to vote or stand for election by violence, threat, deception, bribery, falsification of election documents, or false reporting of election votes, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or deprivation of political rights.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条　以暴力干涉他人婚姻自由的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
犯前款罪，致使被害人死亡的，处二年以上七年以下有期徒刑。  
第一款罪，告诉的才处理。

**Article 257** Whoever uses violence to interfere with another person's freedom of marriage shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention.  
Whoever commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph and causes the death of the victim shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than two years but not more than seven years.  
The crime mentioned in the first paragraph shall be handled only upon complaint.

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条　有配偶而重婚的，或者明知他人有配偶而与之结婚的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 258** Whoever has a spouse and commits bigamy, or knowingly marries another person who has a spouse, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条　明知是现役军人的配偶而与之同居或者结婚的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
利用职权、从属关系，以胁迫手段奸淫现役军人的妻子的，依照[本法第二百三十六条](#art-236)的规定定罪处罚。

**Article 259** Whoever knowingly cohabits with or marries a person who is the spouse of a member of the armed forces on active duty shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.  
Whoever, by taking advantage of his or her position or subordinate relationship and by means of coercion, has sexual intercourse with the wife of a member of the armed forces on active duty shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 236 of this Law.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条　虐待家庭成员，情节恶劣的，处二年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制。  
犯前款罪，致使被害人重伤、死亡的，处二年以上七年以下有期徒刑。  
第一款罪，告诉的才处理，但被害人没有能力告诉，或者因受到强制、威吓无法告诉的除外。  
第二百六十条之一　对未成年人、老年人、患病的人、残疾人等负有监护、看护职责的人虐待被监护、看护的人，情节恶劣的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。  
有第一款行为，同时构成其他犯罪的，依照处罚较重的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-18" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第18条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-19" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第19条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 260** Whoever maltreats a family member, if the circumstances are flagrant, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years, criminal detention or public surveillance.  
Whoever commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph and causes serious injury or death to the victim shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than two years but not more than seven years.  
The crime mentioned in the first paragraph shall be handled only upon complaint, except where the victim is unable to file a complaint or is unable to file a complaint due to coercion or intimidation.  
Article 260-1 Any person who has the duty of guardianship or care for a minor, elderly person, sick person or disabled person and maltreats the person under guardianship or care, if the circumstances are flagrant, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Where an act mentioned in the first paragraph also constitutes another crime, the offender shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions for the heavier punishment.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条　对于年老、年幼、患病或者其他没有独立生活能力的人，负有扶养义务而拒绝扶养，情节恶劣的，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制。

**Article 261** Whoever refuses to fulfill his or her duty to support an elderly person, minor, sick person or other person who has no independent means of living, if the circumstances are flagrant, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention or public surveillance.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条　拐骗不满十四周岁的未成年人，脱离家庭或者监护人的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
第二百六十二条之一　以暴力、胁迫手段组织残疾人或者不满十四周岁的未成年人乞讨的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
第二百六十二条之二　组织未成年人进行盗窃、诈骗、抢夺、敲诈勒索等违反治安管理活动的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-8" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第8条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-17" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第17条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 262** Whoever abducts a minor under the age of 14 away from his or her family or guardian shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.  
Article 262-1 Whoever organizes a disabled person or a minor under the age of 14 to beg by violence or coercion shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Article 262-2 Whoever organizes minors to engage in theft, fraud, snatching, extortion or other activities that violate public security administration shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.

### 第五章　侵犯财产罪

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条　以暴力、胁迫或者其他方法抢劫公私财物的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；有下列情形之一的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑，并处罚金或者没收财产：  
（一）入户抢劫的；  
（二）在公共交通工具上抢劫的；  
（三）抢劫银行或者其他金融机构的；  
（四）多次抢劫或者抢劫数额巨大的；  
（五）抢劫致人重伤、死亡的；  
（六）冒充军警人员抢劫的；  
（七）持枪抢劫的；  
（八）抢劫军用物资或者抢险、救灾、救济物资的。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民检察院关于强迫借贷行为适用法律问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于强迫借贷行为适用法律问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 243** Whoever, by violence, coercion or other means, forcibly takes public or private property shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; whoever falls under any of the following circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment or death and shall also be fined or have his or her property confiscated:  
(1) forcibly entering a residence for robbery;  
(2) committing robbery on a public means of transport;  
(3) robbing a bank or any other financial institution;  
(4) committing robbery repeatedly or robbing a huge amount of property;  
(5) causing serious injury or death to another person during robbery;  
(6) impersonating a member of the armed forces or a police officer to commit robbery;  
(7) committing robbery with a gun;  
(8) robbing military supplies or materials allocated for emergency relief, disaster relief or social assistance.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条　盗窃公私财物，数额较大的，或者多次盗窃、入户盗窃、携带凶器盗窃、扒窃的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民检察院关于单位有关人员组织实施盗窃行为如何适用法律问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于单位有关人员组织实施盗窃行为如何适用法律问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理盗窃刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理盗窃刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理盗窃刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理盗窃刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理盗窃刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理盗窃刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理盗窃刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-13" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理盗窃刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第13条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（五）.md#art-2" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（五）》第2条引用">[6]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-39" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第39条引用">[7]</a></sup>

**Article 264** Whoever steals public or private property in a relatively large amount, or repeatedly steals property, enters a residence to steal property, carries a lethal weapon to steal property, or pickpockets property, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined; whoever steals property in a huge amount or under other serious circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; whoever steals property in an especially huge amount or under other especially serious circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment and shall also be fined or have his or her property confiscated.

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条　以牟利为目的，盗接他人通信线路、复制他人电信码号或者明知是盗接、复制的电信设备、设施而使用的，依照[本法第二百六十四条](#art-264)的规定定罪处罚。

**Article 265** Whoever, for the purpose of profit, illicitly connects to another person's communication line, copies another person's telecommunication code number, or knowingly uses telecommunication equipment or facilities that have been illicitly connected or copied shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 264 of this Law.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条　诈骗公私财物，数额较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。本法另有规定的，依照规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/法律解释/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第二百六十六条的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第二百六十六条的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 266** Whoever defrauds public or private property in a relatively large amount shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined; whoever defrauds property in a huge amount or under other serious circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; whoever defrauds property in an especially huge amount or under other especially serious circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment and shall also be fined or have his or her property confiscated. Where this Law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条　抢夺公私财物，数额较大的，或者多次抢夺的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
携带凶器抢夺的，依照[本法第二百六十三条](#art-263)的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理抢夺刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理抢夺刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理抢夺刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理抢夺刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理抢夺刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理抢夺刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第4条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-20" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第20条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 267** Whoever forcibly seizes public or private property in a relatively large amount, or repeatedly seizes property, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined; whoever seizes property in a huge amount or under other serious circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; whoever seizes property in an especially huge amount or under other especially serious circumstances shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment and shall also be fined or have his or her property confiscated.  
Whoever carries a lethal weapon to seize property shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 263 of this Law.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条　聚众哄抢公私财物，数额较大或者有其他严重情节的，对首要分子和积极参加的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。

**Article 268** Whoever gathers a crowd to forcibly seize public or private property, if the amount is relatively large or there are other serious circumstances, the ringleader and active participants shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined; if the amount is huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, they shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条　犯盗窃、诈骗、抢夺罪，为窝藏赃物、抗拒抓捕或者毁灭罪证而当场使用暴力或者以暴力相威胁的，依照[本法第二百六十三条](#art-263)的规定定罪处罚。

**Article 269** Whoever commits the crime of theft, fraud or forcible seizure and, on the spot, uses violence or threatens to use violence to conceal stolen goods, resist arrest or destroy criminal evidence shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 263 of this Law.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条　将代为保管的他人财物非法占为己有，数额较大，拒不退还的，处二年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处二年以上五年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
将他人的遗忘物或者埋藏物非法占为己有，数额较大，拒不交出的，依照前款的规定处罚。  
本条罪，告诉的才处理。

**Article 270** Whoever unlawfully takes possession of another person's property that is in his or her custody, if the amount is relatively large and he or she refuses to return it, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years, criminal detention or a fine; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than two years but not more than five years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever unlawfully takes possession of another person's lost property or buried property, if the amount is relatively large and he or she refuses to hand it over, shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
The crime mentioned in this Article shall be handled only upon complaint.

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条　公司、企业或者其他单位的工作人员，利用职务上的便利，将本单位财物非法占为己有，数额较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；数额巨大的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额特别巨大的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金。  
国有公司、企业或者其他国有单位中从事公务的人员和国有公司、企业或者其他国有单位委派到非国有公司、企业以及其他单位从事公务的人员有前款行为的，依照[本法第三百八十二条](#art-382)、第三百八十三条的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-29" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第29条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 271** Any staff member of a company, enterprise or other unit who, by taking advantage of his or her position, unlawfully takes possession of the property of the unit, if the amount is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also be fined; if the amount is huge, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; if the amount is especially huge, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment and shall also be fined.  
Any person who performs public duties in a state-owned company, enterprise or other state-owned unit, or any person who is assigned by a state-owned company, enterprise or other state-owned unit to a non-state-owned company, enterprise or other unit to perform public duties, who commits the act mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Articles 382 and 383 of this Law.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条　公司、企业或者其他单位的工作人员，利用职务上的便利，挪用本单位资金归个人使用或者借贷给他人，数额较大、超过三个月未还的，或者虽未超过三个月，但数额较大、进行营利活动的，或者进行非法活动的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；挪用本单位资金数额巨大的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑；数额特别巨大的，处七年以上有期徒刑。  
国有公司、企业或者其他国有单位中从事公务的人员和国有公司、企业或者其他国有单位委派到非国有公司、企业以及其他单位从事公务的人员有前款行为的，依照[本法第三百八十四条](#art-384)的规定定罪处罚。  
有第一款行为，在提起公诉前将挪用的资金退还的，可以从轻或者减轻处罚。其中，犯罪较轻的，可以减轻或者免除处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-30" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第30条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 272** Any staff member of a company, enterprise or other unit who, by taking advantage of his or her position, misappropriates the funds of the unit for personal use or lends them to another person, if the amount is relatively large and he or she fails to repay it within three months, or if the amount is relatively large and he or she uses them for profit-making activities, or if he or she uses them for illegal activities, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; if the amount of misappropriated funds is huge, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years; if the amount is especially huge, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than seven years.  
Any person who performs public duties in a state-owned company, enterprise or other state-owned unit, or any person who is assigned by a state-owned company, enterprise or other state-owned unit to a non-state-owned company, enterprise or other unit to perform public duties, who commits the act mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 384 of this Law.  
Where a person commits the act mentioned in the first paragraph and returns the misappropriated funds before a public prosecution is initiated, he or she may be given a lighter or mitigated punishment. If the crime is minor, he or she may be given a mitigated punishment or exempted from punishment.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条　挪用用于救灾、抢险、防汛、优抚、扶贫、移民、救济款物，情节严重，致使国家和人民群众利益遭受重大损害的，对直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民检察院关于挪用失业保险基金和下岗职工基本生活保障资金的行为适用法律问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于挪用失业保险基金和下岗职工基本生活保障资金的行为适用法律问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 273** Whoever misappropriates materials allocated for emergency relief, disaster relief, flood control, social assistance, poverty alleviation, resettlement or social aid, if the circumstances are serious and cause significant damage to the interests of the state and the people, the directly liable person shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; if the circumstances are especially serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条　敲诈勒索公私财物，数额较大或者多次敲诈勒索的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用信息网络实施诽谤等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理敲诈勒索刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理敲诈勒索刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理敲诈勒索刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理敲诈勒索刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理敲诈勒索刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理敲诈勒索刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第4条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-40" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第40条引用">[5]</a></sup>

**Article 274** Whoever extorts public or private property, if the amount is relatively large or the extortion is committed repeatedly, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the amount is huge or there are other serious circumstances, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; if the amount is especially huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条　故意毁坏公私财物，数额较大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者罚金；数额巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院关于审理破坏公用电信设施刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理破坏公用电信设施刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 275** Whoever intentionally destroys public or private property, if the amount is relatively large or there are other serious circumstances, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or a fine; if the amount is huge or there are other especially serious circumstances, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条　由于泄愤报复或者其他个人目的，毁坏机器设备、残害耕畜或者以其他方法破坏生产经营的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。  
第二百七十六条之一　以转移财产、逃匿等方法逃避支付劳动者的劳动报酬或者有能力支付而不支付劳动者的劳动报酬，数额较大，经政府有关部门责令支付仍不支付的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；造成严重后果的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。  
有前两款行为，尚未造成严重后果，在提起公诉前支付劳动者的劳动报酬，并依法承担相应赔偿责任的，可以减轻或者免除处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-41" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第41条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 276** Whoever, out of resentment or revenge or for other personal purposes, destroys machinery or equipment, injures or kills draft animals, or otherwise disrupts production or business operations, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance; if the circumstances are serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.  
Article 276-1 Whoever evades paying labor remuneration to workers by transferring property or absconding, or refuses to pay labor remuneration even though he or she has the ability to do so, if the amount is relatively large and he or she still fails to pay after being ordered to do so by the relevant government department, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if serious consequences are caused, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Where a unit commits the crime mentioned in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Where the acts mentioned in the preceding two paragraphs have not caused serious consequences, and the offender pays the labor remuneration to the workers before a public prosecution is initiated and bears the corresponding liability for compensation according to law, he or she may be given a mitigated punishment or exempted from punishment.

### 第六章　妨害社会管理秩序罪

#### 第一节　扰乱公共秩序罪

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条　以暴力、威胁方法阻碍国家机关工作人员依法执行职务的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者罚金。  
以暴力、威胁方法阻碍全国人民代表大会和地方各级人民代表大会代表依法执行代表职务的，依照前款的规定处罚。  
在自然灾害和突发事件中，以暴力、威胁方法阻碍红十字会工作人员依法履行职责的，依照第一款的规定处罚。  
故意阻碍国家安全机关、公安机关依法执行国家安全工作任务，未使用暴力、威胁方法，造成严重后果的，依照第一款的规定处罚。  
暴力袭击正在依法执行职务的人民警察的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制；使用枪支、管制刀具，或者以驾驶机动车撞击等手段，严重危及其人身安全的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第8条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-12" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理袭警刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第12条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-21" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第21条引用">[6]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-31" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第31条引用">[7]</a></sup>

**Article 277** Whoever, by means of violence or threat, obstructs a functionary of a state organ from performing his or her duties according to law shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or a fine.  
Whoever, by means of violence or threat, obstructs a deputy to the National People's Congress or a local People's Congress at any level from performing his or her duties as a deputy according to law shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Whoever, during a natural disaster or an emergency, obstructs a staff member of the Red Cross Society from performing his or her duties according to law by means of violence or threat shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the first paragraph.  
Whoever intentionally obstructs a state security organ or a public security organ from performing its tasks of safeguarding state security according to law, without resorting to violence or threat, and causes serious consequences, shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the first paragraph.  
Whoever violently assaults a People's Police officer who is lawfully performing his or her duties shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance; whoever uses a firearm, controlled cutting tool, or means such as driving a motor vehicle to ram into the officer, seriously endangering his or her personal safety, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条　煽动群众暴力抗拒国家法律、行政法规实施的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；造成严重后果的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 278** Whoever incites a crowd to violently resist the implementation of national laws or administrative regulations shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or deprivation of political rights; if serious consequences are caused, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条　冒充国家机关工作人员招摇撞骗的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
冒充人民警察招摇撞骗的，依照前款的规定从重处罚。

**Article 279** Whoever impersonates a functionary of a state organ to swindle and deceive shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or deprivation of political rights; if the circumstances are serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.  
Whoever impersonates a People's Police officer to swindle and deceive shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条　伪造、变造、买卖或者盗窃、抢夺、毁灭国家机关的公文、证件、印章的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
伪造公司、企业、事业单位、人民团体的印章的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利，并处罚金。  
伪造、变造、买卖居民身份证、护照、社会保障卡、驾驶证等依法可以用于证明身份的证件的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
第二百八十条之一　在依照国家规定应当提供身份证明的活动中，使用伪造、变造的或者盗用他人的居民身份证、护照、社会保障卡、驾驶证等依法可以用于证明身份的证件，情节严重的，处拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金。  
有前款行为，同时构成其他犯罪的，依照处罚较重的规定定罪处罚。  
第二百八十条之二　盗用、冒用他人身份，顶替他人取得的高等学历教育入学资格、公务员录用资格、就业安置待遇的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金。  
组织、指使他人实施前款行为的，依照前款的规定从重处罚。  
国家工作人员有前两款行为，又构成其他犯罪的，依照数罪并罚的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理与盗窃、抢劫、诈骗、抢夺机动车相关刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理与盗窃、抢劫、诈骗、抢夺机动车相关刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理伪造、贩卖伪造的高等院校学历、学位证明刑事案件如何适用法律问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理伪造、贩卖伪造的高等院校学历、学位证明刑事案件如何适用法律问题的解释》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-22" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第22条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-23" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第23条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-32" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第32条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../经济、税务与金融/金融、证券与保险/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定.md#art-2" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定》第2条引用">[6]</a></sup>

**Article 280** Whoever forges, alters, buys or sells, or steals, robs or destroys official documents, certificates or seals of a state organ shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or deprivation of political rights and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever forges seals of a company, enterprise, institution or people's organization shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or deprivation of political rights and shall also be fined.  
Whoever forges, alters, buys or sells resident identity cards, passports, social security cards, driver's licenses or other identity documents that may be used according to law to prove identity shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or deprivation of political rights and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he or she shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Article 280-1 Whoever, in an activity that requires the provision of identity documents according to state regulations, uses forged or altered identity documents, or fraudulently uses another person's resident identity card, passport, social security card, driver's license or other identity documents that may be used according to law to prove identity, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined.  
Where an act mentioned in the preceding paragraph also constitutes another crime, the offender shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions for the heavier punishment.  
Article 280-2 Whoever fraudulently uses or usurps another person's identity to usurp the other person's qualifications for higher education admission, civil service recruitment or employment placement benefits shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined.  
Whoever organizes or instructs another person to commit the act mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Where a state functionary commits the acts mentioned in the preceding two paragraphs and also constitutes another crime, he or she shall be punished in accordance with the provisions on combined punishment for several crimes.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条　非法生产、买卖人民警察制式服装、车辆号牌等专用标志、警械，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。

**Article 281** : Whoever unlawfully produces or sells the uniforms, vehicle license plates and other special symbols, or police equipment of the People's Police, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and/or a fine.  
Where a unit commits the crime in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons directly in charge and other persons directly responsible shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条　以窃取、刺探、收买方法，非法获取国家秘密的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。  
非法持有属于国家绝密、机密的文件、资料或者其他物品，拒不说明来源与用途的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理组织考试作弊等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理组织考试作弊等刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 282** : Whoever unlawfully obtains State secrets by theft, espionage, or purchase shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights; where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.  
Whoever unlawfully holds documents, materials, or other articles classified as top secret or secret of the State and refuses to explain their source or purpose shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance.

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条　非法生产、销售专用间谍器材或者窃听、窃照专用器材的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-24" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第24条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 283** : Whoever unlawfully produces or sells special espionage equipment or special equipment for eavesdropping or surreptitious photography shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance, and/or a fine; where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years, and a fine.  
Where a unit commits the crime in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the persons directly in charge and other persons directly responsible shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条　非法使用窃听、窃照专用器材，造成严重后果的，处二年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制。  
第二百八十四条之一　在法律规定的国家考试中，组织作弊的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
为他人实施前款犯罪提供作弊器材或者其他帮助的，依照前款的规定处罚。  
为实施考试作弊行为，向他人非法出售或者提供第一款规定的考试的试题、答案的，依照第一款的规定处罚。
