Whoever, without permission of the relevant competent department under the State Council, imports solid waste as raw materials, thereby causing a major environmental pollution accident, resulting in heavy losses to public or private property or seriously endangering human health, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention, and shall be fined; if the consequences are especially serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years, and shall be fined.  
Whoever, under the pretext of using it as raw material, imports solid waste, liquid waste or gaseous waste that cannot be used as raw materials shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions of paragraph 2 and paragraph 3 of Article 152 of this Law.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条　违反保护水产资源法规，在禁渔区、禁渔期或者使用禁用的工具、方法捕捞水产品，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者罚金。

**Article 340** Whoever, in violation of the regulations on protection of aquatic resources, catches aquatic products in a forbidden fishing zone, during a forbidden fishing period or by using forbidden fishing gear or methods, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or a fine.

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条　非法猎捕、杀害国家重点保护的珍贵、濒危野生动物的，或者非法收购、运输、出售国家重点保护的珍贵、濒危野生动物及其制品的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
违反狩猎法规，在禁猎区、禁猎期或者使用禁用的工具、方法进行狩猎，破坏野生动物资源，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者罚金。  
违反野生动物保护管理法规，以食用为目的非法猎捕、收购、运输、出售第一款规定以外的在野外环境自然生长繁殖的陆生野生动物，情节严重的，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-41" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第41条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/法律解释/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第三百四十一条、第三百一十二条的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第三百四十一条、第三百一十二条的解释》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 341** : Whoever illegally hunts or kills precious or endangered wildlife under special State protection, or illegally purchases, transports or sells such wildlife and the products thereof, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years and shall also be fined or have his property confiscated.  
Whoever, in violation of hunting laws and regulations, hunts in a closed hunting area, during a closed hunting season, or uses prohibited tools or methods, thus damaging wildlife resources, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or a fine.  
Whoever, in violation of wildlife protection management laws and regulations, illegally hunts, purchases, transports, or sells, for the purpose of consumption, terrestrial wildlife that grows and reproduces naturally in the wild environment, other than that specified in the first paragraph, if the circumstances are serious, shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条　违反土地管理法规，非法占用耕地、林地等农用地，改变被占用土地用途，数量较大，造成耕地、林地等农用地大量毁坏的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金。  
第三百四十二条之一　违反自然保护地管理法规，在国家公园、国家级自然保护区进行开垦、开发活动或者修建建筑物，造成严重后果或者有其他恶劣情节的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金。  
有前款行为，同时构成其他犯罪的，依照处罚较重的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理破坏黑土地资源刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理破坏黑土地资源刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（二）.md#art-1" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（二）》第1条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-42" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第42条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 342** : Whoever, in violation of land administration laws and regulations, illegally occupies agricultural land such as cultivated land or forest land, changes the use of the occupied land, if the area is relatively large and causes substantial damage to such agricultural land, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention and shall also, or shall only, be fined.  
Article 342a: Whoever, in violation of nature reserve management laws and regulations, engages in reclamation, development activities or builds structures in a national park or national-level nature reserve, causing serious consequences or having other egregious circumstances, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention and shall also, or shall only, be fined.  
If an act prescribed in the preceding paragraph also constitutes another crime, the person shall be convicted and punished in accordance with the provisions that impose a heavier punishment.

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条　违反矿产资源法的规定，未取得采矿许可证擅自采矿，擅自进入国家规划矿区、对国民经济具有重要价值的矿区和他人矿区范围采矿，或者擅自开采国家规定实行保护性开采的特定矿种，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
违反矿产资源法的规定，采取破坏性的开采方法开采矿产资源，造成矿产资源严重破坏的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理破坏黑土地资源刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理破坏黑土地资源刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-47" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第47条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 343** : Whoever, in violation of the provisions of the Mineral Resources Law, mines without a mining license, enters without authorization a mining area covered by State plans or of great value to the national economy or another person's mining area, or mines without authorization a specific mineral species for which protective mining is prescribed by the State, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever, in violation of the provisions of the Mineral Resources Law, uses destructive mining methods to mine mineral resources, causing serious damage to mineral resources, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条　违反国家规定，非法采伐、毁坏珍贵树木或者国家重点保护的其他植物的，或者非法收购、运输、加工、出售珍贵树木或者国家重点保护的其他植物及其制品的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
第三百四十四条之一　违反国家规定，非法引进、释放或者丢弃外来入侵物种，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-43" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第43条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）.md#art-6" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）》第6条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 344** : Whoever, in violation of State regulations, illegally fells or destroys precious trees or other plants under special State protection, or illegally purchases, transports, processes or sells precious trees or other plants under special State protection and the products thereof, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Article 344a: Whoever, in violation of State regulations, illegally introduces, releases or discards invasive alien species, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also, or shall only, be fined.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条　盗伐森林或者其他林木，数量较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；数量巨大的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数量特别巨大的，处七年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
违反森林法的规定，滥伐森林或者其他林木，数量较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；数量巨大的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
非法收购、运输明知是盗伐、滥伐的林木，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
盗伐、滥伐国家级自然保护区内的森林或者其他林木的，从重处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 345** : Whoever steals felled trees from forests or other woodlands, if the volume is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the volume is large, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined; if the volume is especially large, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever, in violation of the provisions of the Forest Law, destroys forests or other woodlands by excessive felling, if the volume is relatively large, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the volume is large, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever illegally purchases or transports, knowing that the trees are stolen felled or excessively felled, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever steals fells or excessively fells trees in forests or other woodlands within a national-level nature reserve shall be given a severe punishment.

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条　单位犯本节第三百三十八条至第三百四十五条规定之罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照本节各该条的规定处罚。

**Article 346** : Where a unit commits the crimes prescribed in Articles 338 through 345 of this Section, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and other persons directly responsible shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the respective articles of this Section.

#### 第七节　走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品罪

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条　走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品，无论数量多少，都应当追究刑事责任，予以刑事处罚。  
走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品，有下列情形之一的，处十五年有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑，并处没收财产：  
（一）走私、贩卖、运输、制造鸦片一千克以上、海洛因或者甲基苯丙胺五十克以上或者其他毒品数量大的；  
（二）走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品集团的首要分子；  
（三）武装掩护走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品的；  
（四）以暴力抗拒检查、拘留、逮捕，情节严重的；  
（五）参与有组织的国际贩毒活动的。  
走私、贩卖、运输、制造鸦片二百克以上不满一千克、海洛因或者甲基苯丙胺十克以上不满五十克或者其他毒品数量较大的，处七年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
走私、贩卖、运输、制造鸦片不满二百克、海洛因或者甲基苯丙胺不满十克或者其他少量毒品的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
单位犯第二款、第三款、第四款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照各该款的规定处罚。  
利用、教唆未成年人走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品，或者向未成年人出售毒品的，从重处罚。  
对多次走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品，未经处理的，毒品数量累计计算。

**Article 347** : Whoever smuggles, traffics in, transports, or manufactures narcotic drugs, regardless of the quantity, shall be investigated for criminal liability and shall be subject to criminal punishment.  
Whoever smuggles, traffics in, transports, or manufactures narcotic drugs under any of the following circumstances shall be sentenced to 15 years of fixed-term imprisonment, life imprisonment or the death penalty and shall also have their property confiscated:  
(1) smuggling, trafficking, transporting or manufacturing more than 1,000 grams of opium, more than 50 grams of heroin or methamphetamine, or a large quantity of other narcotic drugs;  
(2) being a ringleader of a drug smuggling, trafficking, transporting or manufacturing group;  
(3) armed escort of the smuggling, trafficking, transporting or manufacturing of narcotic drugs;  
(4) violently resisting inspection, detention or arrest under serious circumstances; or  
(5) participating in organized international drug trafficking activities.  
Whoever smuggles, traffics in, transports or manufactures more than 200 grams but less than 1,000 grams of opium, more than 10 grams but less than 50 grams of heroin or methamphetamine, or a relatively large quantity of other narcotic drugs shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever smuggles, traffics, transports or manufactures less than 200 grams of opium, less than 10 grams of heroin or methamphetamine, or small quantities of other narcotic drugs shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Where a unit commits the crime prescribed in the second, third or fourth paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and other persons directly responsible shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the respective paragraphs.  
Whoever utilizes or instigates a minor to engage in the smuggling, trafficking, transporting or manufacturing of narcotic drugs or sells such drugs to a minor shall be given a severe punishment.  
With respect to repeated offending where smuggling, trafficking, transporting or manufacturing of narcotic drugs has not been dealt with, the quantity of drugs shall be aggregated.

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条　非法持有鸦片一千克以上、海洛因或者甲基苯丙胺五十克以上或者其他毒品数量大的，处七年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金；非法持有鸦片二百克以上不满一千克、海洛因或者甲基苯丙胺十克以上不满五十克或者其他毒品数量较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。

**Article 348** : Whoever illegally possesses more than 1,000 grams of opium, more than 50 grams of heroin or methamphetamine, or a large quantity of other narcotic drugs shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than seven years or life imprisonment and shall also be fined; whoever illegally possesses more than 200 grams but less than 1,000 grams of opium, more than 10 grams but less than 50 grams of heroin or methamphetamine, or a relatively large quantity of other narcotic drugs shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条　包庇走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品的犯罪分子的，为犯罪分子窝藏、转移、隐瞒毒品或者犯罪所得的财物的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
缉毒人员或者其他国家机关工作人员掩护、包庇走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品的犯罪分子的，依照前款的规定从重处罚。  
犯前两款罪，事先通谋的，以走私、贩卖、运输、制造毒品罪的共犯论处。

**Article 349** : Whoever harbors a criminal who smuggles, traffics in, transports or manufactures narcotic drugs or whoever harbors, transfers or conceals drugs or the proceeds of crime for such a criminal shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.  
If a drug enforcement officer or other functionary of a State organ covers up or harbors a criminal who smuggles, traffics in, transports or manufactures narcotic drugs, they shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Whoever, having prior collusion, commits the crimes prescribed in the preceding two paragraphs shall be punished as an accomplice in the crime of smuggling, trafficking, transporting or manufacturing narcotic drugs.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条　违反国家规定，非法生产、买卖、运输醋酸酐、乙醚、三氯甲烷或者其他用于制造毒品的原料、配剂，或者携带上述物品进出境，情节较重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；情节特别严重的，处七年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
明知他人制造毒品而为其生产、买卖、运输前款规定的物品的，以制造毒品罪的共犯论处。  
单位犯前两款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前两款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-41" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第41条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 350** : Whoever, in violation of State regulations, illegally produces, trades in, or transports acetic anhydride, ether, chloroform or other raw materials or precursors used in the manufacture of narcotic drugs, or carries the above-mentioned articles into or out of the country, if the circumstances are relatively serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are especially serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than seven years and shall also be fined or have his property confiscated.  
Whoever knowingly produces, trades in, or transports the articles specified in the preceding paragraph for a person who manufactures narcotic drugs shall be punished as an accomplice in the crime of manufacturing narcotic drugs.  
Where a unit commits the crime prescribed in the preceding two paragraphs, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and other persons directly responsible shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding two paragraphs.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条　非法种植罂粟、大麻等毒品原植物的，一律强制铲除。有下列情形之一的，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金：  
（一）种植罂粟五百株以上不满三千株或者其他毒品原植物数量较大的；  
（二）经公安机关处理后又种植的；  
（三）抗拒铲除的。  
非法种植罂粟三千株以上或者其他毒品原植物数量大的，处五年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
非法种植罂粟或者其他毒品原植物，在收获前自动铲除的，可以免除处罚。

**Article 351** : Whoever illegally cultivates poppy plants, cannabis plants or other mother plants for narcotic drugs shall be forced to eradicate them without exception. Under any of the following circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined:  
(1) cultivating more than 500 but less than 3,000 poppy plants, or a relatively large quantity of other mother plants for narcotic drugs;  
(2) cultivating again after having been subjected to treatment by a public security organ; or  
(3) resisting eradication.  
Whoever illegally cultivates more than 3,000 poppy plants, or a large quantity of other mother plants for narcotic drugs, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years and shall also be fined or have his property confiscated.  
Where a person voluntarily eradicates the illegally cultivated poppy plants or other mother plants for narcotic drugs before harvest, he may be exempted from punishment.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条　非法买卖、运输、携带、持有未经灭活的罂粟等毒品原植物种子或者幼苗，数量较大的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金。

**Article 352** : Whoever illegally trades in, transports, carries or possesses a relatively large quantity of seeds or seedlings of poppy plants or other mother plants for narcotic drugs that have not been deactivated shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also, or shall only, be fined.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条　引诱、教唆、欺骗他人吸食、注射毒品的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
强迫他人吸食、注射毒品的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
引诱、教唆、欺骗或者强迫未成年人吸食、注射毒品的，从重处罚。

**Article 353** : Whoever induces, instigates, or deceives another into ingesting or injecting a narcotic drug shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever compels another to ingest or inject a narcotic drug shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever induces, instigates, deceives or compels a minor to ingest or inject a narcotic drug shall be given a severe punishment.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条　容留他人吸食、注射毒品的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金。

**Article 354** : Whoever provides shelter for another to ingest or inject a narcotic drug shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条　依法从事生产、运输、管理、使用国家管制的麻醉药品、精神药品的人员，违反国家规定，向吸食、注射毒品的人提供国家规定管制的能够使人形成瘾癖的麻醉药品、精神药品的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。向走私、贩卖毒品的犯罪分子或者以牟利为目的，向吸食、注射毒品的人提供国家规定管制的能够使人形成瘾癖的麻醉药品、精神药品的，依照[本法第三百四十七条](#art-347)的规定定罪处罚。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。  
第三百五十五条之一　引诱、教唆、欺骗运动员使用兴奋剂参加国内、国际重大体育竞赛，或者明知运动员参加上述竞赛而向其提供兴奋剂，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金。  
组织、强迫运动员使用兴奋剂参加国内、国际重大体育竞赛的，依照前款的规定从重处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-44" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第44条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 355** : Any person who, according to law, produces, transports, manages or uses controlled narcotic or psychotropic substances, in violation of State regulations, provides narcotics or psychotropic substances that are controlled under State regulations and are addictive to a person who ingests or injects drugs shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and shall also be fined. Whoever provides the substances controlled under State regulations that can cause addiction to criminals engaged in smuggling or trafficking in narcotic drugs, or provides such substances for profit to persons who ingest or inject drugs, shall be convicted and punished in accordance with Article 347 of this Law.  
Where a unit commits the crime prescribed in the preceding paragraph, the unit shall be fined, and the person directly in charge and other persons directly responsible shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Article 355a: Whoever induces, instigates, or deceives an athlete into using a doping substance to participate in a major domestic or international sports competition, or knowingly provides a doping substance to an athlete participating in such a competition, if the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and shall also be fined.  
Whoever organizes or compels an athlete to use a doping substance to participate in a major domestic or international sports competition shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条　因走私、贩卖、运输、制造、非法持有毒品罪被判过刑，又犯本节规定之罪的，从重处罚。

**Article 356** : Whoever has previously been sentenced for the crime of smuggling, trafficking, transporting, manufacturing or illegally possessing narcotic drugs, and subsequently commits any crime prescribed in this Section, shall be given a severe punishment.

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条　本法所称的毒品，是指鸦片、海洛因、甲基苯丙胺（冰毒）、吗啡、大麻、可卡因以及国家规定管制的其他能够使人形成瘾癖的麻醉药品和精神药品。  
毒品的数量以查证属实的走私、贩卖、运输、制造、非法持有毒品的数量计算，不以纯度折算。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/毒品与走私/最高人民检察院关于《非药用类麻醉药品和精神药品管制品种增补目录》能否作为认定毒品依据的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于《非药用类麻醉药品和精神药品管制品种增补目录》能否作为认定毒品依据的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 357** : For the purposes of this Law, "narcotic drugs" means opium, heroin, methamphetamine (crystal meth), morphine, cannabis, cocaine, and other narcotic and psychotropic substances that are under State regulations and can cause addiction.  
The quantity of narcotic drugs shall be calculated based on the quantity verified to have been smuggled, trafficked, transported, manufactured or illegally possessed, and shall not be converted based on purity.

#### 第八节　组织、强迫、引诱、容留、介绍卖淫罪

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条　组织、强迫他人卖淫的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；情节严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
组织、强迫未成年人卖淫的，依照前款的规定从重处罚。  
犯前两款罪，并有杀害、伤害、强奸、绑架等犯罪行为的，依照数罪并罚的规定处罚。  
为组织卖淫的人招募、运送人员或者有其他协助组织他人卖淫行为的，处五年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/人身犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于依法惩治性侵害未成年人犯罪的意见.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院、公安部、司法部关于依法惩治性侵害未成年人犯罪的意见》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-42" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第42条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-48" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第48条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 358** : Whoever organizes or compels another to engage in prostitution shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment and shall also be fined or have his property confiscated.  
Whoever organizes or compels a minor to engage in prostitution shall be given a severe punishment in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.  
Whoever commits the crimes prescribed in the preceding two paragraphs and also commits criminal acts such as killing, injuring, raping or kidnapping shall be punished in accordance with the provisions on concurrent punishment for multiple crimes.  
Whoever recruits or transports persons for an organizer of prostitution, or otherwise assists in organizing another to engage in prostitution, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条　引诱、容留、介绍他人卖淫的，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
引诱不满十四周岁的幼女卖淫的，处五年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金。

**Article 359** : Whoever induces, harbors or procures another to engage in prostitution shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined; if the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years and shall also be fined.  
Whoever induces a girl under the age of fourteen to engage in prostitution shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条　明知自己患有梅毒、淋病等严重性病卖淫、嫖娼的，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民检察院关于构成嫖宿幼女罪主观上是否需要具备明知要件的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于构成嫖宿幼女罪主观上是否需要具备明知要件的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-43" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第43条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 360** : Whoever, knowing that he or she is suffering from a serious venereal disease such as syphilis or gonorrhea, engages in prostitution or patronizes prostitution, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention or public surveillance and shall also be fined.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条　旅馆业、饮食服务业、文化娱乐业、出租汽车业等单位的人员，利用本单位的条件，组织、强迫、引诱、容留、介绍他人卖淫的，依照[本法第三百五十八条](#art-358)、第三百五十九条的规定定罪处罚。  
前款所列单位的主要负责人，犯前款罪的，从重处罚。

**Article 361** : Personnel of hotels, catering services, cultural entertainment, taxi services and other such units who, using the conditions of their unit, organize, compel, induce, harbor, or introduce others to engage in prostitution shall be convicted and punished in accordance with Articles 358 and 359 of this Law.  
Where a principal responsible person of a unit listed in the preceding paragraph commits the crime in the preceding paragraph, a severe punishment shall be imposed.

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条　旅馆业、饮食服务业、文化娱乐业、出租汽车业等单位的人员，在公安机关查处卖淫、嫖娼活动时，为违法犯罪分子通风报信，情节严重的，依照[本法第三百一十条](#art-310)的规定定罪处罚。

**Article 362** : Personnel of hotels, catering services, cultural entertainment, taxi services and other such units who, when a public security organ investigates and deals with prostitution or whoring activities, tip off the illegal or criminal elements, where the circumstances are serious, shall be convicted and punished in accordance with Article 310 of this Law.

#### 第九节　制作、贩卖、传播淫秽物品罪

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条　以牟利为目的，制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽物品的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
为他人提供书号，出版淫秽书刊的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；明知他人用于出版淫秽书刊而提供书号的，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第5条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 363** : Whoever, for the purpose of profit, produces, reproduces, publishes, sells, or disseminates pornographic materials shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance and shall also be fined; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and a fine; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment and a fine or confiscation of property.  
Whoever provides a book number to another person for publishing pornographic books or periodicals shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance and shall also be fined exclusively or concurrently; whoever knowingly provides a book number to another person for publishing pornographic books or periodicals shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条　传播淫秽的书刊、影片、音像、图片或者其他淫秽物品，情节严重的，处二年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制。  
组织播放淫秽的电影、录像等音像制品的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
制作、复制淫秽的电影、录像等音像制品组织播放的，依照第二款的规定从重处罚。  
向不满十八周岁的未成年人传播淫秽物品的，从重处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 364** : Whoever disseminates pornographic books, periodicals, films, audiovisual recordings, pictures or other pornographic materials, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years, criminal detention, or public surveillance.  
Whoever organizes the broadcast of pornographic films, videos or other audiovisual recordings shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance and shall also be fined; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and a fine.  
Whoever produces or reproduces pornographic films, videos or other audiovisual recordings and organizes their broadcast shall be severely punished in accordance with the provisions of paragraph 2.  
Whoever disseminates pornographic materials to minors who have not attained the age of 18 shall be severely punished.

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条　组织进行淫秽表演的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处罚金；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。

**Article 365** : Whoever organizes an obscene performance shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance and shall also be fined; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years and a fine.

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条　单位犯本节第三百六十三条、第三百六十四条、第三百六十五条规定之罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照各该条的规定处罚。

**Article 366** : Where a unit commits any of the crimes provided for in Articles 363, 364, and 365 of this Section, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the directly responsible supervisor and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the respective articles.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条　本法所称淫秽物品，是指具体描绘性行为或者露骨宣扬色情的诲淫性的书刊、影片、录像带、录音带、图片及其他淫秽物品。  
有关人体生理、医学知识的科学著作不是淫秽物品。  
包含有色情内容的有艺术价值的文学、艺术作品不视为淫秽物品。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理利用互联网、移动通讯终端、声讯台制作、复制、出版、贩卖、传播淫秽电子信息刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 367** : For the purposes of this Law, 'pornographic materials' refer to obscene books, periodicals, films, videotapes, audiotapes, pictures and other pornographic materials that specifically depict sexual acts or blatantly publicize pornography.  
Scientific works on human physiology or medical knowledge shall not be considered pornographic materials.  
Literary or artistic works of artistic value that contain pornographic content shall not be considered pornographic materials.

### 第七章　危害国防利益罪

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条　以暴力、威胁方法阻碍军人依法执行职务的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者罚金。  
故意阻碍武装部队军事行动，造成严重后果的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 368** : Whoever obstructs a military person in the lawful performance of duties by means of violence or threat shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance or a fine.  
Whoever intentionally obstructs a military operation of the armed forces, causing serious consequences, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条　破坏武器装备、军事设施、军事通信的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制；破坏重要武器装备、军事设施、军事通信的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。  
过失犯前款罪，造成严重后果的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成特别严重后果的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。  
战时犯前两款罪的，从重处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（五）.md#art-3" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（五）》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 369** : Whoever destroys weapons, equipment, military installations, or military communications shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance; whoever destroys important weapons, equipment, military installations, or military communications shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.  
Whoever negligently commits the crime in the preceding paragraph, causing serious consequences, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; whoever causes especially serious consequences shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.  
Whoever commits the crimes in the preceding two paragraphs during wartime shall be severely punished.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条　明知是不合格的武器装备、军事设施而提供给武装部队的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。  
过失犯前款罪，造成严重后果的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成特别严重后果的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。  
单位犯第一款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照第一款的规定处罚。

**Article 370** : Whoever knowingly provides substandard weapons, equipment, or military installations to the armed forces shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.  
Whoever negligently commits the crime in the preceding paragraph, causing serious consequences, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; whoever causes especially serious consequences shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.  
Where a unit commits the crime in the first paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the directly responsible supervisor and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the first paragraph.

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条　聚众冲击军事禁区，严重扰乱军事禁区秩序的，对首要分子，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；对其他积极参加的，处五年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利。  
聚众扰乱军事管理区秩序，情节严重，致使军事管理区工作无法进行，造成严重损失的，对首要分子，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑；对其他积极参加的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利。

**Article 371** : Whoever assembles a crowd to charge into a military restricted zone, severely disrupting the order of the military restricted zone, shall, with respect to the ringleader, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; with respect to other active participants, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights.  
Whoever assembles a crowd to disrupt the order of a military administrative zone, where the circumstances are serious, rendering the work of the military administrative zone impossible and causing serious losses, shall, with respect to the ringleader, be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years; with respect to other active participants, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights.

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条　冒充军人招摇撞骗的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 372** : Whoever impersonates a military person to cheat and bluff shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条　煽动军人逃离部队或者明知是逃离部队的军人而雇用，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制。

**Article 373** : Whoever incites a military person to desert the unit or employs such a person knowing that he or she is a military person who has deserted the unit, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance.

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条　在征兵工作中徇私舞弊，接送不合格兵员，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成特别严重后果的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 374** : Whoever engages in fraud and malpractice in conscription work, recruiting and delivering substandard recruits, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; whoever causes especially serious consequences shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条　伪造、变造、买卖或者盗窃、抢夺武装部队公文、证件、印章的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役、管制或者剥夺政治权利；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
非法生产、买卖武装部队制式服装，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金。  
伪造、盗窃、买卖或者非法提供、使用武装部队车辆号牌等专用标志，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制，并处或者单处罚金；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
单位犯第二款、第三款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照各该款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-12" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第12条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 375** : Whoever forges, alters, buys, sells, steals, or forcibly seizes official documents, certificates, or seals of the armed forces shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, public surveillance, or deprivation of political rights; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.  
Whoever illegally produces or buys or sells uniforms of the armed forces, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance and shall also be fined exclusively or concurrently.  
Whoever forges, steals, buys, sells, or illegally provides or uses special signs such as vehicle number plates of the armed forces, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance and shall also be fined exclusively or concurrently; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and a fine.  
Where a unit commits the crime in the second or third paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the directly responsible supervisor and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the respective paragraphs.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条　预备役人员战时拒绝、逃避征召或者军事训练，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
公民战时拒绝、逃避服役，情节严重的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 376** : A reservist who, during wartime, refuses or evades conscription or military training, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.  
A citizen who, during wartime, refuses or evades military service, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条　战时故意向武装部队提供虚假敌情，造成严重后果的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；造成特别严重后果的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑。

**Article 377** : Whoever, during wartime, intentionally provides false enemy information to the armed forces, causing serious consequences, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; whoever causes especially serious consequences shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment.

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条　战时造谣惑众，扰乱军心的，处三年以下有期徒刑、拘役或者管制；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 378** : Whoever, during wartime, spreads rumors to confuse and disrupt the morale of the armed forces shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, criminal detention, or public surveillance; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条　战时明知是逃离部队的军人而为其提供隐蔽处所、财物，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 379** : Whoever, during wartime, knowingly provides a hiding place or property to a military person who has deserted the unit, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条　战时拒绝或者故意延误军事订货，情节严重的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成严重后果的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 380** : Where a unit refuses or intentionally delays military orders during wartime, where the circumstances are serious, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the directly responsible supervisor and other directly liable persons shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; whoever causes serious consequences shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条　战时拒绝军事征收、征用，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 381** : Whoever, during wartime, refuses military requisition or procurement, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

### 第八章　贪污贿赂罪

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条　国家工作人员利用职务上的便利，侵吞、窃取、骗取或者以其他手段非法占有公共财物的，是贪污罪。  
受国家机关、国有公司、企业、事业单位、人民团体委托管理、经营国有财产的人员，利用职务上的便利，侵吞、窃取、骗取或者以其他手段非法占有国有财物的，以贪污论。  
与前两款所列人员勾结，伙同贪污的，以共犯论处。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/法律解释/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第九十三条第二款的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第九十三条第二款的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 382** : A state functionary who, by taking advantage of his or her position, embezzles, steals, swindles, or illegally appropriates public property by other means shall be guilty of the crime of embezzlement.  
A person who is entrusted by state organs, state-owned companies, enterprises, institutions, or people's organizations to manage or operate state-owned property, and who, by taking advantage of his or her position, embezzles, steals, swindles, or illegally appropriates such property by other means shall be considered to have committed embezzlement.  
Whoever conspires with and jointly commits embezzlement with a person described in the preceding two paragraphs shall be dealt with as an accomplice.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条　对犯贪污罪的，根据情节轻重，分别依照下列规定处罚：  
（一）贪污数额较大或者有其他较重情节的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金。  
（二）贪污数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
（三）贪污数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产；数额特别巨大，并使国家和人民利益遭受特别重大损失的，处无期徒刑或者死刑，并处没收财产。  
对多次贪污未经处理的，按照累计贪污数额处罚。  
犯第一款罪，在提起公诉前如实供述自己罪行、真诚悔罪、积极退赃，避免、减少损害结果的发生，有第一项规定情形的，可以从轻、减轻或者免除处罚；有第二项、第三项规定情形的，可以从轻处罚。  
犯第一款罪，有第三项规定情形被判处死刑缓期执行的，人民法院根据犯罪情节等情况可以同时决定在其死刑缓期执行二年期满依法减为无期徒刑后，终身监禁，不得减刑、假释。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-44" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第44条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 383** : With respect to a person who commits the crime of embezzlement, punishment shall be imposed in accordance with the following provisions based on the seriousness of the circumstances:  
(1) Where the amount of embezzlement is relatively large, or where there are other relatively serious circumstances, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention, and a fine shall also be imposed.  
(2) Where the amount of embezzlement is huge, or where there are other serious circumstances, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years, and a fine or confiscation of property shall also be imposed.  
(3) Where the amount of embezzlement is especially huge, or where there are other especially serious circumstances, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment, and a fine or confiscation of property shall also be imposed; where the amount is especially huge and the interests of the State and the people suffer especially heavy losses, the sentence shall be life imprisonment or the death penalty, and confiscation of property shall also be imposed.  
Where there have been multiple acts of embezzlement that have not been dealt with, punishment shall be imposed based on the cumulative amount of embezzlement.  
Where a person commits the crime in the first paragraph and, before a public prosecution is initiated, truthfully confesses his or her crime, sincerely repents, and actively returns the stolen property, thus avoiding or reducing the occurrence of harmful consequences, and if the circumstances fall under paragraph (1), the punishment may be mitigated or exempted; if the circumstances fall under paragraph (2) or (3), the punishment may be mitigated.  
Where a person commits the crime in the first paragraph and is sentenced to death with a two-year reprieve due to the circumstances under paragraph (3), the People's Court may, depending on the criminal circumstances and other relevant factors, also decide to impose life imprisonment without commutation or parole after the two-year reprieve expires and the sentence is commuted to life imprisonment upon completion of the relevant procedures according to law.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条　国家工作人员利用职务上的便利，挪用公款归个人使用，进行非法活动的，或者挪用公款数额较大、进行营利活动的，或者挪用公款数额较大、超过三个月未还的，是挪用公款罪，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑。挪用公款数额巨大不退还的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑。  
挪用用于救灾、抢险、防汛、优抚、扶贫、移民、救济款物归个人使用的，从重处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民检察院关于挪用失业保险基金和下岗职工基本生活保障资金的行为适用法律问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于挪用失业保险基金和下岗职工基本生活保障资金的行为适用法律问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-30" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第30条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/法律解释/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第三百八十四条第一款的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第三百八十四条第一款的解释》第1条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 384** : A state functionary who, by taking advantage of his or her position, misappropriates public funds for personal use to engage in illegal activities, or misappropriates a relatively large amount of public funds for profit-making activities, or misappropriates a relatively large amount of public funds that remain unpaid for more than three months, shall be guilty of the crime of misappropriation of public funds, and shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years. Where the amount of misappropriated public funds is huge and cannot be returned, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment.  
Whoever misappropriates funds allocated for disaster relief, emergency rescue, flood control, special care for disabled servicemen and family members of revolutionary martyrs, poverty alleviation, resettlement, or relief, and uses them for personal purposes, shall be severely punished.

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条　国家工作人员利用职务上的便利，索取他人财物的，或者非法收受他人财物，为他人谋取利益的，是受贿罪。  
国家工作人员在经济往来中，违反国家规定，收受各种名义的回扣、手续费，归个人所有的，以受贿论处。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/全国人大常委会关于中华人民共和国刑法第三百一十三条的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人大常委会关于中华人民共和国刑法第三百一十三条的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）.md#art-8" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）》第8条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/法律解释/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第三百一十三条的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第三百一十三条的解释》第1条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 385** : A state functionary who, by taking advantage of his or her position, extorts property from another person, or illegally accepts another person's property and seeks benefits for that person, shall be guilty of the crime of accepting bribes.  
A state functionary who, in economic activities, violates state regulations by accepting various kinds of kickbacks or service charges in any name for personal ownership shall be considered to have accepted bribes.

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条　对犯受贿罪的，根据受贿所得数额及情节，依照[本法第三百八十三条](#art-383)的规定处罚。索贿的从重处罚。

**Article 386** : With respect to a person who commits the crime of accepting bribes, punishment shall be imposed in accordance with the provisions of Article 383 of this Law based on the amount of bribes accepted and the circumstances. Extortion of bribes shall be subject to a severe punishment.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条　国家机关、国有公司、企业、事业单位、人民团体，索取、非法收受他人财物，为他人谋取利益，情节严重的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
前款所列单位，在经济往来中，在帐外暗中收受各种名义的回扣、手续费的，以受贿论，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）.md#art-4" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 387** : Where a state organ, a state-owned company, enterprise, institution, or a people's organization extorts or illegally accepts another person's property and seeks benefits for that person, where the circumstances are serious, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the directly responsible supervisor and other directly liable persons shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.  
Where a unit listed in the preceding paragraph, in economic activities, secretly accepts various kinds of kickbacks or service charges in any name outside the accounts, it shall be considered to have accepted bribes and shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条　国家工作人员利用本人职权或者地位形成的便利条件，通过其他国家工作人员职务上的行为，为请托人谋取不正当利益，索取请托人财物或者收受请托人财物的，以受贿论处。  
第三百八十八条之一　国家工作人员的近亲属或者其他与该国家工作人员关系密切的人，通过该国家工作人员职务上的行为，或者利用该国家工作人员职权或者地位形成的便利条件，通过其他国家工作人员职务上的行为，为请托人谋取不正当利益，索取请托人财物或者收受请托人财物，数额较大或者有其他较重情节的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；数额巨大或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；数额特别巨大或者有其他特别严重情节的，处七年以上有期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
离职的国家工作人员或者其近亲属以及其他与其关系密切的人，利用该离职的国家工作人员原职权或者地位形成的便利条件实施前款行为的，依照前款的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-13" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第13条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 388** : A state functionary who, by taking advantage of the convenience formed by his or her own authority or position, through the conduct of another state functionary in his or her official capacity, seeks illegitimate benefits for the client and extorts or accepts property from the client, shall be considered to have accepted bribes.  
Article 388-1: A close relative of a state functionary or another person who is closely related to the state functionary, through the conduct of the state functionary in his or her official capacity, or by taking advantage of the convenience formed by the state functionary's authority or position, through the conduct of another state functionary in his or her official capacity, seeks illegitimate benefits for the client and extorts or accepts property from the client, where the amount is relatively large or where there are other relatively serious circumstances, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and a fine; where the amount is huge or where there are other serious circumstances, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and a fine; where the amount is especially huge or where there are other especially serious circumstances, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than seven years and a fine or confiscation of property.  
Where a former state functionary, or his or her close relative, or another person closely related to the former state functionary, commits the act in the preceding paragraph by taking advantage of the convenience formed by the former state functionary's former authority or position, the punishment shall be determined by conviction and sentencing in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-389"></a>第三百八十九条　为谋取不正当利益，给予国家工作人员以财物的，是行贿罪。  
在经济往来中，违反国家规定，给予国家工作人员以财物，数额较大的，或者违反国家规定，给予国家工作人员以各种名义的回扣、手续费的，以行贿论处。  
因被勒索给予国家工作人员以财物，没有获得不正当利益的，不是行贿。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-49" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第49条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 389** : Whoever gives property to a state functionary in order to seek illegitimate benefits shall be guilty of the crime of offering bribes.  
Whoever, in economic activities, violates state regulations by giving a relatively large amount of property to a state functionary, or violates state regulations by giving various kinds of kickbacks or service charges in any name to a state functionary, shall be considered to have offered bribes.  
Whoever gives property to a state functionary due to extortion and obtains no illegitimate benefits shall not be considered to have offered bribes.

<a id="art-390"></a>第三百九十条　对犯行贿罪的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；因行贿谋取不正当利益，情节严重的，或者使国家利益遭受重大损失的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；情节特别严重的，或者使国家利益遭受特别重大损失的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑，并处罚金或者没收财产。  
行贿人在被追诉前主动交待行贿行为的，可以从轻或者减轻处罚。其中，犯罪较轻的，对侦破重大案件起关键作用的，或者有重大立功表现的，可以减轻或者免除处罚。  
第三百九十条之一　为谋取不正当利益，向国家工作人员的近亲属或者其他与该国家工作人员关系密切的人，或者向离职的国家工作人员或者其近亲属以及其他与其关系密切的人行贿的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金；情节严重的，或者使国家利益遭受重大损失的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金；情节特别严重的，或者使国家利益遭受特别重大损失的，处七年以上十年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第4条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第10条引用">[6]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-13" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理行贿刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第13条引用">[7]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-45" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第45条引用">[8]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-46" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第46条引用">[9]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）.md#art-5" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）》第5条引用">[10]</a></sup>

**Article 390** : With respect to a person who commits the crime of offering bribes, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention and a fine; where the person seeks illegitimate benefits by offering bribes, and the circumstances are serious, or causes heavy losses to the interests of the State, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years and a fine; where the circumstances are especially serious, or causes especially heavy losses to the interests of the State, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment and a fine or confiscation of property.  
Where the bribe-giver voluntarily confesses the act of offering bribes before being prosecuted, the punishment may be mitigated or reduced; where the crime is relatively minor, or where the confession plays a key role in detecting a major case, or where there is major meritorious performance, the punishment may be reduced or exempted.  
Article 390-1: Whoever offers bribes to a close relative of a state functionary or another person closely related to the state functionary, or to a former state functionary or his or her close relative or another person closely related to the former state functionary, in order to seek illegitimate benefits, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and a fine; where the circumstances are serious, or causes heavy losses to the interests of the State, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and a fine; where the circumstances are especially serious, or causes especially heavy losses to the interests of the State, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than seven years but not more than ten years and a fine.  
Where a unit commits the crime in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the directly responsible supervisor and other directly liable persons shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and a fine.

<a id="art-391"></a>第三百九十一条　为谋取不正当利益，给予国家机关、国有公司、企业、事业单位、人民团体以财物的，或者在经济往来中，违反国家规定，给予各种名义的回扣、手续费的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金。  
单位犯前款罪的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，依照前款的规定处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-47" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第47条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）.md#art-6" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）》第6条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 391** : Whoever gives property to a state organ, state-owned company, enterprise, institution, or people's organization in order to seek illegitimate benefits, or violates state regulations by giving various kinds of kickbacks or service charges in any name in economic activities, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and a fine.  
Where a unit commits the crime in the preceding paragraph, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the directly responsible supervisor and other directly liable persons shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-392"></a>第三百九十二条　向国家工作人员介绍贿赂，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金。  
介绍贿赂人在被追诉前主动交待介绍贿赂行为的，可以减轻处罚或者免除处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-48" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第48条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 392** : Whoever introduces a bribe to a state functionary, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and a fine.  
Where the introducer of the bribe voluntarily confesses the act of introducing the bribe before being prosecuted, the punishment may be reduced or exempted.

<a id="art-393"></a>第三百九十三条　单位为谋取不正当利益而行贿，或者违反国家规定，给予国家工作人员以回扣、手续费，情节严重的，对单位判处罚金，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处罚金。因行贿取得的违法所得归个人所有的，依照[本法第三百八十九条](#art-389)、第三百九十条的规定定罪处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-49" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第49条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）.md#art-7" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十二）》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 393** : Where a unit offers bribes to seek illegitimate benefits, or violates state regulations by giving kickbacks or service charges to a state functionary, where the circumstances are serious, a fine shall be imposed on the unit, and the directly responsible supervisor and other directly liable persons shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention and a fine. Where the illegal gains obtained from offering bribes are owned by an individual, punishment shall be imposed by conviction and sentencing in accordance with the provisions of Articles 389 and 390 of this Law.

<a id="art-394"></a>第三百九十四条　国家工作人员在国内公务活动或者对外交往中接受礼物，依照国家规定应当交公而不交公，数额较大的，依照[本法第三百八十二条](#art-382)、第三百八十三条的规定定罪处罚。

**Article 394** : Where a state functionary accepts gifts in domestic official activities or in foreign exchanges, which should be turned over to the State according to state regulations, but fails to do so, and the amount is relatively large, punishment shall be imposed by conviction and sentencing in accordance with the provisions of Articles 382 and 383 of this Law.

<a id="art-395"></a>第三百九十五条　国家工作人员的财产、支出明显超过合法收入，差额巨大的，可以责令该国家工作人员说明来源，不能说明来源的，差额部分以非法所得论，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；差额特别巨大的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。财产的差额部分予以追缴。  
国家工作人员在境外的存款，应当依照国家规定申报。数额较大、隐瞒不报的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节较轻的，由其所在单位或者上级主管机关酌情给予行政处分。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）.md#art-14" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（七）》第14条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 395** : Where the property or expenditures of a state functionary clearly exceed his or her lawful income, and the difference is huge, the state functionary may be ordered to explain the source; if the source cannot be explained, the difference shall be deemed illegal income, and the punishment shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the difference is especially huge, the punishment shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years. The difference in property shall be recovered.  
A state functionary shall declare his or her deposits held abroad in accordance with state regulations. Where the amount is relatively large and concealed without declaration, the punishment shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are relatively minor, the unit to which the person belongs or the competent authority at a higher level may impose an administrative sanction as appropriate.

<a id="art-396"></a>第三百九十六条　国家机关、国有公司、企业、事业单位、人民团体，违反国家规定，以单位名义将国有资产集体私分给个人，数额较大的，对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役，并处或者单处罚金；数额巨大的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑，并处罚金。  
司法机关、行政执法机关违反国家规定，将应当上缴国家的罚没财物，以单位名义集体私分给个人的，依照前款的规定处罚。

**Article 396** : Where a state organ, state-owned company, enterprise, institution, or people's organization violates state regulations and distributes state-owned assets collectively to individuals in the name of the unit, where the amount is relatively large, the directly responsible supervisor and other directly liable persons shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention and a fine exclusively or concurrently; where the amount is huge, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years and a fine.  
Where a judicial organ or an administrative law enforcement organ violates state regulations and distributes fines and confiscated property that should be turned over to the State to individuals collectively in the name of the unit, punishment shall be imposed in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

### 第九章　渎职罪

<a id="art-397"></a>第三百九十七条　国家机关工作人员滥用职权或者玩忽职守，致使公共财产、国家和人民利益遭受重大损失的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。本法另有规定的，依照规定。  
国家机关工作人员徇私舞弊，犯前款罪的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。本法另有规定的，依照规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../经济、税务与金融/农业、资源与能源/最高人民检察院关于对林业主管部门工作人员在发放林木采伐许可证之外滥用职权玩忽职守致使森林遭受严重破坏的行为适用法律问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于对林业主管部门工作人员在发放林木采伐许可证之外滥用职权玩忽职守致使森林遭受严重破坏的行为适用法律问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/财产犯罪/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理与盗窃、抢劫、诈骗、抢夺机动车相关刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理与盗窃、抢劫、诈骗、抢夺机动车相关刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理渎职刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释（一）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理渎职刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释（一）》第1条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理非法制造、买卖、运输、储存毒鼠强等禁用剧毒化学品刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理非法制造、买卖、运输、储存毒鼠强等禁用剧毒化学品刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第4条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../经济、税务与金融/金融、证券与保险/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定.md#art-6" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于惩治骗购外汇、逃汇和非法买卖外汇犯罪的决定》第6条引用">[5]</a></sup>

**Article 397** : Where a state organ functionary abuses his or her power or neglects his or her duty, causing heavy losses to public property, the interests of the State and the people, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years. Where this Law provides otherwise, those provisions shall prevail.  
Where a state organ functionary commits the crime in the preceding paragraph through engaging in malpractices for personal gain, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years. Where this Law provides otherwise, those provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-398"></a>第三百九十八条　国家机关工作人员违反保守国家秘密法的规定，故意或者过失泄露国家秘密，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。  
非国家机关工作人员犯前款罪的，依照前款的规定酌情处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-36" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第36条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 398** : Where a state organ functionary violates the provisions of the Law on Guarding State Secrets, intentionally or negligently divulging state secrets, where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.  
Where a person who is not a state organ functionary commits the crime in the preceding paragraph, punishment shall be imposed as appropriate in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-399"></a>第三百九十九条　司法工作人员徇私枉法、徇情枉法，对明知是无罪的人而使他受追诉、对明知是有罪的人而故意包庇不使他受追诉，或者在刑事审判活动中故意违背事实和法律作枉法裁判的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑。  
在民事、行政审判活动中故意违背事实和法律作枉法裁判，情节严重的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
在执行判决、裁定活动中，严重不负责任或者滥用职权，不依法采取诉讼保全措施、不履行法定执行职责，或者违法采取诉讼保全措施、强制执行措施，致使当事人或者其他人的利益遭受重大损失的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；致使当事人或者其他人的利益遭受特别重大损失的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
司法工作人员收受贿赂，有前三款行为的，同时又构成[本法第三百八十五条](#art-385)规定之罪的，依照处罚较重的规定定罪处罚。  
第三百九十九条之一　依法承担仲裁职责的人员，在仲裁活动中故意违背事实和法律作枉法裁决，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）.md#art-20" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（六）》第20条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）.md#art-8" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（四）》第8条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 399** : Where a judicial functionary engages in malpractices for personal gain, or bends the law for personal favor, causing a person whom he or she knows to be innocent to be subjected to prosecution, or intentionally shielding a person whom he or she knows to be guilty from prosecution, or intentionally acts contrary to facts and law to make a wrongful judgment in criminal adjudication activities, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years.  
Where a judicial functionary intentionally acts contrary to facts and law to make a wrongful judgment in civil or administrative adjudication activities, where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years.  
Where a judicial functionary, in the execution of a judgment or ruling, is seriously irresponsible or abuses his or her power, failing to take litigation preservation measures according to law, failing to perform statutory enforcement duties, or illegally taking litigation preservation measures or compulsory enforcement measures, causing heavy losses to the interests of the parties or other persons, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the losses to the interests of the parties or other persons are especially heavy, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years.  
Where a judicial functionary accepts bribes and commits any of the acts in the preceding three paragraphs, and the act also constitutes the crime provided for in Article 385 of this Law, the punishment shall be imposed by conviction and sentencing in accordance with the provision that provides for a heavier punishment.  
Article 399-1: Where a person who undertakes statutory arbitration duties intentionally acts contrary to facts and law to make a wrongful award in arbitration activities, where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-400"></a>第四百条　司法工作人员私放在押的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者罪犯的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑。  
司法工作人员由于严重不负责任，致使在押的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者罪犯脱逃，造成严重后果的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成特别严重后果的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民检察院关于工人等非监管机关在编监管人员私放在押人员行为和失职致使在押人员脱逃行为适用法律问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于工人等非监管机关在编监管人员私放在押人员行为和失职致使在押人员脱逃行为适用法律问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 400** : Where a judicial functionary releases a criminal suspect, defendant, or criminal in custody without authorization, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially serious, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years.  
Where a judicial functionary, due to serious irresponsibility, causes a criminal suspect, defendant, or criminal in custody to escape, resulting in serious consequences, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where especially serious consequences are caused, the sentence shall be fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-401"></a>第四百零一条　司法工作人员徇私舞弊，对不符合减刑、假释、暂予监外执行条件的罪犯，予以减刑、假释或者暂予监外执行的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 401** Where a judicial officer engages in malpractices for personal gain, and commutes the sentence of, grants parole to, or temporarily serves a sentence outside prison for a convict who does not meet the conditions for commutation of punishment, parole, or temporary service of sentence outside prison, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-402"></a>第四百零二条　行政执法人员徇私舞弊，对依法应当移交司法机关追究刑事责任的不移交，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成严重后果的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 402** Where an administrative law enforcement officer engages in malpractices for personal gain, and fails to transfer a person who shall be transferred according to law to the judicial authorities for investigation of criminal liability, where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where serious consequences are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-403"></a>第四百零三条　国家有关主管部门的国家机关工作人员，徇私舞弊，滥用职权，对不符合法律规定条件的公司设立、登记申请或者股票、债券发行、上市申请，予以批准或者登记，致使公共财产、国家和人民利益遭受重大损失的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
上级部门强令登记机关及其工作人员实施前款行为的，对其直接负责的主管人员，依照前款的规定处罚。

**Article 403** Where a state functionary of the relevant competent department of the State, by engaging in malpractices for personal gain and abusing his power, approves or registers the establishment, registration, application for issuance or listing of stocks or bonds of a company that does not meet the conditions prescribed by law, thereby causing significant losses to public property, the State, or the interests of the people, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.  
Where the superior department compels the registration authority and its functionaries to commit the act prescribed in the preceding paragraph, the directly responsible person in charge shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-404"></a>第四百零四条　税务机关的工作人员徇私舞弊，不征或者少征应征税款，致使国家税收遭受重大损失的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成特别重大损失的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 404** Where a functionary of the tax authority engages in malpractices for personal gain, and fails to collect or under-collect the tax payable, thereby causing significant losses to the State's tax revenue, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where especially significant losses are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-405"></a>第四百零五条　税务机关的工作人员违反法律、行政法规的规定，在办理发售发票、抵扣税款、出口退税工作中，徇私舞弊，致使国家利益遭受重大损失的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；致使国家利益遭受特别重大损失的，处五年以上有期徒刑。  
其他国家机关工作人员违反国家规定，在提供出口货物报关单、出口收汇核销单等出口退税凭证的工作中，徇私舞弊，致使国家利益遭受重大损失的，依照前款的规定处罚。

**Article 405** Where a functionary of the tax authority, in violation of the provisions of laws and administrative regulations, engages in malpractices for personal gain in selling invoices, offsetting tax, or refunding export tax, thereby causing significant losses to the State's interests, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where especially significant losses are caused to the State's interests, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.  
Where another state functionary, in violation of State regulations, engages in malpractices for personal gain in providing certificates such as export goods customs declarations and export collection verification certificates for tax refund, thereby causing significant losses to the State's interests, he shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-406"></a>第四百零六条　国家机关工作人员在签订、履行合同过程中，因严重不负责任被诈骗，致使国家利益遭受重大损失的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；致使国家利益遭受特别重大损失的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 406** Where a state functionary, due to gross irresponsibility in signing or performing a contract, is defrauded, thereby causing significant losses to the State's interests, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where especially significant losses are caused to the State's interests, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-407"></a>第四百零七条　林业主管部门的工作人员违反森林法的规定，超过批准的年采伐限额发放林木采伐许可证或者违反规定滥发林木采伐许可证，情节严重，致使森林遭受严重破坏的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../经济、税务与金融/农业、资源与能源/最高人民检察院关于对林业主管部门工作人员在发放林木采伐许可证之外滥用职权玩忽职守致使森林遭受严重破坏的行为适用法律问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民检察院关于对林业主管部门工作人员在发放林木采伐许可证之外滥用职权玩忽职守致使森林遭受严重破坏的行为适用法律问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 407** Where a functionary of the forestry competent authority, in violation of the provisions of the Forest Law, issues forest tree felling licenses exceeding the approved annual felling quota, or abuses the issuance of forest tree felling licenses in violation of regulations, where the circumstances are severe and causes severe damage to forests, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-408"></a>第四百零八条　负有环境保护监督管理职责的国家机关工作人员严重不负责任，导致发生重大环境污染事故，致使公私财产遭受重大损失或者造成人身伤亡的严重后果的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
第四百零八条之一　负有食品药品安全监督管理职责的国家机关工作人员，滥用职权或者玩忽职守，有下列情形之一，造成严重后果或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成特别严重后果或者有其他特别严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑：  
（一）瞒报、谎报食品安全事故、药品安全事件的；  
（二）对发现的严重食品药品安全违法行为未按规定查处的；  
（三）在药品和特殊食品审批审评过程中，对不符合条件的申请准予许可的；  
（四）依法应当移交司法机关追究刑事责任不移交的；  
（五）有其他滥用职权或者玩忽职守行为的。  
徇私舞弊犯前款罪的，从重处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）.md#art-49" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（八）》第49条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-45" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第45条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 408** Where a state functionary responsible for environmental protection supervision and administration is grossly irresponsible, resulting in a major environmental pollution accident, causing significant losses to public or private property, or serious consequences of personal injury or death, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.  
Article 408-1 Where a state functionary responsible for food and drug safety supervision and administration abuses his power or neglects his duty, falls under any of the following circumstances, and causes serious consequences or has other severe circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where especially serious consequences are caused or there are other especially severe circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years:
