(1) concealing or falsely reporting food safety accidents or drug safety incidents;  
(2) failing to investigate and punish serious food and drug safety violations as legally required;  
(3) granting permission to applications that do not meet the conditions during the approval and evaluation of drugs and special foods;  
(4) failing to transfer matters that shall be transferred according to law to the judicial authorities for investigation of criminal liability;  
(5) having other acts of abusing power or neglecting duty.  
Where a person engages in malpractices for personal gain and commits the crime in the preceding paragraph, he shall be given a severe punishment.

<a id="art-409"></a>第四百零九条　从事传染病防治的政府卫生行政部门的工作人员严重不负责任，导致传染病传播或者流行，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 409** Where a functionary of the government health administration department engaged in the prevention and control of infectious diseases is grossly irresponsible, resulting in the spread or prevalence of infectious diseases, where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-410"></a>第四百一十条　国家机关工作人员徇私舞弊，违反土地管理法规，滥用职权，非法批准征收、征用、占用土地，或者非法低价出让国有土地使用权，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；致使国家或者集体利益遭受特别重大损失的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/法律解释/全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第二百二十八条、第三百四十二条、第四百一十条的解释.md#art-1" title="被《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于《中华人民共和国刑法》第二百二十八条、第三百四十二条、第四百一十条的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 410** Where a state functionary engages in malpractices for personal gain, violates the provisions of land management regulations, abuses his power, and illegally approves the requisition, expropriation, or occupation of land, or illegally transfers the right to use State-owned land at a low price, where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where especially significant losses are caused to the State or collective interests, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-411"></a>第四百一十一条　海关工作人员徇私舞弊，放纵走私，情节严重的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 411** Where a customs functionary engages in malpractices for personal gain and indulges in smuggling, where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are especially severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-412"></a>第四百一十二条　国家商检部门、商检机构的工作人员徇私舞弊，伪造检验结果的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成严重后果的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
前款所列人员严重不负责任，对应当检验的物品不检验，或者延误检验出证、错误出证，致使国家利益遭受重大损失的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 412** Where a functionary of the State commodity inspection department or commodity inspection institution engages in malpractices for personal gain and forges inspection results, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where serious consequences are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years.  
Where a person listed in the preceding paragraph is grossly irresponsible, fails to inspect items that should be inspected, or delays the issuance of inspection certificates or issues erroneous certificates, thereby causing significant losses to the State's interests, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-413"></a>第四百一十三条　动植物检疫机关的检疫人员徇私舞弊，伪造检疫结果的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成严重后果的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
前款所列人员严重不负责任，对应当检疫的检疫物不检疫，或者延误检疫出证、错误出证，致使国家利益遭受重大损失的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 413** Where an inspection officer of an animal and plant quarantine authority engages in malpractices for personal gain and forges inspection results, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where serious consequences are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years.  
Where a person listed in the preceding paragraph is grossly irresponsible, fails to inspect items that should be inspected, or delays the issuance of inspection certificates or issues erroneous certificates, thereby causing significant losses to the State's interests, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-414"></a>第四百一十四条　对生产、销售伪劣商品犯罪行为负有追究责任的国家机关工作人员，徇私舞弊，不履行法律规定的追究职责，情节严重的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理生产、销售伪劣商品刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于办理生产、销售伪劣商品刑事案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第8条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 414** Where a state functionary responsible for investigating and punishing criminal activities related to the production and sale of counterfeit and substandard goods engages in malpractices for personal gain and fails to perform his investigation duties as prescribed by law, where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-415"></a>第四百一十五条　负责办理护照、签证以及其他出入境证件的国家机关工作人员，对明知是企图偷越国（边）境的人员，予以办理出入境证件的，或者边防、海关等国家机关工作人员，对明知是偷越国（边）境的人员，予以放行的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 415** Where a state functionary responsible for handling passports, visas, and other exit-entry documents, knowingly issues exit-entry documents to persons attempting to illegally cross the national (border) boundary, or a state functionary of border defense, customs, or other authorities, knowingly lets persons illegally crossing the national (border) boundary pass, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-416"></a>第四百一十六条　对被拐卖、绑架的妇女、儿童负有解救职责的国家机关工作人员，接到被拐卖、绑架的妇女、儿童及其家属的解救要求或者接到其他人的举报，而对被拐卖、绑架的妇女、儿童不进行解救，造成严重后果的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
负有解救职责的国家机关工作人员利用职务阻碍解救的，处二年以上七年以下有期徒刑；情节较轻的，处二年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 416** Where a state functionary responsible for rescuing trafficked or kidnapped women or children, upon receiving a rescue request from the trafficked or kidnapped women or children or their family members, or upon receiving a report from others, fails to rescue them, thereby causing serious consequences, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention.  
Where a state functionary responsible for rescue duties uses his position to obstruct the rescue, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than two years but not more than seven years; where the circumstances are relatively minor, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than two years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-417"></a>第四百一十七条　有查禁犯罪活动职责的国家机关工作人员，向犯罪分子通风报信、提供便利，帮助犯罪分子逃避处罚的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 417** Where a state functionary responsible for investigating and suppressing criminal activities tips off criminals, provides convenience to them, or helps them evade punishment, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-418"></a>第四百一十八条　国家机关工作人员在招收公务员、学生工作中徇私舞弊，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 418** Where a state functionary engages in malpractices for personal gain in recruiting civil servants or students, where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-419"></a>第四百一十九条　国家机关工作人员严重不负责任，造成珍贵文物损毁或者流失，后果严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 419** Where a state functionary is grossly irresponsible, resulting in the damage or loss of valuable cultural relics, where the consequences are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

### 第十章　军人违反职责罪

<a id="art-420"></a>第四百二十条　军人违反职责，危害国家军事利益，依照法律应当受刑罚处罚的行为，是军人违反职责罪。

**Article 420** An act committed by a serviceman in violation of his duties, endangering the military interests of the State, and for which criminal punishment shall be imposed according to law constitutes a crime of dereliction of duty by servicemen.

<a id="art-421"></a>第四百二十一条　战时违抗命令，对作战造成危害的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；致使战斗、战役遭受重大损失的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。

**Article 421** Where a person disobeys orders during wartime, thereby causing harm to combat operations, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; where significant losses are caused to battles or campaigns, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.

<a id="art-422"></a>第四百二十二条　故意隐瞒、谎报军情或者拒传、假传军令，对作战造成危害的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；致使战斗、战役遭受重大损失的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。

**Article 422** Where a person intentionally conceals or falsely reports military intelligence, or refuses to transmit or transmits false military orders, thereby causing harm to combat operations, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; where significant losses are caused to battles or campaigns, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.

<a id="art-423"></a>第四百二十三条　在战场上贪生怕死，自动放下武器投降敌人的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑。  
投降后为敌人效劳的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。

**Article 423** Where a person, being cowardly on the battlefield, voluntarily lays down his weapons and surrenders to the enemy, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment.  
Where a person serves the enemy after surrendering, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.

<a id="art-424"></a>第四百二十四条　战时临阵脱逃的，处三年以下有期徒刑；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；致使战斗、战役遭受重大损失的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。

**Article 424** Where a person deserts from the battlefield during wartime, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; where significant losses are caused to battles or campaigns, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.

<a id="art-425"></a>第四百二十五条　指挥人员和值班、值勤人员擅离职守或者玩忽职守，造成严重后果的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成特别严重后果的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。  
战时犯前款罪的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 425** Where a commander or a person on duty or on guard absents himself from his post without permission or is derelict in his duty, causing serious consequences, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where especially serious consequences are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.  
Where a person commits the crime in the preceding paragraph during wartime, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-426"></a>第四百二十六条　以暴力、威胁方法，阻碍指挥人员或者值班、值勤人员执行职务的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑。战时从重处罚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-50" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第50条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 426** Where a person obstructs a commander or a person on duty or on guard from performing his duties by using violence or threats, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment. A severe punishment shall be imposed during wartime.

<a id="art-427"></a>第四百二十七条　滥用职权，指使部属进行违反职责的活动，造成严重后果的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 427** Where a person abuses his power and directs subordinates to engage in activities that violate their duties, causing serious consequences, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are especially severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-428"></a>第四百二十八条　指挥人员违抗命令，临阵畏缩，作战消极，造成严重后果的，处五年以下有期徒刑；致使战斗、战役遭受重大损失或者有其他特别严重情节的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 428** Where a commander disobeys orders, flinches on the battlefield, or acts passively in combat, causing serious consequences, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years; where significant losses are caused to battles or campaigns, or there are other especially severe circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-429"></a>第四百二十九条　在战场上明知友邻部队处境危急请求救援，能救援而不救援，致使友邻部队遭受重大损失的，对指挥人员，处五年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 429** Where, on the battlefield, a commander knowingly sees that a neighboring friendly unit is in a critical situation and requests rescue, but is able to rescue and fails to do so, thereby causing significant losses to the friendly unit, the commander shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years.

<a id="art-430"></a>第四百三十条　在履行公务期间，擅离岗位，叛逃境外或者在境外叛逃，危害国家军事利益的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处五年以上有期徒刑。  
驾驶航空器、舰船叛逃的，或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。

**Article 430** Where a person, during the performance of official duties, absents himself from his post without permission, defects to a foreign country, or defects while abroad, thereby endangering the military interests of the State, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.  
Where a person defects by piloting an aircraft or a ship, or where there are other especially severe circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.

<a id="art-431"></a>第四百三十一条　以窃取、刺探、收买方法，非法获取军事秘密的，处五年以下有期徒刑；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑。  
为境外的机构、组织、人员窃取、刺探、收买、非法提供军事秘密的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-46" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第46条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 431** Where a person illegally obtains military secrets by means of theft, espionage, or bribery, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years.  
Where a person steals, spies, buys, or illegally provides military secrets for an institution, organization, or individual outside the country, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.

<a id="art-432"></a>第四百三十二条　违反保守国家秘密法规，故意或者过失泄露军事秘密，情节严重的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。  
战时犯前款罪的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑。

**Article 432** Where a person violates the regulations on preserving State secrets, intentionally or negligently divulges military secrets, where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are especially severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years.  
Where a person commits the crime in the preceding paragraph during wartime, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment.

<a id="art-433"></a>第四百三十三条　战时造谣惑众，动摇军心的，处三年以下有期徒刑；情节严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑或者无期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）.md#art-51" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（九）》第51条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 433** Where a person spreads rumors to confuse and shake the morale of the troops during wartime, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years or life imprisonment.

<a id="art-434"></a>第四百三十四条　战时自伤身体，逃避军事义务的，处三年以下有期徒刑；情节严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 434** Where a person injures himself during wartime to evade military duties, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-435"></a>第四百三十五条　违反兵役法规，逃离部队，情节严重的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。  
战时犯前款罪的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/综合司法解释/最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于对军人非战时逃离部队的行为能否定罪处罚问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院、最高人民检察院关于对军人非战时逃离部队的行为能否定罪处罚问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 435** Where a person violates military service regulations and deserts the armed forces, where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.  
Where a person commits the crime in the preceding paragraph during wartime, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-436"></a>第四百三十六条　违反武器装备使用规定，情节严重，因而发生责任事故，致人重伤、死亡或者造成其他严重后果的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；后果特别严重的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 436** Where a person violates the regulations on the use of weapons and equipment, where the circumstances are severe, thereby causing a responsibility accident resulting in serious injury, death, or other serious consequences, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where the consequences are especially severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-437"></a>第四百三十七条　违反武器装备管理规定，擅自改变武器装备的编配用途，造成严重后果的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成特别严重后果的，处三年以上七年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 437** Where a person violates the regulations on the management of weapons and equipment, and arbitrarily changes the purpose of allocation of weapons and equipment, causing serious consequences, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where especially serious consequences are caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than seven years.

<a id="art-438"></a>第四百三十八条　盗窃、抢夺武器装备或者军用物资的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。  
盗窃、抢夺枪支、弹药、爆炸物的，依照[本法第一百二十七条](#art-127)的规定处罚。

**Article 438** Where a person steals or forcibly seizes weapons, equipment, or military supplies, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially severe, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.  
Where a person steals or forcibly seizes firearms, ammunition, or explosives, he shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of Article 127 of this Law.

<a id="art-439"></a>第四百三十九条　非法出卖、转让军队武器装备的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑；出卖、转让大量武器装备或者有其他特别严重情节的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。

**Article 439** Where a person illegally sells or transfers military weapons and equipment, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years; where a large amount of weapons and equipment are sold or transferred, or where there are other especially severe circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.

<a id="art-440"></a>第四百四十条　违抗命令，遗弃武器装备的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；遗弃重要或者大量武器装备的，或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 440** Where a person disobeys orders and abandons weapons and equipment, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where important or a large amount of weapons and equipment are abandoned, or where there are other severe circumstances, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-441"></a>第四百四十一条　遗失武器装备，不及时报告或者有其他严重情节的，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役。

**Article 441** : A person who loses weapons or equipment and fails to report promptly, or who commits other serious circumstances, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention.

<a id="art-442"></a>第四百四十二条　违反规定，擅自出卖、转让军队房地产，情节严重的，对直接责任人员，处三年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；情节特别严重的，处三年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 442** : A person who violates regulations by selling or transferring military real estate without authorization, where the circumstances are serious, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years or criminal detention; where the circumstances are especially serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-443"></a>第四百四十三条　滥用职权，虐待部属，情节恶劣，致人重伤或者造成其他严重后果的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；致人死亡的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 443** : A person who abuses his authority to maltreat subordinates, where the circumstances are despicable and cause serious injury to a person or other serious consequences, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where death is caused, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-444"></a>第四百四十四条　在战场上故意遗弃伤病军人，情节恶劣的，对直接责任人员，处五年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 444** : A person who, on the battlefield, intentionally abandons wounded or sick military personnel, where the circumstances are despicable, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years.

<a id="art-445"></a>第四百四十五条　战时在救护治疗职位上，有条件救治而拒不救治危重伤病军人的，处五年以下有期徒刑或者拘役；造成伤病军人重残、死亡或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 445** : A person who, during wartime, in a medical rescue or treatment position, has the conditions to treat but refuses to treat a critically wounded or sick military person, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years or criminal detention; where the wounded or sick military person suffers severe disability or death, or where other serious circumstances exist, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years.

<a id="art-446"></a>第四百四十六条　战时在军事行动地区，残害无辜居民或者掠夺无辜居民财物的，处五年以下有期徒刑；情节严重的，处五年以上十年以下有期徒刑；情节特别严重的，处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑或者死刑。

**Article 446** : A person who, during wartime, in an area of military operation, cruelly injures innocent residents or plunders their property, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years; where the circumstances are serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years but not more than ten years; where the circumstances are especially serious, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or the death penalty.

<a id="art-447"></a>第四百四十七条　私放俘虏的，处五年以下有期徒刑；私放重要俘虏、私放俘虏多人或者有其他严重情节的，处五年以上有期徒刑。

**Article 447** : A person who releases a prisoner of war without authorization shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than five years; where he releases an important prisoner of war, releases multiple prisoners of war, or where other serious circumstances exist, he shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-448"></a>第四百四十八条　虐待俘虏，情节恶劣的，处三年以下有期徒刑。

**Article 448** : A person who maltreats a prisoner of war, where the circumstances are despicable, shall be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years.

<a id="art-449"></a>第四百四十九条　在战时，对被判处三年以下有期徒刑没有现实危险宣告缓刑的犯罪军人，允许其戴罪立功，确有立功表现时，可以撤销原判刑罚，不以犯罪论处。

**Article 449** : During wartime, a military person who is sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years and is granted probation because there is no immediate danger, shall be permitted to serve with credit and make amends by meritorious service. Where meritorious service is actually performed, the original sentence may be revoked, and the person shall not be deemed to have committed a crime.

<a id="art-450"></a>第四百五十条　本章适用于中国人民解放军的现役军官、文职干部、士兵及具有军籍的学员和中国人民武装警察部队的现役警官、文职干部、士兵及具有军籍的学员以及文职人员、执行军事任务的预备役人员和其他人员。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）.md#art-47" title="被《中华人民共和国刑法修正案（十一）》第47条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 450** : This Chapter shall apply to active-duty officers, civilian cadres, soldiers, and military-registered students of the People's Liberation Army, and to active-duty police officers, civilian cadres, soldiers, and military-registered students of the Chinese People's Armed Police Force, as well as civilian personnel, reserve personnel performing military missions, and other persons.

<a id="art-451"></a>第四百五十一条　本章所称战时，是指国家宣布进入战争状态、部队受领作战任务或者遭敌突然袭击时。  
部队执行戒严任务或者处置突发性暴力事件时，以战时论。  
附　　则

**Article 451** : For the purposes of this Chapter, "wartime" means the period when the state declares a state of war, the military unit receives combat missions, or is subjected to a sudden enemy attack.  
When a military unit carries out martial law tasks or handles sudden violent incidents, it shall be deemed as wartime.  
Supplementary Provisions

<a id="art-452"></a>第四百五十二条　本法自1997年10月1日起施行。  
列于本法附件一的全国人民代表大会常务委员会制定的条例、补充规定和决定，已纳入本法或者已不适用，自本法施行之日起，予以废止。  
列于本法附件二的全国人民代表大会常务委员会制定的补充规定和决定予以保留。其中，有关行政处罚和行政措施的规定继续有效；有关刑事责任的规定已纳入本法，自本法施行之日起，适用本法规定。  
附件一  
全国人民代表大会常务委员会制定的下列条例、补充规定和决定，已纳入本法或者已不适用，自本法施行之日起，予以废止：  
1.中华人民共和国惩治军人违反职责罪暂行条例  
2.关于严惩严重破坏经济的罪犯的决定  
3.关于严惩严重危害社会治安的犯罪分子的决定  
4.关于惩治走私罪的补充规定  
5.关于惩治贪污罪贿赂罪的补充规定  
6.关于惩治泄露国家秘密犯罪的补充规定  
7.关于惩治捕杀国家重点保护的珍贵、濒危野生动物犯罪的补充规定  
8.关于惩治侮辱中华人民共和国国旗国徽罪的决定  
9.关于惩治盗掘古文化遗址古墓葬犯罪的补充规定  
10.关于惩治劫持航空器犯罪分子的决定  
11.关于惩治假冒注册商标犯罪的补充规定  
12.关于惩治生产、销售伪劣商品犯罪的决定  
13.关于惩治侵犯著作权的犯罪的决定  
14.关于惩治违反公司法的犯罪的决定  
15.关于处理逃跑或者重新犯罪的劳改犯和劳教人员的决定  
附件二  
全国人民代表大会常务委员会制定的下列补充规定和决定予以保留，其中，有关行政处罚和行政措施的规定继续有效；有关刑事责任的规定已纳入本法，自本法施行之日起，适用本法规定：  
1.关于禁毒的决定  
2.关于惩治走私、制作、贩卖、传播淫秽物品的犯罪分子的决定  
3.关于严禁卖淫嫖娼的决定  
4.关于严惩拐卖、绑架妇女、儿童的犯罪分子的决定  
5.关于惩治偷税、抗税犯罪的补充规定  
6.关于严惩组织、运送他人偷越国（边）境犯罪的补充规定  
7.关于惩治破坏金融秩序犯罪的决定  
8.关于惩治虚开、伪造和非法出售增值税专用发票犯罪的决定

**Article 452** : This Law shall come into force on October 1, 1997.  
The regulations, supplementary provisions, and decisions formulated by the Standing Committee of the National People's Congress listed in Appendix I of this Law, which have been incorporated into this Law or are no longer applicable, shall be abolished as of the date of entry into force of this Law.  
The supplementary provisions and decisions formulated by the Standing Committee of the National People's Congress listed in Appendix II of this Law shall be retained. Among them, the provisions on administrative penalties and administrative measures shall continue to be effective; the provisions on criminal liability have been incorporated into this Law, and from the date of entry into force of this Law, the provisions of this Law shall apply.  
Appendix I  
The following regulations, supplementary provisions, and decisions formulated by the Standing Committee of the National People's Congress, which have been incorporated into this Law or are no longer applicable, shall be abolished as of the date of entry into force of this Law:  
1. Interim Regulations of the People's Republic of China on Punishment of Soldiers Who Commit Crimes Contrary to Their Duties  
2. Decision on Severely Punishing Criminals Who Seriously Sabotage the Economy  
3. Decision on Severely Punishing Criminals Who Seriously Endanger Public Security  
4. Supplementary Provisions on Punishment of Smuggling Crimes  
5. Supplementary Provisions on Punishment of Embezzlement and Bribery Crimes  
6. Supplementary Provisions on Punishment of Crimes of Leaking State Secrets  
7. Supplementary Provisions on Punishment of Crimes of Killing or Capturing Precious or Endangered Wild Animals under Special State Protection  
8. Decision on Punishment of Crimes of Insulting the National Flag and National Emblem of the People's Republic of China  
9. Supplementary Provisions on Punishment of Crimes of Illegally Excavating Ancient Cultural Sites and Ancient Tombs  
10. Decision on Punishment of Criminals Who Hijack Aircraft  
11. Supplementary Provisions on Punishment of Crimes of Counterfeiting Registered Trademarks  
12. Decision on Punishment of Crimes of Producing or Selling Counterfeit or Substandard Commodities  
13. Decision on Punishment of Crimes of Copyright Infringement  
14. Decision on Punishment of Crimes Violating the Company Law  
15. Decision on Handling Prisoners and Persons Undergoing Re-education through Labor Who Escape or Commit New Crimes  
Appendix II  
The following supplementary provisions and decisions formulated by the Standing Committee of the National People's Congress are retained, among which the provisions on administrative penalties and administrative measures shall continue to be effective; the provisions on criminal liability have been incorporated into this Law, and from the date of entry into force of this Law, the provisions of this Law shall apply:  
1. Decision on Prohibiting Drugs  
2. Decision on Punishment of Criminals Who Smuggle, Produce, Sell, or Disseminate Obscene Articles  
3. Decision on Prohibiting Prostitution and Soliciting Prostitutes  
4. Decision on Severely Punishing Criminals Who Abduct or Kidnap Women or Children  
5. Supplementary Provisions on Punishment of Crimes of Tax Evasion or Refusal to Pay Tax  
6. Supplementary Provisions on Severely Punishing Crimes of Organizing or Transporting Persons Illegally Across National Borders (Frontiers)  
7. Decision on Punishment of Crimes Disrupting Financial Order  
8. Decision on Punishment of Crimes of Falsely Issuing, Forging, or Illegally Selling Value-Added Tax Invoices
